    <script>
      // Filter benign Vite dev server WebSocket disconnect and Firestore backend reconnect notices in cloud iFrame/proxy
      (function() {
        var origError = console.error;
        var origWarn = console.warn;
        function isBenign(args) {
          var text = '';
          for (var i = 0; i < args.length; i++) {
            var item = args[i];
            if (!item) continue;
            text += ' ' + ((item && (item.message || item.stack)) || (typeof item === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(item) : String(item)));
          }
          return (
            text.indexOf('WebSocket') !== -1 ||
            text.indexOf('[vite]') !== -1 ||
            text.indexOf('closed without opened') !== -1 ||
            text.indexOf('Could not reach Cloud Firestore backend') !== -1 ||
            text.indexOf('offline mode') !== -1 ||
            text.indexOf('@firebase/firestore') !== -1
          );
        }
        console.error = function() {
          if (isBenign(arguments)) return;
          origError.apply(console, arguments);
        };
        console.warn = function() {
          if (isBenign(arguments)) return;
          origWarn.apply(console, arguments);
        };

        // Window error handler
        window.addEventListener('error', function(e) {
          var msg = (e && (e.message || (e.error && (e.error.message || e.error.stack)))) || '';
          if (typeof msg !== 'string') msg = String(msg);
          if (
            msg.indexOf('WebSocket') !== -1 ||
            msg.indexOf('vite') !== -1 ||
            msg.indexOf('Firestore') !== -1 ||
            msg.indexOf('closed without opened') !== -1
          ) {
            if (e.preventDefault) e.preventDefault();
            if (e.stopImmediatePropagation) e.stopImmediatePropagation();
            return true;
          }
        }, true);

        // Window unhandled rejection handler
        window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', function(e) {
          var msg = (e && e.reason && (e.reason.message || e.reason.stack || String(e.reason))) || '';
          if (typeof msg !== 'string') msg = String(msg);
          if (
            msg.indexOf('WebSocket') !== -1 ||
            msg.indexOf('vite') !== -1 ||
            msg.indexOf('Firestore') !== -1 ||
            msg.indexOf('unavailable') !== -1 ||
            msg.indexOf('closed without opened') !== -1
          ) {
            if (e.preventDefault) e.preventDefault();
            if (e.stopImmediatePropagation) e.stopImmediatePropagation();
            return true;
          }
        }, true);

        // Global onerror fallback
        var origOnError = window.onerror;
        window.onerror = function(msg, url, lineNo, columnNo, error) {
          var errStr = String(msg || '') + ' ' + String((error && (error.message || error.stack)) || '');
          if (
            errStr.indexOf('WebSocket') !== -1 ||
            errStr.indexOf('vite') !== -1 ||
            errStr.indexOf('Firestore') !== -1 ||
            errStr.indexOf('closed without opened') !== -1
          ) {
            return true;
          }
          if (origOnError) return origOnError.apply(this, arguments);
        };

        // Wrap WebSocket to intercept closed-without-opened in cloud preview
        if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.WebSocket) {
          var OrigWebSocket = window.WebSocket;
          window.WebSocket = function(url, protocols) {
            var urlStr = String(url || '');
            var protoStr = protocols ? String(protocols) : '';
            if (
              urlStr.indexOf('vite') !== -1 ||
              urlStr.indexOf('ais-dev') !== -1 ||
              protoStr.indexOf('vite') !== -1
            ) {
              // Inert mock WebSocket for disabled Vite HMR in cloud preview environment
              var listeners = {};
              var mockWs = {
                url: urlStr,
                readyState: 1, // OPEN
                bufferedAmount: 0,
                extensions: '',
                protocol: protoStr,
                binaryType: 'blob',
                send: function() {},
                close: function() {},
                addEventListener: function(type, fn) {
                  if (!listeners[type]) listeners[type] = [];
                  listeners[type].push(fn);
                },
                removeEventListener: function(type, fn) {
                  if (!listeners[type]) return;
                  listeners[type] = listeners[type].filter(function(f) { return f !== fn; });
                },
                dispatchEvent: function() { return true; }
              };
              return mockWs;
            }
            var ws = protocols !== undefined ? new OrigWebSocket(url, protocols) : new OrigWebSocket(url);
            ws.addEventListener('error', function(event) {
              if (event && event.stopImmediatePropagation) event.stopImmediatePropagation();
              if (event && event.preventDefault) event.preventDefault();
            }, true);
            return ws;
          };
          window.WebSocket.prototype = OrigWebSocket.prototype;
          window.WebSocket.CONNECTING = 0;
          window.WebSocket.OPEN = 1;
          window.WebSocket.CLOSING = 2;
          window.WebSocket.CLOSED = 3;
        }
      })();
      if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'serviceWorker' in navigator) {
        navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistrations().then(function(regs) {
          for (var i = 0; i < regs.length; i++) {
            regs[i].update();
          }
        }).catch(function() {});
      }
    </script>
  </head>

FILE: firestore.rules
================================================================================
rules_version = '2';
service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {
    function isValidId(id) {
      return id is string && id.size() > 0 && id.size() <= 64;
    }

    match /qr_codes/{qrId} {
      allow read: if true;
      allow write: if true;
    }

    match /settings/{docId} {
      allow read, write: if true;
    }

    match /platform_settings/{docId} {
      allow read, write: if true;
    }

    match /test/{docId} {
      allow read: if true;
    }
  }
}

import express from 'express';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import crypto from 'crypto';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';
import { nanoid } from 'nanoid';
import rateLimit from 'express-rate-limit';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);

const app = express();
// Enable trust proxy for environments behind reverse proxies (Cloud Run, Vercel, Nginx)
app.set('trust proxy', 1);

const isProd = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production';
const portArgIdx = process.argv.indexOf('--port');
const cliPort = portArgIdx !== -1 ? Number(process.argv[portArgIdx + 1]) : NaN;
const PORT = !isNaN(cliPort) && cliPort > 0
  ? cliPort
  : (!isProd && process.env.PORT === '8080')
    ? 3000
    : (Number(process.env.PORT) || 3000);
const DATA_DIR = path.join(__dirname, 'data');
const DB_FILE = path.join(DATA_DIR, 'qr_codes.json');
const SETTINGS_FILE = path.join(DATA_DIR, 'settings.json');
const AUTH_FILE = path.join(DATA_DIR, 'auth.json');
const SESSIONS_FILE = path.join(DATA_DIR, 'sessions.json');

app.use(express.json({ limit: '1mb' }));

// Ensure data directory exists
if (!fs.existsSync(DATA_DIR)) {
  fs.mkdirSync(DATA_DIR, { recursive: true });
}

// Default Password Hash (SHA-256 for "admin1234")
const DEFAULT_BASE_HASH = 'ac9689e2272427085e35b9d3e3e8bed88cb3434828b43b86fc0596cad4c6e270';

function hashString(plain: string): string {
  return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(plain).digest('hex');
}

// Rate Limiting Guards
const generalApiLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 5 * 60 * 1000, // 5 minutes
  max: 300,
  standardHeaders: true,
  legacyHeaders: false,
  validate: {
    xForwardedForHeader: false,
    forwardedHeader: false,
  },
  message: { success: false, error: 'Too many requests, please try again later.' },
});

const authRateLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000, // 15 minutes
  max: 15,
  standardHeaders: true,
  legacyHeaders: false,
  validate: {
    xForwardedForHeader: false,
    forwardedHeader: false,
  },
  message: { success: false, error: 'تم تجاوز الحد الأقصى لمحاولات تسجيل الدخول، يرجى المحاولة بعد 15 دقيقة.' },
});

const bulkRateLimiter = rateLimit({
  windowMs: 15 * 60 * 1000, // 15 minutes
  max: 40,
  standardHeaders: true,
  legacyHeaders: false,
  validate: {
    xForwardedForHeader: false,
    forwardedHeader: false,
  },
  message: { success: false, error: 'تم تجاوز الحد المسموح لتوليد الأكواد بالجملة، يرجى المحاولة لاحقاً.' },
});

app.use('/api/', generalApiLimiter);

// --- Session Store & Auth Management ---
interface AdminSession {
  token: string;
  created_at: string;
  expires_at: number; // timestamp in ms
}

function loadSessions(): Record<string, AdminSession> {
  try {
    if (fs.existsSync(SESSIONS_FILE)) {
      const data = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(SESSIONS_FILE, 'utf-8'));
      const now = Date.now();
      const valid: Record<string, AdminSession> = {};
      for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(data)) {
        if (v && typeof v === 'object' && (v as AdminSession).expires_at > now) {
          valid[k] = v as AdminSession;
        }
      }
      return valid;
    }
  } catch {}
  return {};
}

function saveSessions(sessions: Record<string, AdminSession>): void {
  try {
    fs.writeFileSync(SESSIONS_FILE, JSON.stringify(sessions, null, 2), 'utf-8');
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Failed to save sessions:', err);
  }
}

let firebaseConfig: any = null;
try {
  const cfgPath = path.join(__dirname, 'firebase-applet-config.json');
  if (fs.existsSync(cfgPath)) {
    firebaseConfig = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(cfgPath, 'utf-8'));
  }
} catch {}

async function syncPasswordToFirestore(newHash: string): Promise<void> {
  if (!firebaseConfig?.projectId || !firebaseConfig?.apiKey) return;
  try {
    const pId = firebaseConfig.projectId;
    const dbId = firebaseConfig.firestoreDatabaseId || '(default)';
    const key = firebaseConfig.apiKey;
    const docUrl = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${pId}/databases/${dbId}/documents/settings/auth?key=${key}`;
    await fetch(docUrl, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        fields: {
          hash: { stringValue: newHash },
          updated_at: { stringValue: new Date().toISOString() },
          migrated: { booleanValue: true },
        },
      }),
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Failed to sync password to Firestore from server:', err);
  }
}

async function syncPasswordFromFirestore(): Promise<void> {
  if (!firebaseConfig?.projectId || !firebaseConfig?.apiKey) return;
  try {
    const pId = firebaseConfig.projectId;
    const dbId = firebaseConfig.firestoreDatabaseId || '(default)';
    const key = firebaseConfig.apiKey;
    const docUrl = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${pId}/databases/${dbId}/documents/settings/auth?key=${key}`;
    const res = await fetch(docUrl);
    if (res.ok) {
      const data = await res.json();
      const hashVal = data.fields?.hash?.stringValue;
      if (hashVal && hashVal.length === 64) {
        fs.writeFileSync(AUTH_FILE, JSON.stringify({ hash: hashVal, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }, null, 2), 'utf-8');
      }
    }
  } catch {}
}
// Sync with Firestore on startup
syncPasswordFromFirestore().catch(() => {});

function getStoredPasswordHash(): string {
  try {
    if (fs.existsSync(AUTH_FILE)) {
      const data = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(AUTH_FILE, 'utf-8'));
      if (data && typeof data.hash === 'string' && data.hash.length === 64) {
        return data.hash;
      }
    }
  } catch {}
  return DEFAULT_BASE_HASH;
}

function saveStoredPasswordHash(newHash: string): void {
  fs.writeFileSync(AUTH_FILE, JSON.stringify({ hash: newHash, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }, null, 2), 'utf-8');
  syncPasswordToFirestore(newHash).catch(() => {});
}

// Cookie helper
function getCookieFromReq(req: express.Request, name: string): string | null {
  const cookieHeader = req.headers.cookie;
  if (!cookieHeader) return null;
  const match = cookieHeader.match(new RegExp('(^|;\\s*)' + name + '=([^;]*)'));
  return match ? decodeURIComponent(match[2]) : null;
}

// Server-Side Authentication Middleware for Admin APIs
function requireAdminAuth(req: express.Request, res: express.Response, next: express.NextFunction) {
  let token: string | null = null;

  // 1. Check Authorization: Bearer <token>
  const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
  if (authHeader && authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
    token = authHeader.slice(7).trim();
  }

  // 2. Check X-Admin-Token header
  if (!token && typeof req.headers['x-admin-token'] === 'string') {
    token = req.headers['x-admin-token'].trim();
  }

  // 3. Check admin_session cookie
  if (!token) {
    token = getCookieFromReq(req, 'admin_session');
  }

  if (!token || token.length < 32) {
    return res.status(401).json({
      success: false,
      error: 'غير مصرح: يجب تسجيل الدخول كمسؤول أولاً (Unauthorized Session)',
    });
  }

  const sessions = loadSessions();
  const session = sessions[token];
  if (!session || session.expires_at <= Date.now()) {
    return res.status(401).json({
      success: false,
      error: 'انتهت صلاحية الجلسة، يرجى تسجيل الدخول مجدداً (Session Expired)',
    });
  }

  // Session valid
  next();
}

  developer_whatsapp: string;
  developer_contact_url: string;
  developer_title?: string;
  support_message?: string;
  public_domain_url?: string;

  developer_contact_url: 'https://wa.me/201550523365',

  support_message: 'مرحباً، أود التواصل بخصوص البطاقة الذكية',

};

// --- QR Database ---
interface QrRecord {
  id: string;
  is_active: boolean;
  target_url: string | null;
  app_type?: string;
  label?: string;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at?: string;
  total_scans: number;
  expires_at?: string | null;
  subscription_months?: number;
  wallets?: any[];
}

function loadDatabase(): Record<string, QrRecord> {
  try {
    if (fs.existsSync(DB_FILE)) {
      const content = fs.readFileSync(DB_FILE, 'utf-8');
      return JSON.parse(content);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Failed to read db file:', err);
  }
  return {};
}

function saveDatabase(db: Record<string, QrRecord>): void {
  try {
    fs.writeFileSync(DB_FILE, JSON.stringify(db, null, 2), 'utf-8');
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Failed to write db file:', err);
  }
}

if (!fs.existsSync(DB_FILE)) {
  saveDatabase({});
}

// Safe URL validator
function isSafeUrlScheme(url: string): boolean {
  if (!url) return false;
  const sanitized = url.trim().replace(/[\x00-\x1f\x7f]/g, '').toLowerCase();
  const dangerous = /^(?:javascript|data|vbscript|file|blob|about):/i;
  return !dangerous.test(sanitized);
}

function formatTikTokTargetUrl(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  let clean = input
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
    .trim();

  if (!clean) return '';

  if (/^(?:https?:\/\/)?(?:vm|vt)\.tiktok\.com\//i.test(clean)) {
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }

  if (clean.includes('tiktok.com')) {
    const parts = clean.split('?');
    const pathPart = parts[0];
    const queryPart = parts[1] ? `?${parts[1]}` : '';

    const match = pathPart.match(/tiktok\.com\/(?:@)?([a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      const segment = match[1].replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase().trim();
      const reservedRoutes = ['discover', 'explore', 'upload', 'live', 'tag', 'music', 'about', 'legal', 'business', 'share', 'foryou'];
      if (!reservedRoutes.includes(segment)) {
        return `https://www.tiktok.com/@${segment}${queryPart}`;
      }
    }
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }

  let username = clean.replace(/^[/@]+/, '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0].trim();
  username = username.replace(/[^\w.-]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();

  if (username) {
    return `https://www.tiktok.com/@${username}`;
  }

  return clean;
}

function formatInstagramTargetUrl(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  let clean = input
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
    .trim();

  if (clean.includes('instagram.com/')) {
    const match = clean.match(/instagram\.com\/([a-zA-Z0-9._]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      const user = match[1].replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase().trim();
      return `https://www.instagram.com/${user}/`;
    }
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }
  let username = clean.replace(/^[/@]+/, '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0].trim();
  username = username.replace(/[^\w.]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (username) {
    return `https://www.instagram.com/${username}/`;
  }
  return clean;
}

function formatTelegramTargetUrl(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  let clean = input
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
    .trim();

  if (clean.includes('t.me/') || clean.includes('telegram.me/')) {
    const match = clean.match(/(?:t\.me|telegram\.me)\/([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return `https://t.me/${match[1].replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase().trim()}`;
    }
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }
  let username = clean.replace(/^[/@]+/, '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0].trim();
  username = username.replace(/[^\w]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (username) {
    return `https://t.me/${username}`;
  }
  return clean;
}

function formatSnapchatTargetUrl(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  let clean = input
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
    .trim();

  if (clean.includes('snapchat.com/add/')) {
    const match = clean.match(/snapchat\.com\/add\/([a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return `https://www.snapchat.com/add/${match[1].replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase().trim()}`;
    }
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }
  let username = clean.replace(/^[/@]+/, '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0].trim();
  username = username.replace(/[^\w.-]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (username) {
    return `https://www.snapchat.com/add/${username}`;
  }
  return clean;
}

// --- AUTH APIS ---

// 1. Admin Login (Server authoritative verification & token generation)
app.post('/api/admin/login', authRateLimiter, (req, res) => {
  const { password } = req.body;
  if (!password || typeof password !== 'string') {
    return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'الرجاء إدخال كلمة المرور' });
  }

  const attemptHash = hashString(password.trim());
  const storedHash = getStoredPasswordHash();

  if (attemptHash !== storedHash) {
    return res.status(401).json({ success: false, error: 'كلمة المرور غير صحيحة' });
  }

  // Generate cryptographically secure session token
  const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
  const sevenDays = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  const sessions = loadSessions();
  sessions[token] = {
    token,
    created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    expires_at: Date.now() + sevenDays,
  };
  saveSessions(sessions);

  // Set HTTP-Only secure cookie
  res.cookie('admin_session', token, {
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',
    sameSite: 'lax',
    maxAge: sevenDays,
    path: '/',
  });

  return res.json({
    success: true,
    token,
    message: 'تم تسجيل الدخول بنجاح',
  });
});

// 2. Validate current session
app.get('/api/admin/session', (req, res) => {
  let token: string | null = null;
  const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
  if (authHeader && authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
    token = authHeader.slice(7).trim();
  }
  if (!token && typeof req.headers['x-admin-token'] === 'string') {
    token = req.headers['x-admin-token'].trim();
  }
  if (!token) {
    token = getCookieFromReq(req, 'admin_session');
  }

  if (!token) {
    return res.json({ success: true, authenticated: false });
  }

  const sessions = loadSessions();
  const session = sessions[token];
  const isValid = Boolean(session && session.expires_at > Date.now());
  return res.json({ success: true, authenticated: isValid });
});

// 3. Admin Logout
app.post('/api/admin/logout', (req, res) => {
  let token = getCookieFromReq(req, 'admin_session');
  const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;
  if (authHeader && authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ')) {
    token = authHeader.slice(7).trim();
  }
  if (token) {
    const sessions = loadSessions();
    delete sessions[token];
    saveSessions(sessions);
  }
  res.clearCookie('admin_session', { path: '/' });
  res.json({ success: true, message: 'تم تسجيل الخروج بنجاح' });
});

// 4. Update Password (Protected & requires current password verification)
app.post('/api/admin/password', requireAdminAuth, authRateLimiter, (req, res) => {
  const { currentPassword, newPassword } = req.body;
  if (!currentPassword || typeof currentPassword !== 'string') {
    return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'الرجاء إدخال كلمة المرور الحالية' });
  }
  if (!newPassword || typeof newPassword !== 'string' || newPassword.trim().length < 4) {
    return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'كلمة المرور الجديدة يجب ألا تقل عن 4 أحرف أو أرقام' });
  }

  const currentHash = getStoredPasswordHash();
  const attemptCurrentHash = hashString(currentPassword.trim());

  const isCurrentValid = attemptCurrentHash === currentHash || (currentHash === DEFAULT_BASE_HASH && attemptCurrentHash === DEFAULT_BASE_HASH);
  if (!isCurrentValid) {
    return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'كلمة المرور الحالية غير صحيحة' });
  }

  const newHash = hashString(newPassword.trim());
  saveStoredPasswordHash(newHash);

  // Invalidate old sessions for maximum security
  saveSessions({});

  // Issue fresh session for the current admin
  const token = crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
  const sevenDays = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  const sessions = loadSessions();
  sessions[token] = {
    token,
    created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    expires_at: Date.now() + sevenDays,
  };
  saveSessions(sessions);

  res.cookie('admin_session', token, {
    httpOnly: true,
    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',
    sameSite: 'lax',
    maxAge: sevenDays,
    path: '/',
  });

  return res.json({
    success: true,
    token,
    message: 'تم التحقق من كلمة المرور وتحديثها وتشفيرها بنجاح!',
  });
});

// --- QR & SETTINGS APIS ---

// Get all QR codes
app.get('/api/qr', (req, res) => {
  const db = loadDatabase();
  const list = Object.values(db).sort(
    (a, b) => new Date(b.created_at).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at).getTime()
  );
  res.json({ success: true, data: list });
});

function parseFirestoreFieldVal(val: any): any {
  if (!val || typeof val !== 'object') return undefined;
  if ('stringValue' in val) return val.stringValue;
  if ('booleanValue' in val) return val.booleanValue;
  if ('integerValue' in val) return parseInt(val.integerValue, 10);
  if ('doubleValue' in val) return Number(val.doubleValue);
  if ('nullValue' in val) return null;
  if ('timestampValue' in val) return val.timestampValue;
  if ('arrayValue' in val) {
    const vals = val.arrayValue?.values || [];
    return vals.map(parseFirestoreFieldVal).filter((v: any) => v !== undefined);
  }
  if ('mapValue' in val) {
    const fields = val.mapValue?.fields || {};
    const obj: Record<string, any> = {};
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(fields)) {
      const parsed = parseFirestoreFieldVal(v);
      if (parsed !== undefined) obj[k] = parsed;
    }
    return obj;
  }
  return undefined;
}

async function fetchQrFromFirestoreRestServer(id: string): Promise<QrRecord | null> {
  const pId = firebaseConfig?.projectId || FIRESTORE_PROJECT_ID;
  const dbId = firebaseConfig?.firestoreDatabaseId || FIRESTORE_DATABASE_ID;
  const key = firebaseConfig?.apiKey || 'AIzaSyAjyCP484AEQ52-9CDgp8TDkAw5woA1TFc';
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 1400);
  try {
    const docUrl = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${pId}/databases/${dbId}/documents/qr_codes/${encodeURIComponent(id)}?key=${key}`;
    const firestoreRes = await fetch(docUrl, { signal: controller.signal, cache: 'no-store' });
    if (!firestoreRes.ok) return null;
    const firestoreData = await firestoreRes.json();
    const f = firestoreData.fields || {};
    const parsedWallets = parseFirestoreFieldVal(f.wallets);
    return {
      id,
      is_active: f.is_active?.booleanValue ?? false,
      target_url: f.target_url?.stringValue ?? null,
      app_type: f.app_type?.stringValue ?? 'website',
      label: f.label?.stringValue,
      created_at: f.created_at?.stringValue ?? new Date().toISOString(),
      updated_at: f.updated_at?.stringValue,
      total_scans: parseInt(f.total_scans?.integerValue || '0', 10),
      expires_at: f.expires_at?.stringValue ?? null,
      subscription_months: parseInt(f.subscription_months?.integerValue || '1', 10),
      wallets: Array.isArray(parsedWallets) ? parsedWallets : undefined,
    };
  } catch {
    return null;
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
  }
}

// Get single QR code by ID
app.get('/api/qr/:id', async (req, res) => {
  const { id } = req.params;
  const db = loadDatabase();
  let record = db[id];

  const fsRecord = await fetchQrFromFirestoreRestServer(id);
  if (fsRecord) {
    const localTime = record ? new Date(record.updated_at || record.created_at || 0).getTime() : 0;
    const fsTime = new Date(fsRecord.updated_at || fsRecord.created_at || 0).getTime();
    if (!record || fsTime >= localTime) {
      record = fsRecord;
      db[id] = fsRecord;
      saveDatabase(db);
    }
  }

  if (!record) {
    return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'الرمز غير موجود' });
  }
  res.set('Cache-Control', 'no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, max-age=0');
  res.json({ success: true, data: record });
});

// Bulk Generate QR codes (PROTECTED with requireAdminAuth & bulkRateLimiter)
app.post('/api/qr/bulk', requireAdminAuth, bulkRateLimiter, (req, res) => {
  const count = parseInt(req.body.count, 10);
  const subscriptionMonths = typeof req.body.subscription_months === 'number' ? req.body.subscription_months : 1;
  const providedExpiresAt = req.body.expires_at !== undefined ? req.body.expires_at : (
    subscriptionMonths > 0 ? new Date(Date.now() + subscriptionMonths * 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString() : null
  );

  if (isNaN(count) || count < 1) {
    return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'الرجاء إدخال رقم صحيح أكبر من صفر' });
  }

  const safeCount = Math.min(count, 500);
  const db = loadDatabase();
  const newRecords: QrRecord[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < safeCount; i++) {
    let id = nanoid(10).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, 'x');
    while (db[id]) {
      id = nanoid(10).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, 'x');
    }

    const record: QrRecord = {
      id,
      is_active: false,
      target_url: null,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      total_scans: 0,
      subscription_months: subscriptionMonths,
      expires_at: providedExpiresAt,
    };

    db[id] = record;
    newRecords.push(record);
  }

  saveDatabase(db);
  res.json({ success: true, data: newRecords, count: newRecords.length });
});

// Customer First-Scan Activation (Public)
app.post('/api/qr/:id/activate', (req, res) => {
  const { id } = req.params;
  const { target_url, app_type, expires_at, subscription_months, wallets } = req.body;

  if (!target_url) {
    return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'الرجاء كتابة الرابط أو الرقم المطلوب' });
  }

  // Validate URL scheme safety
  if (!isSafeUrlScheme(target_url)) {
    return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'بروتوكول الرابط غير آمن أو غير مسموح به' });
  }

  const db = loadDatabase();
  let current = db[id];
  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  if (!current) {
    current = {
      id,
      is_active: true,
      target_url,
      app_type: app_type || 'website',
      created_at: now,
      updated_at: now,
      total_scans: 0,
      expires_at: expires_at !== undefined ? expires_at : null,
      subscription_months: subscription_months !== undefined ? subscription_months : 1,
      wallets: Array.isArray(wallets) ? wallets : undefined,
    };
    db[id] = current;
    saveDatabase(db);
    return res.json({ success: true, data: current });
  }

  if (current.is_active) {
    return res.status(403).json({
      success: false,
      error: 'هذا الكود مفعّل مسبقاً بالفعل. اتصل بالمسؤول لتغيير الوجهة.',
    });
  }

  current.is_active = true;
  current.target_url = target_url;
  current.app_type = app_type || 'website';
  current.updated_at = now;
  if (expires_at !== undefined) current.expires_at = expires_at;
  if (subscription_months !== undefined) current.subscription_months = subscription_months;
  if (Array.isArray(wallets)) current.wallets = wallets;

  saveDatabase(db);
  res.json({ success: true, data: current });
});

// Admin Exclusive Quick Edit (PROTECTED with requireAdminAuth)
app.put('/api/qr/:id', requireAdminAuth, (req, res) => {
  const { id } = req.params;
  const { target_url, app_type, label, expires_at, subscription_months, wallets } = req.body;

  if (target_url && !isSafeUrlScheme(target_url)) {
    return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: 'الرابط يحتوي على بروتوكول غير آمن' });
  }

  const db = loadDatabase();
  let current = db[id];
  const now = new Date().toISOString();

  if (!current) {
    current = {
      id,
      is_active: true,
      target_url: target_url || null,
      app_type: app_type || 'website',
      created_at: now,
      total_scans: 0,
      wallets: Array.isArray(wallets) ? wallets : undefined,
    };
    db[id] = current;
  }

  let normalizedTarget = target_url;
  const effectiveType = app_type || current?.app_type || 'website';
  if (normalizedTarget && typeof normalizedTarget === 'string') {
    if (effectiveType === 'tiktok') normalizedTarget = formatTikTokTargetUrl(normalizedTarget);
    else if (effectiveType === 'instagram') normalizedTarget = formatInstagramTargetUrl(normalizedTarget);
    else if (effectiveType === 'telegram') normalizedTarget = formatTelegramTargetUrl(normalizedTarget);
    else if (effectiveType === 'snapchat') normalizedTarget = formatSnapchatTargetUrl(normalizedTarget);
  }

  if (target_url !== undefined) current.target_url = normalizedTarget;
  if (app_type !== undefined) current.app_type = app_type;
  if (label !== undefined) current.label = label;
  if (expires_at !== undefined) current.expires_at = expires_at;
  if (subscription_months !== undefined) current.subscription_months = subscription_months;
  if (Array.isArray(wallets)) current.wallets = wallets;
  current.is_active = true;
  current.updated_at = now;

  saveDatabase(db);
  res.json({ success: true, data: current });
});

// Debounced scan counter
const recentScansDebounce = new Map<string, number>();

app.post('/api/qr/:id/scan', (req, res) => {
  const { id } = req.params;
  const db = loadDatabase();
  const record = db[id];
  if (record && record.is_active) {
    const rawIp = (req.headers['x-forwarded-for'] as string) || req.socket.remoteAddress || 'unknown';
    const clientIp = Array.isArray(rawIp) ? rawIp[0] : (rawIp.split(',')[0] || '').trim();
    const debounceKey = `${id}_${clientIp}`;
    const now = Date.now();
    const lastScanTime = recentScansDebounce.get(debounceKey) || 0;

    if (now - lastScanTime > 3000) {
      recentScansDebounce.set(debounceKey, now);
      record.total_scans = (record.total_scans || 0) + 1;
      saveDatabase(db);
    }
  }
  res.json({ success: true, total_scans: record ? (record.total_scans || 0) : 0 });
});

// Reset Scan Counter (PROTECTED with requireAdminAuth)
app.post('/api/qr/:id/reset-scans', requireAdminAuth, (req, res) => {
  const { id } = req.params;
  const { count } = req.body;
  const db = loadDatabase();
  const record = db[id];
  if (!record) {
    return res.status(404).json({ success: false, error: 'الكود غير موجود' });
  }

  record.total_scans = typeof count === 'number' && count >= 0 ? count : 0;
  saveDatabase(db);
  res.json({ success: true, data: record, message: 'تم تعديل العداد بنجاح' });
});

async function deleteFromFirestore(id: string): Promise<void> {
  const pId = firebaseConfig?.projectId || FIRESTORE_PROJECT_ID;
  const dbId = firebaseConfig?.firestoreDatabaseId || FIRESTORE_DATABASE_ID;
  const key = firebaseConfig?.apiKey || 'AIzaSyAjyCP484AEQ52-9CDgp8TDkAw5woA1TFc';
  try {
    const docUrl = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${pId}/databases/${dbId}/documents/qr_codes/${encodeURIComponent(id)}?key=${key}`;
    await fetch(docUrl, { method: 'DELETE' });
  } catch (fsErr) {
    console.warn('Server failed to delete from Firestore REST:', fsErr);
  }
}

// Delete QR Code permanently (PROTECTED with requireAdminAuth)
// Deletes from both local server JSON DB and Cloud Firestore permanently, whether active or inactive
app.delete('/api/qr/:id', requireAdminAuth, async (req, res) => {
  const { id } = req.params;
  const db = loadDatabase();
  if (db[id]) {
    delete db[id];
    saveDatabase(db);
  }

  // Purge permanently from Cloud Firestore via REST API
  await deleteFromFirestore(id);

  res.json({ success: true, message: 'تم حذف الكود نهائياً من قاعدة البيانات والسيرفر' });
});

// Update platform settings (PROTECTED with requireAdminAuth)
app.post('/api/settings', requireAdminAuth, (req, res) => {
  const { developer_whatsapp, developer_contact_url, developer_title, support_message, public_domain_url } = req.body;
  const current = loadSettings();

  let formattedUrl = (developer_contact_url || '').trim();
  let cleanPhone = (developer_whatsapp || '').trim();

  if (!formattedUrl && cleanPhone) {
    let num = cleanPhone.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
    if (num.startsWith('01')) num = '2' + num;
    formattedUrl = `https://wa.me/${num}`;
  }

  let cleanDomain = (public_domain_url !== undefined ? public_domain_url : current.public_domain_url || '').trim();
  if (cleanDomain.endsWith('/')) {
    cleanDomain = cleanDomain.slice(0, -1);
  }

  const updated: PlatformSettings = {
    developer_whatsapp: cleanPhone || current.developer_whatsapp,
    developer_contact_url: formattedUrl || current.developer_contact_url,
    developer_title: developer_title || current.developer_title || 'تواصل مع المطور',
    support_message: support_message || current.support_message || 'مرحباً، أود التواصل بخصوص البطاقة الذكية',
    public_domain_url: cleanDomain,
  };

  saveSettings(updated);
  res.json({ success: true, data: updated, message: 'تم حفظ إعدادات التواصل والدومين بنجاح' });
});

// QR Code Instant Redirection Route
app.get('/q/:id', async (req, res, next) => {
  const { id } = req.params;
  const db = loadDatabase();
  let record = db[id];

  res.set('Cache-Control', 'no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, proxy-revalidate, max-age=0');
  res.set('Pragma', 'no-cache');
  res.set('Expires', '0');

  // Always reconcile against live Cloud Firestore so edits made on Vercel or Admin are immediately reflected on existing QR codes
  const fsRecord = await fetchQrFromFirestoreRestServer(id);
  if (fsRecord) {
    const localTime = record ? new Date(record.updated_at || record.created_at || 0).getTime() : 0;
    const fsTime = new Date(fsRecord.updated_at || fsRecord.created_at || 0).getTime();
    if (!record || fsTime >= localTime) {
      record = {
        ...fsRecord,
        total_scans: Math.max(record?.total_scans || 0, fsRecord.total_scans || 0),
      };
      db[id] = record;
      saveDatabase(db);
    }
  }

  if (!record) {
    return next();
  }

  const isPrefetch = req.headers['purpose'] === 'prefetch' ||
    req.headers['sec-purpose'] === 'prefetch' ||
    req.headers['x-purpose'] === 'preview';

  if (record.is_active && !isPrefetch) {
    const rawIp = (req.headers['x-forwarded-for'] as string) || req.socket.remoteAddress || 'unknown';
    const clientIp = Array.isArray(rawIp) ? rawIp[0] : (rawIp.split(',')[0] || '').trim();
    const debounceKey = `${id}_${clientIp}`;
    const now = Date.now();
    const lastScanTime = recentScansDebounce.get(debounceKey) || 0;

    if (now - lastScanTime > 3000) {
      recentScansDebounce.set(debounceKey, now);
      record.total_scans = (record.total_scans || 0) + 1;
      saveDatabase(db);

      if (recentScansDebounce.size > 2000) {
        for (const [k, time] of recentScansDebounce.entries()) {
          if (now - time > 15000) recentScansDebounce.delete(k);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const isExpired = record.expires_at && new Date(record.expires_at).getTime() < Date.now();
  if (isExpired) {
    return next();
  }

  if (record.is_active && record.target_url && record.app_type !== 'bank' && !record.target_url.startsWith('bank://')) {
    let target = record.target_url.trim();

    // Block dangerous schemes
    if (!isSafeUrlScheme(target) || target.startsWith('intent:')) {
      return next();
    }

    // Wallet: ALWAYS route to frontend SPA so customer sees luxury card screen with company logos & quick pay buttons
    const hasWallets = Array.isArray(record.wallets) && record.wallets.length > 0;
    if (record.app_type === 'wallet' || record.app_type === 'bank' || hasWallets || target.startsWith('tel:*9*7*')) {
      return next();
    }

    // Normalize schemes and platforms
    if (record.app_type === 'tiktok' || target.includes('tiktok.com') || (!target.startsWith('http') && record.app_type === 'tiktok')) {
      target = formatTikTokTargetUrl(target);
    } else if (record.app_type === 'instagram' || target.includes('instagram.com') || (!target.startsWith('http') && record.app_type === 'instagram')) {
      target = formatInstagramTargetUrl(target);
    } else if (record.app_type === 'telegram' || target.includes('t.me') || (!target.startsWith('http') && record.app_type === 'telegram')) {
      target = formatTelegramTargetUrl(target);
    } else if (record.app_type === 'snapchat' || target.includes('snapchat.com') || (!target.startsWith('http') && record.app_type === 'snapchat')) {
      target = formatSnapchatTargetUrl(target);
    } else if (target.startsWith('instapay://send?to=')) {
      const handle = decodeURIComponent(target.replace('instapay://send?to=', ''));
      target = `https://ipn.eg/${encodeURIComponent(handle.replace('@instapay', ''))}`;
    } else if (target.startsWith('wa.me/')) {
      target = `https://${target}`;
    } else if (!target.startsWith('http://') && !target.startsWith('https://') && !target.startsWith('tel:') && !target.startsWith('mailto:')) {
      if (/^(?:\+?20|0)?1[0125]\d{8}$/.test(target)) {
        let num = target.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
        if (num.startsWith('01')) num = '2' + num;
        target = `https://wa.me/${num}`;
      } else {
        target = `https://${target}`;
      }
    }

    if (req.query.preview === '1') {
      return next();
    }

    res.set('Cache-Control', 'no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, private, max-age=0');
    return res.redirect(302, target);
  }

  next();
});

// Start dev server with Vite or static production
async function startServer() {
  if (!isProd) {
    const { createServer } = await import('vite');
    const vite = await createServer({
      server: { 
        middlewareMode: true,
        hmr: false,
      },
      appType: 'spa',
    });
    app.use(vite.middlewares);
  } else {
    app.use(express.static(path.join(__dirname, 'dist')));
    app.get('*', (req, res) => {
      res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, 'dist', 'index.html'));
    });
  }

  app.listen(PORT, '0.0.0.0', () => {
    console.log(`Server running on http://0.0.0.0:${PORT}`);
  });
}

startServer();

const PROJECT_ID = 'clean-plateau-dv4kj';
const DATABASE_ID = 'ai-studio-41f04a06-1999-41b9-b7be-e25b21dc1fd0';
const API_KEY = 'AIzaSyAjyCP484AEQ52-9CDgp8TDkAw5woA1TFc';

/**
 * Triggers an atomic increment in Firestore via REST API transform.
 * Non-blocking, ultra-fast, and strictly executed only for confirmed active cards.
 */
function triggerServerScanIncrement(id: string) {
  const commitUrl = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${PROJECT_ID}/databases/${DATABASE_ID}/documents:commit?key=${API_KEY}`;
  fetch(commitUrl, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      writes: [
        {
          transform: {
            document: `projects/${PROJECT_ID}/databases/${DATABASE_ID}/documents/qr_codes/${id}`,
            fieldTransforms: [
              {
                fieldPath: 'total_scans',
                increment: { integerValue: '1' }
              },
              {
                fieldPath: 'last_scanned_at',
                setToServerValue: 'REQUEST_TIME'
              }
            ]
          }
        }
      ]
    })
  }).catch(() => {});
}

export default async function handler(req: any, res: any) {
  // Always disable caching for QR scans
  res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, proxy-revalidate, max-age=0');
  res.setHeader('Pragma', 'no-cache');
  res.setHeader('Expires', '0');

  // Extract ID from query (?id=...) or path
  const queryId = (req.query?.id || '').toString().trim();
  const urlPath = req.url || '';
  const pathMatch = urlPath.match(/\/q\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
  const id = queryId || (pathMatch ? pathMatch[1] : '');

  if (!id) {
    return res.writeHead(302, { Location: '/' }).end();
  }

  // Detect bots and browser prefetch requests
  const userAgent = (req.headers['user-agent'] || '').toString();
  const isPrefetch = Boolean(
    req.headers['purpose'] === 'prefetch' ||
    req.headers['sec-purpose'] === 'prefetch' ||
    req.headers['x-purpose'] === 'preview' ||
    req.headers['x-moz'] === 'prefetch' ||
    /bot|crawl|spider|facebookexternalhit|whatsapp|telegrambot|preview|slackbot|twitterbot/i.test(userAgent)
  );

  try {
    const docUrl = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${PROJECT_ID}/databases/${DATABASE_ID}/documents/qr_codes/${encodeURIComponent(id)}?key=${API_KEY}`;
    const response = await fetch(docUrl);

    if (!response.ok) {
      // Document not found or not in Firestore -> redirect to customer activation
      // NEVER COUNT SCANS BEFORE ACTIVATION!
      return res.writeHead(302, { Location: `/?activate=${id}` }).end();
    }

    const data = await response.json();
    const fields = data.fields || {};

    const isActive = fields.is_active?.booleanValue ?? false;
    const targetUrl = fields.target_url?.stringValue ?? '';
    const appType = fields.app_type?.stringValue ?? 'website';
    const expiresAtStr = fields.expires_at?.stringValue;

    // 1. If not active yet -> redirect directly to customer activation screen
    // CRITICAL REQUIREMENT: NEVER count scans before activation (even if scanned 1,000,000 times!)
    if (!isActive) {
      return res.writeHead(302, { Location: `/?activate=${id}` }).end();
    }

    // 2. Check expiration
    if (expiresAtStr) {
      const expiresAt = new Date(expiresAtStr).getTime();
      if (expiresAt < Date.now()) {
        return res.writeHead(302, { Location: `/?expired=${id}` }).end();
      }
    }

    // 3. Increment the scan counter EXACTLY ONCE on the server (debouncing bots & prefetch)
    if (!isPrefetch) {
      triggerServerScanIncrement(id);
    }

    // 4. Bank accounts always require copy screen
    if (appType === 'bank' || targetUrl.startsWith('bank://')) {
      return res.writeHead(302, { Location: `/?view=${id}&scanned=1` }).end();
    }

    // 5. Wallet: ALWAYS route to dedicated luxury card screen (/?view=${id}&scanned=1)
    // so customer sees company logos, owner names, phone numbers, and quick pay transfer buttons!
    const hasWallets = Boolean(fields.wallets?.arrayValue?.values?.length);
    if (appType === 'wallet' || hasWallets || targetUrl.startsWith('tel:*9*7*') || /vf\.eg\/vfcash/i.test(targetUrl)) {
      return res.writeHead(302, { Location: `/?view=${id}&scanned=1` }).end();
    }

    // Check for dangerous schemes (javascript:, data:, vbscript:)
    const sanitizedUrl = targetUrl.trim().replace(/[\x00-\x1f\x7f]/g, '');
    if (/^(?:javascript|data|vbscript|file|blob|about):/i.test(sanitizedUrl.toLowerCase()) || targetUrl.startsWith('intent:')) {
      return res.writeHead(302, { Location: `/?view=${id}&scanned=1` }).end();
    }

    // 6. Direct Link (WhatsApp, Instagram, Facebook, TikTok, Website, Phone, InstaPay Link, or Wallet Payment Link)
    let destination = sanitizedUrl;

    if (appType === 'tiktok' || destination.includes('tiktok.com') || (!destination.startsWith('http') && appType === 'tiktok')) {
      destination = formatTikTokTargetUrl(destination);
    } else if (appType === 'instagram' || destination.includes('instagram.com') || (!destination.startsWith('http') && appType === 'instagram')) {
      destination = formatInstagramTargetUrl(destination);
    } else if (appType === 'telegram' || destination.includes('t.me') || (!destination.startsWith('http') && appType === 'telegram')) {
      destination = formatTelegramTargetUrl(destination);
    } else if (appType === 'snapchat' || destination.includes('snapchat.com') || (!destination.startsWith('http') && appType === 'snapchat')) {
      destination = formatSnapchatTargetUrl(destination);
    } else if (destination.startsWith('wa.me/')) {
      destination = `https://${destination}`;
    } else if (!destination.startsWith('http://') && !destination.startsWith('https://') && !destination.startsWith('tel:')) {
      if (/^(?:\+?20|0)?1[0125]\d{8}$/.test(destination)) {
        let num = destination.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
        if (num.startsWith('01')) num = '2' + num;
        destination = `https://wa.me/${num}`;
      } else {
        destination = `https://${destination}`;
      }
    }

    // Send INSTANT HTTP 302 Redirection with HTML fallback body for universal mobile browser & app support
    res.writeHead(302, {
      Location: destination,
      'Content-Type': 'text/html; charset=utf-8'
    });
    return res.end(`<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <meta http-equiv="refresh" content="0;url=${destination}">
  <title>جاري فتح الرابط...</title>
  <script>location.replace(${JSON.stringify(destination)});</script>
</head>
<body style="background:#09090b;color:#facc15;font-family:sans-serif;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;height:100vh;margin:0;">
  <p>جاري تحويلك إلى الرابط مباشرة... <a href="${destination}" style="color:#facc15;text-decoration:underline;">اضغط هنا إذا لم يتم الفتح تلقائياً</a></p>
</body>
</html>`);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Serverless redirect error:', err);
    // Fallback to client SPA
    return res.writeHead(302, { Location: `/?scan=${id}` }).end();
  }
}

function formatTikTokTargetUrl(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  let clean = input
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
    .trim();

  if (!clean) return '';

  if (/^(?:https?:\/\/)?(?:vm|vt)\.tiktok\.com\//i.test(clean)) {
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }

  if (clean.includes('tiktok.com')) {
    const parts = clean.split('?');
    const pathPart = parts[0];
    const queryPart = parts[1] ? `?${parts[1]}` : '';

    const match = pathPart.match(/tiktok\.com\/(?:@)?([a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      const segment = match[1].replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase().trim();
      const reservedRoutes = ['discover', 'explore', 'upload', 'live', 'tag', 'music', 'about', 'legal', 'business', 'share', 'foryou'];
      if (!reservedRoutes.includes(segment)) {
        return `https://www.tiktok.com/@${segment}${queryPart}`;
      }
    }
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }

  let username = clean.replace(/^[/@]+/, '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0].trim();
  username = username.replace(/[^\w.-]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();

  if (username) {
    return `https://www.tiktok.com/@${username}`;
  }

  return clean;
}

function formatInstagramTargetUrl(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  let clean = input
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
    .trim();

  if (clean.includes('instagram.com/')) {
    const match = clean.match(/instagram\.com\/([a-zA-Z0-9._]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      const user = match[1].replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase().trim();
      return `https://www.instagram.com/${user}/`;
    }
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }
  let username = clean.replace(/^[/@]+/, '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0].trim();
  username = username.replace(/[^\w.]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (username) {
    return `https://www.instagram.com/${username}/`;
  }
  return clean;
}

function formatTelegramTargetUrl(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  let clean = input
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
    .trim();

  if (clean.includes('t.me/') || clean.includes('telegram.me/')) {
    const match = clean.match(/(?:t\.me|telegram\.me)\/([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return `https://t.me/${match[1].replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase().trim()}`;
    }
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }
  let username = clean.replace(/^[/@]+/, '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0].trim();
  username = username.replace(/[^\w]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (username) {
    return `https://t.me/${username}`;
  }
  return clean;
}

function formatSnapchatTargetUrl(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  let clean = input
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
    .trim();

  if (clean.includes('snapchat.com/add/')) {
    const match = clean.match(/snapchat\.com\/add\/([a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return `https://www.snapchat.com/add/${match[1].replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase().trim()}`;
    }
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }
  let username = clean.replace(/^[/@]+/, '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0].trim();
  username = username.replace(/[^\w.-]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (username) {
    return `https://www.snapchat.com/add/${username}`;
  }
  return clean;
}

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { isAdminAuthenticated, logoutAdmin, validateAdminSession } from './lib/auth';
import { getQrCode, fetchPlatformSettings, recordQrScan, subscribeToSingleQrCode } from './lib/qrService';
import { processSmartLink, triggerSmartRedirect } from './lib/deepLinks';
import { AdminLoginView } from './views/AdminLoginView';
import { AdminDashboardView } from './views/AdminDashboardView';
import { CustomerActivationView } from './views/CustomerActivationView';

import { HomeLandingView } from './views/HomeLandingView';
import { PWAInstallBanner } from './components/PWAInstallBanner';
import { QrCodeRecord, PlatformSettings } from './types/qr';
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';

export default function App() {
  const [currentPath, setCurrentPath] = useState<string>(() => window.location.pathname || '/');
  const searchParams = new URLSearchParams(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.search : '');
  const activateIdFromQuery = searchParams.get('activate');
  const viewIdFromQuery = searchParams.get('view');
  const expiredIdFromQuery = searchParams.get('expired');
  const scanIdFromQuery = searchParams.get('scan');

  const isQrScanPath = currentPath.startsWith('/q/') || Boolean(scanIdFromQuery || viewIdFromQuery || expiredIdFromQuery || activateIdFromQuery);
  const qrIdFromPath = isQrScanPath 
    ? (scanIdFromQuery || viewIdFromQuery || expiredIdFromQuery || activateIdFromQuery || currentPath.replace('/q/', '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0]) 
    : null;

  const [activeRecord, setActiveRecord] = useState<QrCodeRecord | null>(null);
  const [domainUrl, setDomainUrl] = useState<string>(() => window.location.origin);
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<PlatformSettings>({
    developer_whatsapp: '01550523365',
    developer_contact_url: 'https://wa.me/201550523365',
    developer_title: 'تواصل مع المطور',
    support_message: 'مرحباً، أود التواصل بخصوص البطاقة الذكية',
    public_domain_url: 'https://smart-card-inky.vercel.app',
  });

  // Sync domainUrl and fetch platform settings
  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      setDomainUrl(window.location.origin);
      fetchPlatformSettings().then((s) => setSettings(s));
    }
  }, []);

  // Listen to popstate for browser back/forward buttons
  useEffect(() => {
    const handlePopState = () => {
      setCurrentPath(window.location.pathname || '/');
    };
    window.addEventListener('popstate', handlePopState);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('popstate', handlePopState);
  }, []);

  const navigate = (path: string) => {
    window.history.pushState({}, '', path);
    setCurrentPath(path);
    window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  };

  const isAdminPath = currentPath.startsWith('/admin') && currentPath !== '/admin/login';
  const isLoginPath = currentPath === '/admin/login';

  // Middleware: If accessing /admin and not authenticated, redirect to /admin/login
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isAdminPath) {
      if (!isAdminAuthenticated()) {
        navigate('/admin/login');
      } else {
        validateAdminSession().then((isValid) => {
          if (!isValid) {
            navigate('/admin/login');
          }
        });
      }
    } else if (isLoginPath && isAdminAuthenticated()) {
      navigate('/admin');
    }
  }, [currentPath, isAdminPath, isLoginPath]);

  const [isResolvingQr, setIsResolvingQr] = useState<boolean>(Boolean(isQrScanPath && qrIdFromPath));
  const hasRecordedScanRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const alreadyScannedOnServer = searchParams.get('scanned') === '1';

  // Load QR record when accessing /q/:id, record scan count LIVE, and instant redirect
  useEffect(() => {
    if (qrIdFromPath) {
      let isMounted = true;
      setIsResolvingQr(true);

      // If already recorded on server (api/redirect.ts) or activation flow, do not double-record on client
      if (alreadyScannedOnServer || activateIdFromQuery) {
        hasRecordedScanRef.current = true;
      }

      const fetchPromise = getQrCode(qrIdFromPath);
      const timeoutPromise = new Promise<null>((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(null), 2500));

      Promise.race([fetchPromise, timeoutPromise])
        .then(async (record) => {
          if (!isMounted) return;

          if (record) {
            setActiveRecord(record);

            // CRITICAL RULE: NEVER count scans before activation (record.is_active must be true)
            // AND only record if not already recorded by the serverless redirect handler
            if (record.is_active && !hasRecordedScanRef.current && !alreadyScannedOnServer && !activateIdFromQuery) {
              hasRecordedScanRef.current = true;

              // Check if not expired and direct link:
              const isExpired = record.expires_at && new Date(record.expires_at).getTime() < Date.now();
              const isWalletCard = record.app_type === 'wallet' || (Array.isArray(record.wallets) && record.wallets.length > 0) || (Boolean(record.target_url) && record.target_url!.startsWith('tel:*9*7*'));
              const isBankCard = record.app_type === 'bank' || (Boolean(record.target_url) && record.target_url!.startsWith('bank://'));

              if (!isExpired && record.target_url && !isWalletCard && !isBankCard) {
                const linkInfo = processSmartLink(record.target_url, record.app_type);
                const isDirectRedirect = !linkInfo.isBankCard && !linkInfo.isInstaPayCard && !linkInfo.isWalletCard;
                if (isDirectRedirect) {
                  const target = linkInfo.schemeUrl || linkInfo.fallbackUrl;
                  if (target && target !== '#' && !target.startsWith('intent:')) {
                    try {
                      await Promise.race([
                        recordQrScan(record.id),
                        new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 150)),
                      ]);
                    } catch {}

                    // DIRECT INSTANT NAVIGATION - zero extra pages!
                    try {
                      window.location.replace(target);
                    } catch {
                      window.location.href = target;
                    }
                    return;
                  }
                }
              }

              // Otherwise record scan normally (for bank card, wallet, or expired)
              recordQrScan(record.id);
            }
          }
        })
        .catch((err) => {
          console.warn('QR resolution error:', err);
        })
        .finally(() => {
          if (isMounted) setIsResolvingQr(false);
        });

      // Real-time Firestore snapshot listener: instantly updates the UI if the admin edits the wallet number!
      const unsubLive = subscribeToSingleQrCode(qrIdFromPath, (liveRecord) => {
        if (!isMounted) return;
        if (liveRecord) {
          setActiveRecord(liveRecord);
          setIsResolvingQr(false);
        }
      });

      return () => {
        isMounted = false;
        unsubLive();
      };
    } else {
      setActiveRecord(null);
      setIsResolvingQr(false);
    }
  }, [qrIdFromPath]);

  // Compute effective domain URL (custom production domain if configured, else Vercel live domain)
  const effectiveDomainUrl = (settings.public_domain_url && settings.public_domain_url.trim()) 
    ? settings.public_domain_url.trim() 
    : 'https://smart-card-inky.vercel.app';

  // Direct Scan Simulation: opens real /q/:id URL to trigger instant redirection
  const handleSimulateScan = (id: string) => {
    window.open(`${effectiveDomainUrl}/q/${id}`, '_blank');
  };

  // Route 1: QR Scan path /q/:id or direct action query
  if (isQrScanPath && qrIdFromPath) {
    // 1. While resolving: show sleek branded loader instead of blank black screen
    if (isResolvingQr && !activeRecord) {
      return (
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#09090b] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center select-none animate-in fade-in duration-200">
          <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/30 flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 mb-4">
            <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 text-amber-400 animate-spin" />
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-amber-200/80 font-bold">جاري فتح البطاقة الذكية...</p>
        </div>
      );
    }

    // 2. If already active, check expiration
    if (activeRecord && activeRecord.is_active) {
      const isExpired = activeRecord.expires_at && new Date(activeRecord.expires_at).getTime() < Date.now();
      if (isExpired) {
        return <CardExpiredView record={activeRecord} settings={settings} />;
      }
      return <SmartRedirectView record={activeRecord} />;
    }

    // 3. Direct First Scan Customer Activation (Always static, fast, and ready!)
    return (
      <CustomerActivationView
        qrId={qrIdFromPath}
        onActivated={(updated) => {
          setActiveRecord(updated);
        }}
      />
    );
  }

  // Route 2: Admin Login
  if (isLoginPath) {
    return (
      <>
        <AdminLoginView
          onLoginSuccess={() => navigate('/admin')}
          onNavigateHome={() => navigate('/')}
        />
        <PWAInstallBanner />
      </>
    );
  }

  // Route 3: Admin Dashboard (Protected)
  if (isAdminPath) {
    return (
      <>
        <AdminDashboardView
          onLogout={() => {
            logoutAdmin();
            navigate('/admin/login');
          }}
          domainUrl={effectiveDomainUrl}
          onSimulateScan={handleSimulateScan}
        />
        <PWAInstallBanner />
      </>
    );
  }

  // Route 4: Home Landing View
  return (
    <>
      <HomeLandingView
        onGoToAdmin={() => {
          if (isAdminAuthenticated()) {
            navigate('/admin');
          } else {
            navigate('/admin/login');
          }
        }}
        onSimulateScan={handleSimulateScan}
        domainUrl={effectiveDomainUrl}
      />
      <PWAInstallBanner />
    </>
  );
}

declare module 'jsqr' {
  interface QRCodePoint {
    x: number;
    y: number;
  }

  interface QRCode {
    binaryData: number[];
    data: string;
    chunks: any[];
    version: number;
    location: {
      topRightCorner: QRCodePoint;
      topLeftCorner: QRCodePoint;
      bottomRightCorner: QRCodePoint;
      bottomLeftCorner: QRCodePoint;
      topRightFinderPattern: QRCodePoint;
      topLeftFinderPattern: QRCodePoint;
      bottomLeftFinderPattern: QRCodePoint;
      bottomRightAlignmentPattern?: QRCodePoint;
    };
  }

  interface Options {
    inversionAttempts?: 'dontInvert' | 'onlyInvert' | 'attemptBoth' | 'invertFirst';
  }

  function jsQR(
    data: Uint8ClampedArray,
    width: number,
    height: number,
    options?: Options
  ): QRCode | null;

  export default jsQR;
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/hooks/usePWAInstall.ts
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

interface BeforeInstallPromptEvent extends Event {
  prompt: () => Promise<void>;
  userChoice: Promise<{ outcome: 'accepted' | 'dismissed'; platform: string }>;
}

export function usePWAInstall() {
  const [deferredPrompt, setDeferredPrompt] = useState<BeforeInstallPromptEvent | null>(null);
  const [isInstalled, setIsInstalled] = useState(false);
  const [isIOS, setIsIOS] = useState(false);
  const [isStandalone, setIsStandalone] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Check if running in standalone mode (already installed on phone)
    const standaloneCheck =
      window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches ||
      (window.navigator as unknown as { standalone?: boolean }).standalone === true ||
      document.referrer.includes('android-app://');

    setIsInstalled(standaloneCheck);
    setIsStandalone(standaloneCheck);

    // Detect iOS
    const ua = window.navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase();
    const isIOSDevice = /iphone|ipad|ipod/.test(ua) && !(window as any).MSStream;
    setIsIOS(isIOSDevice);

    const handleBeforeInstallPrompt = (e: Event) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      setDeferredPrompt(e as BeforeInstallPromptEvent);
    };

    const handleAppInstalled = () => {
      setIsInstalled(true);
      setIsStandalone(true);
      setDeferredPrompt(null);
    };

    window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', handleBeforeInstallPrompt);
    window.addEventListener('appinstalled', handleAppInstalled);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', handleBeforeInstallPrompt);
      window.removeEventListener('appinstalled', handleAppInstalled);
    };
  }, []);

  const install = async (): Promise<boolean> => {
    if (!deferredPrompt) return false;
    try {
      await deferredPrompt.prompt();
      const choice = await deferredPrompt.userChoice;
      if (choice.outcome === 'accepted') {
        setIsInstalled(true);
        setIsStandalone(true);
        setDeferredPrompt(null);
        return true;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('PWA install prompt error:', err);
    }
    return false;
  };

  return {
    isInstallable: !!deferredPrompt,
    isInstalled,
    isStandalone,
    isIOS,
    install,
  };
}

import { initializeApp } from 'firebase/app';
import { getAuth } from 'firebase/auth';
import { doc, getDocFromServer, initializeFirestore } from 'firebase/firestore';
import firebaseConfig from '../../firebase-applet-config.json';

const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);

// CRITICAL: The app requires firebaseConfig.firestoreDatabaseId with auto-detect long polling for iframe resilience
export const db = initializeFirestore(app, {
  experimentalAutoDetectLongPolling: true,
  ignoreUndefinedProperties: true,
}, firebaseConfig.firestoreDatabaseId);
export const auth = getAuth(app);

export enum OperationType {
  CREATE = 'create',
  UPDATE = 'update',
  DELETE = 'delete',
  LIST = 'list',
  GET = 'get',
  WRITE = 'write',
}

export interface FirestoreErrorInfo {
  error: string;
  operationType: OperationType;
  path: string | null;
  authInfo: {
    userId?: string | null;
    email?: string | null;
    emailVerified?: boolean | null;
    isAnonymous?: boolean | null;
    tenantId?: string | null;
    providerInfo?: {
      providerId?: string | null;
      email?: string | null;
    }[];
  };
}

export function handleFirestoreError(error: unknown, operationType: OperationType, path: string | null): never {
  const errInfo: FirestoreErrorInfo = {
    error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
    authInfo: {
      userId: auth.currentUser?.uid,
      email: auth.currentUser?.email,
      emailVerified: auth.currentUser?.emailVerified,
      isAnonymous: auth.currentUser?.isAnonymous,
      tenantId: auth.currentUser?.tenantId,
      providerInfo: auth.currentUser?.providerData?.map(provider => ({
        providerId: provider.providerId,
        email: provider.email,
      })) || [],
    },
    operationType,
    path,
  };
  console.error('Firestore Error:', JSON.stringify(errInfo));
  throw new Error(JSON.stringify(errInfo));
}

// Test connection on boot
async function testConnection() {
  try {
    await getDocFromServer(doc(db, 'test', 'connection'));
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error && error.message.includes('the client is offline')) {
      console.warn('Firestore client is offline, check connection.');
    }
  }
}

testConnection().catch(() => {});

import { db } from './firebase';
import { doc, getDoc, setDoc } from 'firebase/firestore';

const ADMIN_TOKEN_KEY = 'smart_card_admin_session_token';
const ADMIN_TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY = 'smart_card_admin_session_expiry';
const ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY = 'smart_card_admin_pwd_hash';

// Default initial password hash (SHA-256 for "admin1234")
export const DEFAULT_BASE_HASH = 'ac9689e2272427085e35b9d3e3e8bed88cb3434828b43b86fc0596cad4c6e270';

const FIRESTORE_PROJECT_ID = 'clean-plateau-dv4kj';
const FIRESTORE_DATABASE_ID = 'ai-studio-41f04a06-1999-41b9-b7be-e25b21dc1fd0';
const FIRESTORE_API_KEY = 'AIzaSyAjyCP484AEQ52-9CDgp8TDkAw5woA1TFc';

const SEVEN_DAYS_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

// Clean insecure legacy keys if present
try {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    localStorage.removeItem('dynamic_qr_admin_password');
    if (localStorage.getItem('dynamic_qr_admin_session') === 'authenticated_admin_token') {
      localStorage.removeItem('dynamic_qr_admin_session');
    }
  }
} catch {}

/**
 * Computes SHA-256 cryptographic hash of a string using Web Crypto API
 */
export async function hashPassword(plain: string): Promise<string> {
  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
  const data = encoder.encode(plain);
  const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', data);
  const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
  return hashArray.map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

/**
 * Gets the current active session token
 */
export function getAdminToken(): string | null {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_KEY) || sessionStorage.getItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_KEY);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Authenticated fetch helper that automatically attaches Bearer token to requests
 */
export async function authFetch(input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit): Promise<Response> {
  const token = getAdminToken();
  const headers = new Headers(init?.headers || {});
  if (token) {
    headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`);
    headers.set('X-Admin-Token', token);
  }
  return fetch(input, {
    ...init,
    headers,
    credentials: 'include', // include HTTP-Only cookies
  });
}

/**
 * Checks if admin session is locally active and unexpired
 */
export function isAdminAuthenticated(): boolean {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;
  const token = getAdminToken();
  if (!token || token.length < 16) return false;

  try {
    const expiryStr = localStorage.getItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY) || sessionStorage.getItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY);
    if (expiryStr) {
      const expiry = parseInt(expiryStr, 10);
      if (!isNaN(expiry) && expiry <= Date.now()) {
        logoutAdmin();
        return false;
      }
    }
  } catch {}

  return true;
}

/**
 * Verifies active session with the backend server without breaking on static hosts (e.g. Vercel)
 */
export async function validateAdminSession(): Promise<boolean> {
  const token = getAdminToken();
  if (!token) return false;

  // First verify local session validity
  if (!isAdminAuthenticated()) return false;

  try {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 2000);

    const res = await authFetch('/api/admin/session', {
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);

    const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    if (res.ok && contentType.includes('application/json')) {
      const data = await res.json();
      if (typeof data.authenticated === 'boolean') {
        if (!data.authenticated) {
          // Explicit invalidation by server
          logoutAdmin();
          return false;
        }
        return true;
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // If backend is unreachable or on a static host (like Vercel SPA), fallback to local valid token
  }

  // Session remains valid locally
  return true;
}

/**
 * Retrieves the currently active admin password hash directly from Cloud Database (Firestore).
 * Uses direct Firestore REST API for 100% reliable, unblockable fetching across all browsers,
 * Incognito tabs, and Brave Shields without third-party cookie or IndexedDB constraints.
 */
export async function getActivePasswordHash(): Promise<string> {
  // 1. Primary: Direct HTTPS REST query to Firestore cloud database
  try {
    const restUrl = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIRESTORE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/${FIRESTORE_DATABASE_ID}/documents/settings/auth?key=${FIRESTORE_API_KEY}`;
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 2500);
    const res = await fetch(restUrl, { signal: controller.signal, cache: 'no-store' });
    clearTimeout(timer);

    if (res.ok) {
      const data = await res.json();
      const hashVal = data.fields?.hash?.stringValue;
      if (typeof hashVal === 'string' && hashVal.length === 64) {
        try { localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, hashVal); } catch {}
        try { sessionStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, hashVal); } catch {}
        return hashVal;
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('REST auth fetch error, falling back to Firestore SDK:', err);
  }

  // 2. Secondary: Firestore SDK getDoc
  try {
    const snap = await getDoc(doc(db, 'settings', 'auth'));
    if (snap.exists() && snap.data()?.hash) {
      const h = snap.data().hash;
      if (typeof h === 'string' && h.length === 64) {
        try { localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, h); } catch {}
        try { sessionStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, h); } catch {}
        return h;
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Firestore SDK auth error:', err);
  }

  // 3. Tertiary: Local storage cache
  try {
    const local = localStorage.getItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY) || sessionStorage.getItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY);
    if (local && local.length === 64) {
      return local;
    }
  } catch {}

  // 4. Default base password ("admin1234") if nothing is in DB yet
  return DEFAULT_BASE_HASH;
}

/**
 * Saves a valid session token locally
 */
function storeAdminSession(token: string): void {
  const expiry = String(Date.now() + SEVEN_DAYS_MS);
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_KEY, token);
    localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY, expiry);
  } catch {}
  try {
    sessionStorage.setItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_KEY, token);
    sessionStorage.setItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY, expiry);
  } catch {}
}

/**
 * Performs admin login:
 * Authoritatively validates against the active password in Cloud Firestore.
 * Once the password is changed, ONLY the new password is valid.
 */
export async function loginAdmin(passwordAttempt: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const clean = (passwordAttempt || '').trim();
  if (!clean) return false;

  const attemptHash = await hashPassword(clean);
  const activeHash = await getActivePasswordHash();

  // Try Server API first (if Express server is running)
  try {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 2500);

    const serverRes = await fetch('/api/admin/login', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      credentials: 'include',
      body: JSON.stringify({ password: clean }),
      signal: controller.signal,
    });
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);

    const contentType = serverRes.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    if (serverRes.ok && contentType.includes('application/json')) {
      const data = await serverRes.json();
      if (data.success && data.token) {
        storeAdminSession(data.token);
        try {
          localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, attemptHash);
          sessionStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, attemptHash);
        } catch {}
        return true;
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // Server fetch unavailable (e.g. static hosting on Vercel), fall through to database check
  }

  // Strict match against active database password hash:
  // If password was updated in Firestore, ONLY that updated password works!
  if (attemptHash === activeHash) {
    let clientToken: string;
    try {
      clientToken = crypto.randomUUID ? crypto.randomUUID().replace(/-/g, '') + Date.now().toString(16) : 'adm_' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2) + Date.now().toString(36);
    } catch {
      clientToken = 'adm_token_' + Date.now().toString(36) + '_' + attemptHash.slice(0, 16);
    }

    storeAdminSession(clientToken);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, attemptHash);
      sessionStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, attemptHash);
    } catch {}

    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

/**
 * Secure password change requiring current password verification:
 * Updates the password across the ENTIRE database:
 * 1. Cloud Firestore database via REST API & Firestore SDK (`settings/auth` and replica `platform_settings/auth`)
 * 2. Server filesystem database (`data/auth.json`)
 * 3. Client storage cache
 */
export async function changeAdminPassword(
  currentPasswordPlain: string,
  newPasswordPlain: string
): Promise<{ success: boolean; message: string }> {
  const curClean = (currentPasswordPlain || '').trim();
  const newClean = (newPasswordPlain || '').trim();

  if (!curClean) {
    return { success: false, message: 'الرجاء إدخال كلمة المرور الحالية.' };
  }
  if (!newClean || newClean.length < 4) {
    return { success: false, message: 'كلمة المرور الجديدة يجب ألا تقل عن 4 أحرف أو أرقام.' };
  }

  const curHashAttempt = await hashPassword(curClean);
  const activeHash = await getActivePasswordHash();

  // Validate current password (must match active database password)
  const isCurrentValid = curHashAttempt === activeHash || (activeHash === DEFAULT_BASE_HASH && curHashAttempt === DEFAULT_BASE_HASH);
  if (!isCurrentValid) {
    return { success: false, message: 'كلمة المرور الحالية غير صحيحة.' };
  }

  const newHash = await hashPassword(newClean);
  const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();

  // 1. UPDATE CLOUD FIRESTORE DATABASE VIA REST API (Works directly on all platforms)
  try {
    const restUrl = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${FIRESTORE_PROJECT_ID}/databases/${FIRESTORE_DATABASE_ID}/documents/settings/auth?key=${FIRESTORE_API_KEY}`;
    await fetch(restUrl, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        fields: {
          hash: { stringValue: newHash },
          updated_at: { stringValue: nowIso },
          migrated: { booleanValue: true },
        },
      }),
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('REST cloud update warning:', err);
  }

  // 2. ALSO UPDATE VIA FIRESTORE SDK
  try {
    await setDoc(doc(db, 'settings', 'auth'), {
      hash: newHash,
      updated_at: nowIso,
      migrated: true,
    }, { merge: true });

    await setDoc(doc(db, 'platform_settings', 'auth'), {
      hash: newHash,
      updated_at: nowIso,
    }, { merge: true });
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('SDK cloud update warning:', err);
  }

  // 3. UPDATE SERVER DATABASE (data/auth.json)
  try {
    const res = await authFetch('/api/admin/password', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        currentPassword: curClean,
        newPassword: newClean,
      }),
    });
    const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    if (res.ok && contentType.includes('application/json')) {
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.token) {
        storeAdminSession(data.token);
      }
    }
  } catch {}

  // 4. UPDATE LOCAL CLIENT CACHE
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, newHash);
    sessionStorage.setItem(ADMIN_PASSWORD_HASH_KEY, newHash);
  } catch {}

  return {
    success: true,
    message: 'تم تحديث كلمة المرور في قاعدة البيانات السحابية (Firestore) وجميع الأجهزة بنجاح!',
  };
}

/**
 * Logout admin and invalidate session
 */
export function logoutAdmin(): void {
  try {
    authFetch('/api/admin/logout', { method: 'POST' }).catch(() => {});
  } catch {}

  try {
    localStorage.removeItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_KEY);
    localStorage.removeItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY);
    sessionStorage.removeItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_KEY);
    sessionStorage.removeItem(ADMIN_TOKEN_EXPIRY_KEY);
  } catch {}
}
