import { AppType, BankDetails, DeepLinkInfo, WalletProvider } from '../types/qr';

/**
 * Checks whether the current window is embedded within an iframe
 * (e.g., inside the AI Studio Web Preview environment).
 */
export function isRunningInIframe(): boolean {
  try {
    return window.self !== window.top;
  } catch {
    return true;
  }
}

/**
 * Encodes bank details into a compact bank URI scheme for storage
 */
export function encodeBankDetails(details: BankDetails): string {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  if (details.bankName) params.set('b', details.bankName);
  if (details.accountNumber) params.set('acc', details.accountNumber);
  if (details.accountName) params.set('name', details.accountName);
  if (details.iban) params.set('iban', details.iban);
  if (details.notes) params.set('notes', details.notes);
  return `bank://account?${params.toString()}`;
}

/**
 * Decodes a bank URI scheme back into structured BankDetails
 */
export function decodeBankDetails(uri: string): BankDetails | null {
  if (!uri || !uri.startsWith('bank://')) return null;
  try {
    const query = uri.replace('bank://account?', '').replace('bank://?', '');
    const params = new URLSearchParams(query);
    return {
      bankName: params.get('b') || 'حساب بنكي',
      accountNumber: params.get('acc') || '',
      accountName: params.get('name') || '',
      iban: params.get('iban') || '',
      notes: params.get('notes') || '',
    };
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Validates that a URL does NOT contain dangerous or malicious execution schemes
 * (e.g., javascript:, vbscript:, data:, file:, etc.)
 */
export function isSafeUrlScheme(url: string): boolean {
  if (!url) return false;
  const sanitized = url.trim().replace(/[\x00-\x1f\x7f]/g, '').toLowerCase();
  const dangerous = /^(?:javascript|data|vbscript|file|blob|about):/i;
  return !dangerous.test(sanitized);
}

/**
 * Robustly sanitizes and extracts the true URL from raw inputs,
 * handling Android app share links (e.g. android-app://com.instapay.app/https/ipn.eg/...),
 * double protocols (https://android-app:// or https://https://), and custom schemes.
 */
export function cleanAndSanitizeUrl(input: string): string {
  let s = (input || '').trim();
  if (!s) return '';

  // Immediately reject dangerous script schemes
  if (!isSafeUrlScheme(s)) {
    return '';
  }

  // If already a bank URI scheme, leave as-is
  if (s.startsWith('bank://')) {
    return s;
  }

  // 1. Check if input contains an embedded web URL (common in Android share intents)
  // e.g., android-app://com.instapay.app/https/ipn.eg/S/shenoudazaher/instapay/7CGeaO
  const embeddedHttpMatch = s.match(/(https?:\/\/[^\s]+)/i) || s.match(/(https?\/[^\s]+)/i);
  if (embeddedHttpMatch && !s.startsWith('http://android-app') && !s.startsWith('https://android-app')) {
    let extracted = embeddedHttpMatch[1];
    if (extracted.startsWith('https/') || extracted.startsWith('http/')) {
      extracted = extracted.replace(/^https?\//, 'https://');
    }
    return extracted;
  }

  // 2. Double scheme like https://android-app://
  if (/^https?:\/\/+android-app:\/\//i.test(s)) {
    s = s.replace(/^https?:\/\/+android-app:\/\//i, 'android-app://');
    const innerUrl = s.match(/https?\/[^\s]+/i) || s.match(/https?:\/\/[^\s]+/i);
    if (innerUrl) {
      let clean = innerUrl[0];
      if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
        clean = clean.replace(/^https?\//, 'https://');
      }
      return clean;
    }
    const pkg = s.replace('android-app://', '').split('/')[0];
    return `https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=${pkg}`;
  }

  // 3. Fix double http(s) like https://https:// or https://http://
  s = s.replace(/^https?:\/\/+(https?:\/\/)/i, '$1');

  // 4. Raw android-app:// scheme
  if (s.startsWith('android-app://')) {
    const innerUrl = s.match(/https?\/[^\s]+/i) || s.match(/https?:\/\/[^\s]+/i);
    if (innerUrl) {
      let clean = innerUrl[0];
      if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
        clean = clean.replace(/^https?\//, 'https://');
      }
      return clean;
    }
    const pkg = s.replace('android-app://', '').split('/')[0];
    return `https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=${pkg}`;
  }

  // 5. Standard valid non-http schemes
  if (s.startsWith('tel:') || s.startsWith('mailto:')) {
    return s;
  }

  // 6. Custom scheme like instapay://send?to=
  if (s.startsWith('instapay://send?to=')) {
    const handle = decodeURIComponent(s.replace('instapay://send?to=', ''));
    return handle.replace(/@instapay$/i, '').trim();
  }

  return s;
}

/**
 * Normalizes Eastern Arabic numerals (٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩) to standard ASCII numerals (0123456789)
 */
export function normalizeArabicNumerals(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  const arabicNumerals = ['٠', '١', '٢', '٣', '٤', '٥', '٦', '٧', '٨', '٩'];
  return input.replace(/[٠-٩]/g, (char) => {
    const idx = arabicNumerals.indexOf(char);
    return idx !== -1 ? String(idx) : char;
  });
}

/**
 * Checks if input is an external Quick Payment URL/link or deep link rather than a phone number
 */
export function isQuickPayUrl(input: string): boolean {
  const s = normalizeArabicNumerals(input || '').trim();
  if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(s)) return true;
  if (/^intent:\/\//i.test(s)) return true;
  if (/^android-app:?\/\//i.test(s) || /^android-app\/\//i.test(s)) return true;
  if (/^(vfcash|orangecash|etisalatcash|wepay|ipn):\/\//i.test(s)) return true;

  const hasDomain = s.includes('.eg') || s.includes('.com') || s.includes('.net') || s.includes('.me') || s.includes('.io') || s.startsWith('www.') || s.includes('/');
  const digitsOnly = s.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');

  if (hasDomain && (s.includes('/') || s.includes('www.') || digitsOnly.length < 8)) {
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/**
 * Robustly sanitizes and converts any Quick Pay Link (including Android app links,
 * share intents, and web links) into a safe, clickable URL that never triggers
 * Chrome's DNS_PROBE_FINISHED_NXDOMAIN or failed custom schemes.
 */
export function sanitizeQuickPayUrl(raw: string, provider?: WalletProvider): string {
  let s = (raw || '').trim();
  if (!s) return '';

  // 1. Clean double schemes and malformed android-app schemes:
  s = s.replace(/^https?:\/\/+android-app:?\/\//i, 'android-app://');
  s = s.replace(/^android-app:\/+(?!\/)/i, 'android-app://');
  s = s.replace(/^android-app\/\//i, 'android-app://');
  s = s.replace(/^https?:\/\/+(https?:\/\/)/i, '$1');

  // 2. Check if the string contains an embedded web URL (common in Android share intents)
  const embeddedMatch = s.match(/https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+/i) || s.match(/https?\/[^\s"'<>]+/i);
  if (embeddedMatch) {
    let clean = embeddedMatch[0];
    if (clean.startsWith('https/') || clean.startsWith('http/')) {
      clean = clean.replace(/^https?\//, 'https://');
    }
    return clean;
  }

  // 3. Handle Android app links: android-app://<package>/...
  if (s.startsWith('android-app://')) {
    const pkg = s.replace('android-app://', '').split('/')[0].trim().toLowerCase();
    
    // Vodafone / Ana Vodafone
    if (pkg.includes('vf') || pkg.includes('vodafone') || provider === 'vodafone') {
      return `intent://#Intent;package=com.vf.eg;scheme=anafodafone;S.browser_fallback_url=https%3A%2F%2Fweb.vodafone.com.eg%2Far%2Fvodafone-cash;end`;
    }
    // Orange Cash
    if (pkg.includes('orange') || provider === 'orange') {
      return `intent://#Intent;package=com.orange.mail.egypt;scheme=orangecash;S.browser_fallback_url=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.orange.eg%2Far%2Forange-cash;end`;
    }
    // Etisalat Cash
    if (pkg.includes('etisalat') || provider === 'etisalat') {
      return `intent://#Intent;package=com.etisalat;scheme=etisalatcash;S.browser_fallback_url=https%3A%2F%2Fwww.etisalat.eg;end`;
    }
    // WE Pay
    if (pkg.includes('we') || pkg.includes('telecom') || provider === 'we') {
      return `intent://#Intent;package=eg.telecom.we;scheme=wepay;S.browser_fallback_url=https%3A%2F%2Fte.eg;end`;
    }
    if (pkg) {
      return `https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id=${pkg}`;
    }
    return 'https://web.vodafone.com.eg/ar/vodafone-cash';
  }

  // 4. Intent links are left valid
  if (s.startsWith('intent://')) {
    return s;
  }

  // 5. Normal web links
  if (!s.startsWith('http://') && !s.startsWith('https://')) {
    s = `https://${s}`;
  }

  return s;
}

export interface DetectedWalletInfo {
  provider: 'vodafone' | 'orange' | 'etisalat' | 'we' | 'generic';
  nameAr: string;
  nameEn: string;
  companyNameAr: string;
  cleanPhone: string;
  formattedPhone: string;
  brandColor: string;
}

/**
 * Detects the Egyptian mobile wallet operator from the phone number prefix
 * 010 / ٠١٠ -> Vodafone Cash
 * 012 / ٠١٢ -> Orange Cash
 * 011 / ٠١١ -> Etisalat Cash
 * 015 / ٠١٥ -> WE Pay
 */
export function detectWalletInfo(rawPhone: string): DetectedWalletInfo {
  const normalized = normalizeArabicNumerals(rawPhone || '');
  let cleanPhone = normalized.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');

  // Strip international country codes (+20, 0020, 20)
  if (cleanPhone.startsWith('0020') && cleanPhone.length === 14) {
    cleanPhone = cleanPhone.slice(4);
  } else if (cleanPhone.startsWith('20') && cleanPhone.length === 12) {
    cleanPhone = cleanPhone.slice(2);
  }

  // Prepend missing leading zero (e.g. 1012345678 -> 01012345678)
  if (cleanPhone.length === 10 && cleanPhone.startsWith('1')) {
    cleanPhone = '0' + cleanPhone;
  }

  // Formatted display (010 1234 5678)
  let formattedPhone = cleanPhone;
  if (cleanPhone.length === 11) {
    formattedPhone = `${cleanPhone.slice(0, 3)} ${cleanPhone.slice(3, 7)} ${cleanPhone.slice(7)}`;
  }

  // 010 -> Vodafone Cash
  if (cleanPhone.startsWith('010')) {
    return {
      provider: 'vodafone',
      nameAr: 'ڨودافون كاش',
      nameEn: 'Vodafone Cash',
      companyNameAr: 'فودافون مصر - Vodafone Egypt',
      cleanPhone,
      formattedPhone,
      brandColor: '#E60000',
    };
  }

  // 012 -> Orange Cash
  if (cleanPhone.startsWith('012')) {
    return {
      provider: 'orange',
      nameAr: 'أورانج كاش',
      nameEn: 'Orange Cash',
      companyNameAr: 'أورانج مصر - Orange Egypt',
      cleanPhone,
      formattedPhone,
      brandColor: '#FF7900',
    };
  }

  // 011 -> Etisalat Cash
  if (cleanPhone.startsWith('011')) {
    return {
      provider: 'etisalat',
      nameAr: 'اتصالات كاش',
      nameEn: 'Etisalat Cash (e&)',
      companyNameAr: 'إي آند مصر (اتصالات) - e& Egypt',
      cleanPhone,
      formattedPhone,
      brandColor: '#719E19',
    };
  }

  // 015 -> WE Pay
  if (cleanPhone.startsWith('015')) {
    return {
      provider: 'we',
      nameAr: 'وي باي',
      nameEn: 'WE Pay',
      companyNameAr: 'المصرية للاتصالات - Telecom Egypt (WE)',
      cleanPhone,
      formattedPhone,
      brandColor: '#592C82',
    };
  }

  return {
    provider: 'generic',
    nameAr: 'محفظة كاش إلكترونية',
    nameEn: 'Smart Cash Wallet',
    companyNameAr: 'المحفظة الذكية',
    cleanPhone: cleanPhone || rawPhone,
    formattedPhone: cleanPhone || rawPhone,
    brandColor: '#EAB308',
  };
}

/**
 * Formats and normalizes TikTok username or links to the standard Universal / App Link format:
 * https://www.tiktok.com/@<clean_lowercase_username>
 * This guarantees the TikTok app opens directly to the profile on mobile devices,
 * or cleanly loads the profile page on the web, preventing it from just opening the app's home feed.
 */
export function formatTikTokTargetUrl(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  // 1. Remove all invisible zero-width and RTL/LTR formatting marks
  let clean = normalizeArabicNumerals(input)
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
    .trim();

  if (!clean) return '';

  // 2. TikTok short video/profile share links (vm.tiktok.com / vt.tiktok.com)
  if (/^(?:https?:\/\/)?(?:vm|vt)\.tiktok\.com\//i.test(clean)) {
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }

  // 3. Full or partial tiktok.com URL
  if (clean.includes('tiktok.com')) {
    const parts = clean.split('?');
    const pathPart = parts[0];
    const queryPart = parts[1] ? `?${parts[1]}` : '';

    const match = pathPart.match(/tiktok\.com\/(?:@)?([a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      const segment = match[1].replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase().trim();
      const reservedRoutes = ['discover', 'explore', 'upload', 'live', 'tag', 'music', 'about', 'legal', 'business', 'share', 'foryou'];
      if (!reservedRoutes.includes(segment)) {
        return `https://www.tiktok.com/@${segment}${queryPart}`;
      }
    }
    // If it's a specific link with video ID or something, just ensure https://
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }

  // 4. Raw username (e.g. foodhunteregypt, @foodhunteregypt, FoodHunterEgypt, /@Foodhunteregypt)
  let username = clean.replace(/^[/@]+/, '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0].trim();
  username = username.replace(/[^\w.-]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();

  if (username) {
    return `https://www.tiktok.com/@${username}`;
  }

  return clean;
}

/**
 * Normalizes Instagram usernames/URLs to https://www.instagram.com/<clean_lowercase_username>/
 */
export function formatInstagramTargetUrl(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  let clean = normalizeArabicNumerals(input)
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
    .trim();

  if (clean.includes('instagram.com/')) {
    const match = clean.match(/instagram\.com\/([a-zA-Z0-9._]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      const user = match[1].replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase().trim();
      return `https://www.instagram.com/${user}/`;
    }
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }
  let username = clean.replace(/^[/@]+/, '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0].trim();
  username = username.replace(/[^\w.]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (username) {
    return `https://www.instagram.com/${username}/`;
  }
  return clean;
}

/**
 * Normalizes Telegram usernames/URLs to https://t.me/<clean_lowercase_username>
 */
export function formatTelegramTargetUrl(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  let clean = input
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
    .trim();

  if (clean.includes('t.me/') || clean.includes('telegram.me/')) {
    const match = clean.match(/(?:t\.me|telegram\.me)\/([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return `https://t.me/${match[1].replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase().trim()}`;
    }
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }
  let username = clean.replace(/^[/@]+/, '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0].trim();
  username = username.replace(/[^\w]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (username) {
    return `https://t.me/${username}`;
  }
  return clean;
}

/**
 * Normalizes Snapchat usernames/URLs to https://www.snapchat.com/add/<clean_lowercase_username>
 */
export function formatSnapchatTargetUrl(input: string): string {
  if (!input) return '';
  let clean = input
    .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\uFEFF\u200E\u200F\u202A-\u202E]/g, '')
    .trim()
    .replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '')
    .trim();

  if (clean.includes('snapchat.com/add/')) {
    const match = clean.match(/snapchat\.com\/add\/([a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+)/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return `https://www.snapchat.com/add/${match[1].replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase().trim()}`;
    }
    if (!clean.startsWith('http://') && !clean.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `https://${clean}`;
    }
    return clean;
  }
  let username = clean.replace(/^[/@]+/, '').split('?')[0].split('/')[0].trim();
  username = username.replace(/[^\w.-]/g, '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (username) {
    return `https://www.snapchat.com/add/${username}`;
  }
  return clean;
}

/**
 * Analyzes user input and produces clean, universal URLs, deep links,
 * or bank details that work flawlessly across mobile and desktop.
 */
export function processSmartLink(rawInput: string, preferredType?: AppType): DeepLinkInfo {
  const sanitized = cleanAndSanitizeUrl(rawInput);
  const trimmed = sanitized.trim();

  // Handle Bank Type
  if (preferredType === 'bank' || trimmed.startsWith('bank://')) {
    const bankDetails = decodeBankDetails(trimmed) || {
      bankName: 'حساب بنكي',
      accountNumber: trimmed.replace(/^https?:\/\//, ''),
    };

    return {
      appType: 'bank',
      appTitleAr: bankDetails.bankName || 'حساب بنكي',
      schemeUrl: trimmed.startsWith('bank://') ? trimmed : encodeBankDetails(bankDetails),
      fallbackUrl: '#',
      displayUrl: bankDetails.accountNumber ? `${bankDetails.bankName}: ${bankDetails.accountNumber}` : 'بيانات الحساب البنكي',
      isBankCard: true,
      bankDetails,
    };
  }

  // Handle Smart Wallet (Vodafone Cash, Etisalat, Orange, WE Pay)
  if (preferredType === 'wallet' || trimmed.startsWith('tel:*9*7*')) {
    const normalized = normalizeArabicNumerals(trimmed);
    let cleanTrimmed = normalized;
    if (cleanTrimmed.startsWith('tel:*9*7*')) {
      cleanTrimmed = cleanTrimmed.replace('tel:*9*7*', '').replace('#', '').replace('%23', '');
    }

    // 1. Check if input is a Web URL or Quick Payment Link (e.g. https://... or vf.eg or web.vodafone.com.eg/...)
    if (isQuickPayUrl(cleanTrimmed)) {
      // Detect operator from URL domain / keywords
      let detectedProv: WalletProvider = 'vodafone';
      const lower = cleanTrimmed.toLowerCase();
      if (lower.includes('vodafone') || lower.includes('voda') || lower.includes('vf.eg') || lower.includes('vfcash')) detectedProv = 'vodafone';
      else if (lower.includes('orange')) detectedProv = 'orange';
      else if (lower.includes('etisalat') || lower.includes('e&')) detectedProv = 'etisalat';
      else if (lower.includes('we') || lower.includes('te.eg') || lower.includes('telecom')) detectedProv = 'we';

      const fullUrl = sanitizeQuickPayUrl(cleanTrimmed, detectedProv);

      const provMeta: Record<WalletProvider, { nameAr: string; nameEn: string; companyNameAr: string }> = {
        vodafone: { nameAr: 'ڨودافون كاش', nameEn: 'Vodafone Cash', companyNameAr: 'فودافون مصر - Vodafone Egypt' },
        orange: { nameAr: 'أورانج كاش', nameEn: 'Orange Cash', companyNameAr: 'أورانج مصر - Orange Egypt' },
        etisalat: { nameAr: 'اتصالات كاش', nameEn: 'Etisalat Cash (e&)', companyNameAr: 'إي آند مصر (اتصالات) - e& Egypt' },
        we: { nameAr: 'وي باي', nameEn: 'WE Pay', companyNameAr: 'المصرية للاتصالات - Telecom Egypt (WE)' },
        generic: { nameAr: 'محفظة كاش', nameEn: 'Cash Wallet', companyNameAr: 'محفظة كاش مصرية' },
      };
      const activeMeta = provMeta[detectedProv] || provMeta.vodafone;

      return {
        appType: 'wallet',
        appTitleAr: `${activeMeta.nameAr} - ${activeMeta.nameEn}`,
        schemeUrl: fullUrl,
        fallbackUrl: fullUrl,
        displayUrl: fullUrl.replace(/^https?:\/\//, ''),
        isWalletCard: true, // Show dedicated luxury card with logo, company name, and Quick Pay button!
        walletProvider: detectedProv,
        walletProviderNameAr: activeMeta.nameAr,
        walletProviderNameEn: activeMeta.nameEn,
        walletCompanyNameAr: activeMeta.companyNameAr,
        walletPhone: fullUrl,
      };
    }

    // 2. Otherwise, input is a wallet phone number (e.g. 010..., 012..., 011..., 015..., ٠١٠...)
    const detected = detectWalletInfo(cleanTrimmed);

    return {
      appType: 'wallet',
      appTitleAr: `${detected.nameAr} - ${detected.nameEn}`,
      schemeUrl: '', // Dedicated copy screen only - NO intent or USSD
      fallbackUrl: '',
      displayUrl: detected.cleanPhone,
      isWalletCard: true, // Show dedicated luxury card with logo, company name, phone, and copy button ONLY!
      walletProvider: detected.provider,
      walletProviderNameAr: detected.nameAr,
      walletProviderNameEn: detected.nameEn,
      walletCompanyNameAr: detected.companyNameAr,
      walletPhone: detected.cleanPhone,
      walletFormattedPhone: detected.formattedPhone,
    };
  }

  if (!trimmed) {
    return {
      appType: preferredType || 'website',
      appTitleAr: 'رابط ويب',
      schemeUrl: '',
      fallbackUrl: '',
      displayUrl: '',
    };
  }

  // 1. WhatsApp
  const isWhatsAppInput =
    preferredType === 'whatsapp' ||
    trimmed.includes('wa.me') ||
    trimmed.includes('whatsapp.com') ||
    trimmed.startsWith('whatsapp://') ||
    (!preferredType && /^(?:\+?20|0)?1[0125]\d{8}$/.test(rawInput.trim()));

  if (isWhatsAppInput) {
    let phoneDigits = '';
    let customText = '';

    if (trimmed.includes('wa.me/')) {
      const parts = trimmed.split('wa.me/')[1]?.split('?') || [];
      phoneDigits = parts[0]?.replace(/[^\d]/g, '') || '';
      if (parts[1]?.includes('text=')) {
        customText = new URLSearchParams(parts[1]).get('text') || '';
      }
    } else if (trimmed.includes('phone=')) {
      try {
        const urlObj = new URL(trimmed.startsWith('http') ? trimmed : `https://${trimmed}`);
        phoneDigits = urlObj.searchParams.get('phone')?.replace(/[^\d]/g, '') || '';
        customText = urlObj.searchParams.get('text') || '';
      } catch {
        phoneDigits = trimmed.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
      }
    } else {
      phoneDigits = rawInput.trim().replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
    }

    // Auto format Egyptian numbers: 010... -> 2010...
    if (phoneDigits.startsWith('01') && phoneDigits.length === 11) {
      phoneDigits = '2' + phoneDigits;
    }

    const textQueryParam = customText ? `?text=${encodeURIComponent(customText)}` : '';
    const universalUrl = `https://wa.me/${phoneDigits}${textQueryParam}`;

    return {
      appType: 'whatsapp',
      appTitleAr: 'واتساب WhatsApp',
      schemeUrl: universalUrl,
      fallbackUrl: universalUrl,
      displayUrl: `wa.me/${phoneDigits}`,
    };
  }

  // 2. Phone Call
  const isPhone =
    preferredType === 'phone' ||
    trimmed.startsWith('tel:') ||
    (!preferredType && /^(\+|00)?\d{7,15}$/.test(rawInput.trim().replace(/[\s-]/g, '')));

  if (isPhone) {
    const cleanNumber = rawInput.trim().replace(/[^\d+]/g, '');
    const telUrl = `tel:${cleanNumber}`;
    return {
      appType: 'phone',
      appTitleAr: 'اتصال هاتفي',
      schemeUrl: telUrl,
      fallbackUrl: telUrl,
      displayUrl: cleanNumber,
    };
  }

  // 3. InstaPay Egypt (Quick Pay Link ONLY - Direct instant redirect)
  const isInstaPay =
    preferredType === 'instapay' ||
    trimmed.includes('ipn.eg') ||
    trimmed.includes('instapay');

  if (isInstaPay) {
    let paymentUrl = trimmed;
    if (paymentUrl.startsWith('ipn.eg/')) {
      paymentUrl = `https://${paymentUrl}`;
    } else if (!paymentUrl.startsWith('http://') && !paymentUrl.startsWith('https://')) {
      if (paymentUrl.includes('ipn.eg')) {
        paymentUrl = `https://${paymentUrl}`;
      } else {
        paymentUrl = `https://ipn.eg/${paymentUrl.replace(/^@/, '').replace(/@instapay$/i, '')}`;
      }
    }

    return {
      appType: 'instapay',
      appTitleAr: 'إنستاباي InstaPay',
      schemeUrl: paymentUrl,
      fallbackUrl: paymentUrl,
      displayUrl: paymentUrl.replace(/^https?:\/\//, ''),
      isInstaPayCard: false, // Direct instant redirect to InstaPay quick payment link!
    };
  }



  // 4. Instagram
  const isInstagram =
    preferredType === 'instagram' ||
    trimmed.includes('instagram.com') ||
    (preferredType === undefined && rawInput.trim().startsWith('@') && !trimmed.includes('instapay'));

  if (isInstagram) {
    const universalUrl = formatInstagramTargetUrl(trimmed || rawInput);
    const match = universalUrl.match(/instagram\.com\/([a-zA-Z0-9._]+)/);
    const username = match ? match[1] : trimmed.replace(/^@/, '');

    return {
      appType: 'instagram',
      appTitleAr: 'إنستجرام Instagram',
      schemeUrl: universalUrl,
      fallbackUrl: universalUrl,
      displayUrl: `@${username}`,
    };
  }

  // 5. Facebook
  const isFacebook =
    preferredType === 'facebook' ||
    trimmed.includes('facebook.com') ||
    trimmed.includes('fb.me') ||
    trimmed.includes('fb.watch');

  if (isFacebook) {
    let cleanUrl = trimmed;
    if (!cleanUrl.startsWith('http://') && !cleanUrl.startsWith('https://')) {
      cleanUrl = `https://www.facebook.com/${cleanUrl.replace(/^www\./, '')}`;
    }
    return {
      appType: 'facebook',
      appTitleAr: 'فيسبوك Facebook',
      schemeUrl: cleanUrl,
      fallbackUrl: cleanUrl,
      displayUrl: cleanUrl.replace(/^https?:\/\/(www\.)?facebook\.com\/?/, 'fb.com/'),
    };
  }

  // 6. Telegram
  const isTelegram =
    preferredType === 'telegram' ||
    trimmed.includes('t.me') ||
    trimmed.includes('telegram.me');

  if (isTelegram) {
    const universalUrl = formatTelegramTargetUrl(trimmed || rawInput);
    const match = universalUrl.match(/(?:t\.me|telegram\.me)\/([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)/);
    const handle = match ? match[1] : trimmed.replace(/^@/, '');

    return {
      appType: 'telegram',
      appTitleAr: 'تيليجرام Telegram',
      schemeUrl: universalUrl,
      fallbackUrl: universalUrl,
      displayUrl: `t.me/${handle}`,
    };
  }

  // 7. TikTok (Direct App & Profile Universal Link)
  const isTikTok =
    preferredType === 'tiktok' ||
    trimmed.includes('tiktok.com');

  if (isTikTok) {
    const universalUrl = formatTikTokTargetUrl(trimmed || rawInput);
    const match = universalUrl.match(/tiktok\.com\/@([a-zA-Z0-9_.-]+)/i);
    const displayUser = match ? match[1] : trimmed.replace(/^@/, '');

    return {
      appType: 'tiktok',
      appTitleAr: 'تيك توك TikTok',
      schemeUrl: universalUrl,
      fallbackUrl: universalUrl,
      displayUrl: `tiktok.com/@${displayUser}`,
    };
  }

  // 8. Snapchat
  const isSnapchat =
    preferredType === 'snapchat' ||
    trimmed.includes('snapchat.com');

  if (isSnapchat) {
    let user = trimmed;
    if (trimmed.includes('snapchat.com/add/')) {
      user = trimmed.split('snapchat.com/add/')[1]?.split('?')[0] || '';
    } else {
      user = rawInput.trim().replace(/^@/, '');
    }
    const universalUrl = `https://www.snapchat.com/add/${user}`;
    return {
      appType: 'snapchat',
      appTitleAr: 'سناب شات Snapchat',
      schemeUrl: universalUrl,
      fallbackUrl: universalUrl,
      displayUrl: `snapchat.com/add/${user}`,
    };
  }

  // 9. Standard Web URL / Fallback
  let webUrl = trimmed;
  if (!webUrl.startsWith('http://') && !webUrl.startsWith('https://')) {
    webUrl = `https://${webUrl}`;
  }

  return {
    appType: preferredType || 'website',
    appTitleAr: 'رابط ويب',
    schemeUrl: webUrl,
    fallbackUrl: webUrl,
    displayUrl: webUrl.replace(/^https?:\/\//, ''),
  };
}

/**
 * Triggers instant direct opening of the target URL or app.
 * IMPORTANT: Checks if running inside an iframe (like AI Studio preview),
 * avoiding the X-Frame-Options: DENY iframe crash.
 */
export function triggerSmartRedirect(linkInfo: DeepLinkInfo): void {
  const target = linkInfo.schemeUrl || linkInfo.fallbackUrl;
  if (!target || target === '#' || target.startsWith('intent:') || !isSafeUrlScheme(target)) return;

  const inIframe = isRunningInIframe();

  if (inIframe) {
    // If inside an iframe (AI Studio preview):
    // Trying window.location.replace() to an external domain (e.g. wa.me, ipn.eg)
    // will be blocked by the browser with X-Frame-Options: DENY.
    // Instead, open in top window or new window:
    try {
      if (window.top) {
        window.top.location.href = target;
        return;
      }
    } catch {
      // Cross-origin top access blocked, open in new tab
    }
    window.open(target, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
  } else {
    // On real mobile device or standalone browser tab:
    try {
      window.location.replace(target);
    } catch {
      window.location.href = target;
    }
  }
}

import QRCode from 'qrcode';
import jsQR from 'jsqr';
import { doc, getDoc, getDocFromServer, setDoc, updateDoc, deleteDoc, getDocs, collection, writeBatch, increment, onSnapshot } from 'firebase/firestore';
import { db } from './firebase';
import { AppType, QrCodeRecord, PlatformSettings, WalletItem } from '../types/qr';
import { processSmartLink } from './deepLinks';
import { authFetch } from './auth';
import firebaseConfig from '../../firebase-applet-config.json';

const LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY = 'smart_qr_codes_local_cache';
const SETTINGS_CACHE_KEY = 'smart_qr_platform_settings';

// Helper for offline caching with defensive sanitization
function getLocalCache(): Record<string, QrCodeRecord> {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return {};
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    const clean: Record<string, QrCodeRecord> = {};
    if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') {
      Object.keys(parsed).forEach((k) => {
        if (k && k !== 'undefined' && k !== 'null' && parsed[k]) {
          const recId = parsed[k].id || k;
          if (recId && recId !== 'undefined' && recId !== 'null') {
            clean[recId] = { ...parsed[k], id: recId };
          }
        }
      });
    }
    return clean;
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

function saveLocalCache(cache: Record<string, QrCodeRecord>) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(LOCAL_STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(cache));
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('LocalStorage save warning:', e);
  }
}

/**
 * Recursively strips undefined values from objects/arrays so Firestore never rejects a write.
 */
function cleanForFirestore<T>(val: T): T {
  if (Array.isArray(val)) {
    return val
      .filter((item) => item !== undefined)
      .map((item) => cleanForFirestore(item)) as unknown as T;
  }
  if (val !== null && typeof val === 'object') {
    const out: Record<string, any> = {};
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(val as Record<string, any>)) {
      if (v !== undefined) {
        out[k] = cleanForFirestore(v);
      }
    }
    return out as T;
  }
  return val;
}

function getFirestoreRestConfig() {
  const pId = firebaseConfig?.projectId || 'clean-plateau-dv4kj';
  const dbId = firebaseConfig?.firestoreDatabaseId || 'ai-studio-41f04a06-1999-41b9-b7be-e25b21dc1fd0';
  const apiKey = firebaseConfig?.apiKey || 'AIzaSyAjyCP484AEQ52-9CDgp8TDkAw5woA1TFc';
  return { pId, dbId, apiKey };
}

function parseFirestoreValue(val: any): any {
  if (!val || typeof val !== 'object') return undefined;
  if ('stringValue' in val) return val.stringValue;
  if ('booleanValue' in val) return val.booleanValue;
  if ('integerValue' in val) return parseInt(val.integerValue, 10);
  if ('doubleValue' in val) return Number(val.doubleValue);
  if ('nullValue' in val) return null;
  if ('timestampValue' in val) return val.timestampValue;
  if ('arrayValue' in val) {
    const vals = val.arrayValue?.values || [];
    return vals.map(parseFirestoreValue).filter((v: any) => v !== undefined);
  }
  if ('mapValue' in val) {
    const fields = val.mapValue?.fields || {};
    const obj: Record<string, any> = {};
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(fields)) {
      const parsed = parseFirestoreValue(v);
      if (parsed !== undefined) obj[k] = parsed;
    }
    return obj;
  }
  return undefined;
}

function firestoreRestDocToQrRecord(id: string, docJson: any): QrCodeRecord | null {
  if (!docJson || !docJson.fields) return null;
  const parsed: Record<string, any> = {};
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(docJson.fields)) {
    const pv = parseFirestoreValue(v);
    if (pv !== undefined) parsed[k] = pv;
  }
  return {
    created_at: parsed.created_at || new Date().toISOString(),
    total_scans: typeof parsed.total_scans === 'number' ? parsed.total_scans : 0,
    is_active: Boolean(parsed.is_active),
    target_url: parsed.target_url ?? null,
    ...parsed,
    id: parsed.id || id,
  } as QrCodeRecord;
}

function toFirestoreValue(val: any): any {
  if (val === null) return { nullValue: null };
  if (typeof val === 'boolean') return { booleanValue: val };
  if (typeof val === 'number') {
    return Number.isInteger(val) ? { integerValue: String(val) } : { doubleValue: val };
  }
  if (typeof val === 'string') return { stringValue: val };
  if (Array.isArray(val)) {
    return {
      arrayValue: {
        values: val.filter((v) => v !== undefined).map(toFirestoreValue),
      },
    };
  }
  if (typeof val === 'object') {
    const fields: Record<string, any> = {};
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(val)) {
      if (v !== undefined) {
        fields[k] = toFirestoreValue(v);
      }
    }
    return { mapValue: { fields } };
  }
  return { nullValue: null };
}

async function fetchQrFromFirestoreRest(cleanId: string): Promise<QrCodeRecord | null> {
  try {
    const { pId, dbId, apiKey } = getFirestoreRestConfig();
    const url = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${pId}/databases/${dbId}/documents/qr_codes/${encodeURIComponent(cleanId)}?key=${apiKey}&t=${Date.now()}`;
    const res = await fetch(url, { cache: 'no-store' });
    if (!res.ok) return null;
    const json = await res.json();
    return firestoreRestDocToQrRecord(cleanId, json);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

async function writeQrToFirestoreRest(cleanId: string, record: QrCodeRecord): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const { pId, dbId, apiKey } = getFirestoreRestConfig();
    const url = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${pId}/databases/${dbId}/documents/qr_codes/${encodeURIComponent(cleanId)}?key=${apiKey}`;
    const clean = cleanForFirestore(record);
    const fields: Record<string, any> = {};
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(clean)) {
      if (v !== undefined) {
        fields[k] = toFirestoreValue(v);
      }
    }
    const res = await fetch(url, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ fields }),
      cache: 'no-store',
    });
    return res.ok;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Generates bulk unique QR codes with user-specified count and subscription duration
 */
export async function generateBulkQrCodes(count: number, subscriptionMonths = 1): Promise<QrCodeRecord[]> {
  const safeCount = Math.max(1, count || 1);
  const now = new Date();
  const expiresAt = subscriptionMonths > 0 
    ? new Date(now.getTime() + subscriptionMonths * 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString() 
    : null;

  let generatedRecords: QrCodeRecord[] = [];

  // Try Server API first if running on Node/Express full stack
  try {
    const res = await authFetch('/api/qr/bulk', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ count: safeCount, subscription_months: subscriptionMonths, expires_at: expiresAt }),
    });

    const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    if (res.ok && contentType.includes('application/json')) {
      const json = await res.json();
      if (json.success && Array.isArray(json.data) && json.data.length > 0) {
        generatedRecords = json.data;
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Server bulk generate failed, generating client-side:', err);
  }

  // Fallback client-side generation if server temporarily unreachable or running on static host
  if (generatedRecords.length === 0) {
    const { nanoid } = await import('nanoid');
    for (let i = 0; i < safeCount; i++) {
      const id = nanoid(10).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, 'x');
      const record: QrCodeRecord = {
        id,
        is_active: false,
        target_url: null,
        created_at: now.toISOString(),
        total_scans: 0,
        subscription_months: subscriptionMonths,
        expires_at: expiresAt,
      };
      generatedRecords.push(record);
    }
  }

  // 1. ALWAYS Save to Local Cache immediately
  const cache = getLocalCache();
  generatedRecords.forEach((r) => {
    cache[r.id] = r;
  });
  saveLocalCache(cache);

  // 2. CRITICAL: ALWAYS Save EVERY generated code to Cloud Firestore!
  // This guarantees persistence across reloads, live polling, and all phones!
  try {
    const batch = writeBatch(db);
    for (const item of generatedRecords) {
      batch.set(doc(db, 'qr_codes', item.id), item);
    }
    await batch.commit();
  } catch (fsErr) {
    console.error('Firestore batch save error:', fsErr);
  }

  return generatedRecords;
}

/**
 * Fetch a single QR Code by ID (searches Firestore and Server API in parallel, with local cache fallback)
 */
export async function getQrCode(id: string): Promise<QrCodeRecord | null> {
  const cleanId = id.trim();
  if (!cleanId) return null;

  // 0. Pre-injected server record for instantaneous 0ms resolution
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && (window as any).__INITIAL_QR_RECORD__) {
    const initialRec = (window as any).__INITIAL_QR_RECORD__;
    if (initialRec && initialRec.id === cleanId) {
      const cache = getLocalCache();
      cache[cleanId] = initialRec;
      saveLocalCache(cache);
      return initialRec;
    }
  }

  // 1. Fetch from Firestore Server, Firestore REST API, and Express Server API concurrently
  const docRef = doc(db, 'qr_codes', cleanId);
  const firestoreSdkFetch = getDocFromServer(docRef)
    .then((snap) => (snap.exists() ? (snap.data() as QrCodeRecord) : null))
    .catch(() =>
      getDoc(docRef)
        .then((snap) => (snap.exists() ? (snap.data() as QrCodeRecord) : null))
        .catch(() => null)
    );

  const firestoreRestFetch = fetchQrFromFirestoreRest(cleanId);

  const serverFetch = fetch(`/api/qr/${encodeURIComponent(cleanId)}?t=${Date.now()}`, { cache: 'no-store' })
    .then(async (res) => {
      const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
      if (res.ok && contentType.includes('application/json')) {
        const json = await res.json();
        return (json.success && json.data) ? (json.data as QrCodeRecord) : null;
      }
      return null;
    })
    .catch(() => null);

  try {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled([firestoreRestFetch, firestoreSdkFetch, serverFetch]);
    const candidates = results
      .filter((r): r is PromiseFulfilledResult<QrCodeRecord | null> => r.status === 'fulfilled')
      .map((r) => r.value)
      .filter((r): r is QrCodeRecord => Boolean(r && r.id));

    if (candidates.length > 0) {
      // Pick the candidate with the newest updated_at/created_at timestamp (REST listed first so ties favor REST)
      const bestRecord = candidates.reduce((best, curr) => {
        const bestTime = new Date(best.updated_at || best.created_at || 0).getTime();
        const currTime = new Date(curr.updated_at || curr.created_at || 0).getTime();
        return currTime > bestTime ? curr : best;
      });

      const cache = getLocalCache();
      cache[cleanId] = bestRecord;
      saveLocalCache(cache);
      return bestRecord;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Concurrent QR fetch warning:', err);
  }

  // 2. Fallback to local cache only if all network sources temporarily unreachable
  const cache = getLocalCache();
  if (cache[cleanId]) {
    return cache[cleanId];
  }

  // 3. Clean inactive record if completely new
  const newInactiveRecord: QrCodeRecord = {
    id: cleanId,
    is_active: false,
    target_url: null,
    created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    total_scans: 0,
  };
  return newInactiveRecord;
}

/**
 * Strict lookup for an existing QR card in the database or cache.
 * Returns null if the card was never created/registered.
 */
export async function lookupExistingQrCode(id: string): Promise<QrCodeRecord | null> {
  const cleanId = id.trim();
  if (!cleanId) return null;

  // 1. Check local cache
  const cache = getLocalCache();
  if (cache[cleanId] && cache[cleanId].id) {
    return cache[cleanId];
  }

  // 2. Query Firestore and Server concurrently
  const firestoreFetch = getDoc(doc(db, 'qr_codes', cleanId))
    .then((snap) => (snap.exists() ? (snap.data() as QrCodeRecord) : null))
    .catch(() => null);

  const serverFetch = fetch(`/api/qr/${encodeURIComponent(cleanId)}`)
    .then(async (res) => {
      if (res.ok) {
        const json = await res.json();
        return (json.success && json.data) ? (json.data as QrCodeRecord) : null;
      }
      return null;
    })
    .catch(() => null);

  try {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled([firestoreFetch, serverFetch]);
    const fsRecord = results[0].status === 'fulfilled' ? results[0].value : null;
    const srvRecord = results[1].status === 'fulfilled' ? results[1].value : null;

    const found = fsRecord || srvRecord;
    if (found) {
      cache[cleanId] = found;
      saveLocalCache(cache);
      return found;
    }
  } catch {}

  return null;
}

/**
 * Fetch all QR codes for Admin view (Firestore + API + Local cache)
 */
export async function fetchAllQrCodes(): Promise<QrCodeRecord[]> {
  const mergedMap: Record<string, QrCodeRecord> = {};

  // 1. Primary: Direct Cloud Firestore fetch (Source of Truth)
  try {
    const snap = await getDocs(collection(db, 'qr_codes'));
    if (!snap.empty) {
      snap.forEach((d) => {
        const data = d.data();
        const id = data?.id || d.id;
        if (id && id !== 'undefined' && id !== 'null') {
          const item: QrCodeRecord = {
            created_at: data.created_at || new Date().toISOString(),
            total_scans: data.total_scans || 0,
            is_active: Boolean(data.is_active),
            target_url: data.target_url || null,
            ...data,
            id,
          };
          mergedMap[id] = item;
        }
      });
      // Authoritatively update cache to exclude any deleted codes
      saveLocalCache(mergedMap);
      return Object.values(mergedMap)
        .filter((r): r is QrCodeRecord => Boolean(r && r.id && r.id !== 'undefined' && r.id !== 'null'))
        .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.created_at || 0).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at || 0).getTime());
    }
  } catch (fsErr) {
    console.warn('Firestore fetchAll warning:', fsErr);
  }

  // 2. Try Server API if available and returns JSON
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/qr');
    const contentType = res.headers.get('content-type') || '';
    if (res.ok && contentType.includes('application/json')) {
      const json = await res.json();
      if (json.success && Array.isArray(json.data)) {
        json.data.forEach((r: QrCodeRecord) => {
          if (!mergedMap[r.id] || new Date(r.updated_at || r.created_at).getTime() >= new Date(mergedMap[r.id].updated_at || mergedMap[r.id].created_at).getTime()) {
            mergedMap[r.id] = r;
          }
        });
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // API unreachable
  }

  saveLocalCache(mergedMap);

  return Object.values(mergedMap)
    .filter((r): r is QrCodeRecord => Boolean(r && r.id && r.id !== 'undefined' && r.id !== 'null'))
    .sort(
      (a, b) => new Date(b.created_at || 0).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at || 0).getTime()
    );
}

/**
 * Customer first-time scan activation.
 * Saves directly to Firestore, local cache, and backend API.
 */
export async function activateQrCode(
  id: string,
  rawInput: string,
  preferredType?: AppType,
  wallets?: WalletItem[]
): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string; record?: QrCodeRecord }> {
  const cleanId = id.trim();
  const isWallet = preferredType === 'wallet' || (Array.isArray(wallets) && wallets.length > 0);
  const effectiveAppType = isWallet ? 'wallet' : (preferredType || 'website');
  const effectiveRawInput = (isWallet && Array.isArray(wallets) && wallets.length > 0)
    ? wallets[0].phone
    : rawInput;
  const smartLink = processSmartLink(effectiveRawInput, effectiveAppType);
  const now = new Date();
  const existing = await getQrCode(cleanId);

  // Preserve existing expires_at if set, otherwise default to 1 month from activation
  const defaultExpiry = new Date(now.getTime() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();
  const expires_at = (existing && existing.expires_at !== undefined) 
    ? existing.expires_at 
    : defaultExpiry;
  const subscription_months = (existing && existing.subscription_months !== undefined)
    ? existing.subscription_months
    : 1;

  const effectiveTargetUrl = (smartLink.isWalletCard && smartLink.walletPhone)
    ? smartLink.walletPhone
    : (smartLink.schemeUrl || smartLink.fallbackUrl || effectiveRawInput.trim());

  const cleanWallets = (isWallet && Array.isArray(wallets) && wallets.length > 0)
    ? wallets.map((w) => ({
        phone: (w.phone || '').trim(),
        provider: w.provider || 'vodafone',
        ownerName: (w.ownerName || '').trim(),
      }))
    : [];

  const updatedRecord: QrCodeRecord = cleanForFirestore({
    id: cleanId,
    is_active: true,
    target_url: effectiveTargetUrl,
    app_type: effectiveAppType,
    updated_at: now.toISOString(),
    created_at: existing?.created_at || now.toISOString(),
    total_scans: (existing && existing.is_active && existing.total_scans !== undefined) 
      ? existing.total_scans 
      : 0,
    expires_at,
    subscription_months,
    wallets: cleanWallets,
  });

  // 1. Write to Cloud Firestore via SDK (full replace) + REST API concurrently
  const sdkWrite = setDoc(doc(db, 'qr_codes', cleanId), updatedRecord).catch((fsErr) => {
    console.warn('Firestore direct activate write warning:', fsErr);
  });
  const restWrite = writeQrToFirestoreRest(cleanId, updatedRecord);

  // 2. Sync to Server API
  const serverWrite = fetch(`/api/qr/${encodeURIComponent(cleanId)}/activate`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      target_url: effectiveTargetUrl,
      app_type: effectiveAppType,
      expires_at,
      subscription_months,
      wallets: cleanWallets,
    }),
  }).catch(() => {});

  await Promise.allSettled([sdkWrite, restWrite, serverWrite]);

  // 3. Update local cache
  const cache = getLocalCache();
  cache[cleanId] = updatedRecord;
  saveLocalCache(cache);

  return { success: true, record: updatedRecord };
}

/**
 * Admin Quick Edit: Modify Target URL, label, and subscription expiry.
 * ID is strictly read-only and immutable.
 * Saves simultaneously to Firestore (SDK + REST) AND Server API with full field overwrite so no stale link ever remains.
 */
export async function adminUpdateQrCode(
  id: string,
  rawTargetUrl: string,
  preferredType?: AppType,
  label?: string,
  expiresAt?: string | null,
  subscriptionMonths?: number,
  wallets?: WalletItem[]
): Promise<{ success: boolean; record?: QrCodeRecord }> {
  const cleanId = id.trim();
  const isWallet = preferredType === 'wallet' || (Array.isArray(wallets) && wallets.length > 0);
  const effectiveAppType: AppType = isWallet ? 'wallet' : (preferredType || 'website');

  const sourceInput = (isWallet && Array.isArray(wallets) && wallets.length > 0)
    ? wallets[0].phone
    : rawTargetUrl;

  const smartLink = processSmartLink(sourceInput, effectiveAppType);
  const effectiveTargetUrl = (smartLink.isWalletCard && smartLink.walletPhone)
    ? smartLink.walletPhone
    : (smartLink.schemeUrl || smartLink.fallbackUrl || sourceInput.trim());

  const current: QrCodeRecord = (await getQrCode(cleanId)) || {
    id: cleanId,
    created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    total_scans: 0,
    is_active: true,
    target_url: null,
  };

  const resolvedWallets: WalletItem[] = isWallet
    ? (wallets !== undefined
        ? wallets.map((w) => ({
            phone: (w.phone || '').trim(),
            provider: w.provider || 'vodafone',
            ownerName: (w.ownerName || '').trim(),
          }))
        : (current.wallets || []))
    : [];

  const updatedRecord: QrCodeRecord = cleanForFirestore({
    ...current,
    id: cleanId,
    target_url: effectiveTargetUrl,
    app_type: effectiveAppType,
    is_active: true,
    label: label !== undefined ? label : (current.label ?? ''),
    expires_at: expiresAt !== undefined ? expiresAt : (current.expires_at ?? null),
    subscription_months: subscriptionMonths !== undefined
      ? subscriptionMonths
      : (current.subscription_months ?? 1),
    updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    wallets: resolvedWallets,
  });

  // 1. ALWAYS write full sanitized record to Cloud Firestore (SDK setDoc overwrite + REST PATCH)
  const sdkWrite = setDoc(doc(db, 'qr_codes', cleanId), updatedRecord).catch((fsErr) => {
    console.warn('Firestore admin update warning:', fsErr);
  });
  const restWrite = writeQrToFirestoreRest(cleanId, updatedRecord);

  // 2. ALWAYS sync to Server API if running full-stack Node/Express
  const serverWrite = authFetch(`/api/qr/${encodeURIComponent(cleanId)}`, {
    method: 'PUT',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({
      target_url: effectiveTargetUrl,
      app_type: effectiveAppType,
      label: updatedRecord.label,
      expires_at: updatedRecord.expires_at,
      subscription_months: updatedRecord.subscription_months,
      wallets: resolvedWallets,
    }),
  }).catch((err) => {
    console.warn('Server admin update sync failed:', err);
  });

  await Promise.allSettled([sdkWrite, restWrite, serverWrite]);

  // 3. Update local cache
  const cache = getLocalCache();
  cache[cleanId] = updatedRecord;
  saveLocalCache(cache);

  return { success: true, record: updatedRecord };
}

/**
 * Quick Subscription Renewal: Extends subscription by N months (+30 days per month).
 * If card is still active, adds to existing expires_at. If expired, adds from now.
 */
export async function renewQrSubscription(
  id: string,
  monthsToAdd = 1
): Promise<{ success: boolean; record?: QrCodeRecord }> {
  const current = await getQrCode(id);
  if (!current) return { success: false };

  const nowMs = Date.now();
  let baseMs = nowMs;
  if (current.expires_at) {
    const existingMs = new Date(current.expires_at).getTime();
    if (existingMs > nowMs) {
      baseMs = existingMs;
    }
  }

  const addedDays = monthsToAdd * 30;
  const newExpiresAt = new Date(baseMs + addedDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString();

  const updatedRecord: QrCodeRecord = {
    ...current,
    expires_at: newExpiresAt,
    subscription_months: (current.subscription_months || 0) + monthsToAdd,
    updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  };

  try {
    await setDoc(doc(db, 'qr_codes', id), {
      expires_at: newExpiresAt,
      subscription_months: updatedRecord.subscription_months,
      updated_at: updatedRecord.updated_at,
    }, { merge: true });
  } catch (fsErr) {
    console.warn('Firestore renew write warning:', fsErr);
  }

  try {
    fetch(`/api/qr/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        expires_at: newExpiresAt,
        subscription_months: updatedRecord.subscription_months,
      }),
    }).catch(() => {});
  } catch {}

  const cache = getLocalCache();
  cache[id] = updatedRecord;
  saveLocalCache(cache);

  return { success: true, record: updatedRecord };
}

/**
 * Set custom expiration date or make card permanent (null)
 */
export async function setQrExpiration(
  id: string,
  expiresAt: string | null
): Promise<{ success: boolean; record?: QrCodeRecord }> {
  const current = await getQrCode(id);
  if (!current) return { success: false };

  const updatedRecord: QrCodeRecord = {
    ...current,
    expires_at: expiresAt,
    updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  };

  try {
    await setDoc(doc(db, 'qr_codes', id), {
      expires_at: expiresAt,
      updated_at: updatedRecord.updated_at,
    }, { merge: true });
  } catch (fsErr) {
    console.warn('Firestore set expiration warning:', fsErr);
  }

  try {
    fetch(`/api/qr/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`, {
      method: 'PUT',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ expires_at: expiresAt }),
    }).catch(() => {});
  } catch {}

  const cache = getLocalCache();
  cache[id] = updatedRecord;
  saveLocalCache(cache);

  return { success: true, record: updatedRecord };
}

/**
 * Real-time Live Firestore Subscription for QR Codes
 * Pushes live updates whenever any scan or activation happens without page refresh!
 */
export function subscribeToQrCodes(
  callback: (records: QrCodeRecord[]) => void
): () => void {
  try {
    const qrCol = collection(db, 'qr_codes');
    const unsubscribe = onSnapshot(
      qrCol,
      (snapshot) => {
        // Authoritative live snapshot: populate strictly from current Firestore docs
        // so that deleted codes disappear immediately and are never kept as ghost records
        const liveMap: Record<string, QrCodeRecord> = {};
        snapshot.forEach((d) => {
          const data = d.data();
          const id = data?.id || d.id;
          if (id && id !== 'undefined' && id !== 'null') {
            const item: QrCodeRecord = {
              created_at: data.created_at || new Date().toISOString(),
              total_scans: data.total_scans || 0,
              is_active: Boolean(data.is_active),
              target_url: data.target_url || null,
              ...data,
              id,
            };
            liveMap[id] = item;
          }
        });
        const list = Object.values(liveMap)
          .filter((item): item is QrCodeRecord => Boolean(item && item.id && item.id !== 'undefined' && item.id !== 'null'))
          .sort(
            (a, b) => new Date(b.created_at || 0).getTime() - new Date(a.created_at || 0).getTime()
          );
        saveLocalCache(liveMap);
        callback(list);
      },
      (err) => {
        console.warn('Firestore onSnapshot subscription warning:', err);
      }
    );
    return unsubscribe;
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('subscribeToQrCodes error:', err);
    return () => {};
  }
}

/**
 * Real-time live subscription for a single QR code document.
 * Immediately pushes updates (e.g. when Admin changes number or wallet operator)
 * so customer screens or active view updates instantly without reload!
 */
export function subscribeToSingleQrCode(
  id: string,
  callback: (record: QrCodeRecord | null) => void
): () => void {
  const cleanId = id.trim();
  if (!cleanId) return () => {};

  try {
    const docRef = doc(db, 'qr_codes', cleanId);
    const unsubscribe = onSnapshot(
      docRef,
      (snapshot) => {
        if (snapshot.exists()) {
          const rec = snapshot.data() as QrCodeRecord;
          const cache = getLocalCache();
          cache[cleanId] = rec;
          saveLocalCache(cache);
          callback(rec);
        }
      },
      (err) => {
        console.warn('Single QR live subscription warning:', err);
      }
    );
    return unsubscribe;
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('subscribeToSingleQrCode error:', err);
    return () => {};
  }
}

/**
 * Record a scan count authoritatively in Firestore & local cache.
 * CRITICAL RULE: NEVER count scans before activation (is_active: false), even if scanned 1,000,000 times!
 * Only counts once per scan event, debounced against duplicate reloads/renders.
 */
export async function recordQrScan(id: string): Promise<void> {
  const cleanId = id.trim();
  if (!cleanId) return;

  // 1. Session / navigation deduplication: prevent double-counting on browser reload or re-renders
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    const sessionKey = `scanned_session_${cleanId}`;
    const lastScannedTime = sessionStorage.getItem(sessionKey);
    const now = Date.now();
    if (lastScannedTime && (now - parseInt(lastScannedTime, 10)) < 30000) {
      // Debounce: already counted within 30 seconds on this device/session
      return;
    }
    sessionStorage.setItem(sessionKey, now.toString());
  }

  // 2. Strict check: verify the card is actually active before incrementing!
  const cache = getLocalCache();
  const cached = cache[cleanId];
  if (cached && !cached.is_active) {
    // Card is not active yet: NEVER count scans before activation!
    return;
  }

  // Check Firestore document state if not in cache or ambiguous
  try {
    const docSnap = await getDoc(doc(db, 'qr_codes', cleanId));
    if (!docSnap.exists()) return;
    const data = docSnap.data();
    if (!data.is_active) {
      // Card is not active: DO NOT count scan!
      return;
    }
  } catch {
    if (!cached || !cached.is_active) return;
  }

  // 3. Direct Firestore atomic increment with setDoc merge (bulletproof on Vercel)
  try {
    const qrRef = doc(db, 'qr_codes', cleanId);
    await setDoc(qrRef, {
      total_scans: increment(1),
      last_scanned_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    }, { merge: true });
  } catch (fsErr) {
    console.warn('Firestore scan increment warning:', fsErr);
  }

  // 4. Also notify backend API if available (keepalive preserves request during navigation)
  try {
    fetch(`/api/qr/${encodeURIComponent(cleanId)}/scan`, { 
      method: 'POST',
      keepalive: true 
    }).catch(() => {});
  } catch {}

  // 5. Update local cache
  if (cache[cleanId]) {
    cache[cleanId].total_scans = (cache[cleanId].total_scans || 0) + 1;
    cache[cleanId].last_scanned_at = new Date().toISOString();
    saveLocalCache(cache);
  }
}

/**
 * Reset or manually adjust scan count
 */
export async function resetQrScanCount(id: string, count = 0): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const res = await authFetch(`/api/qr/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/reset-scans`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ count }),
    });
    if (res.ok) {
      const cache = getLocalCache();
      if (cache[id]) {
        cache[id].total_scans = count;
        saveLocalCache(cache);
      }
      return true;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Failed to reset scan count:', err);
  }

  try {
    await updateDoc(doc(db, 'qr_codes', id), { total_scans: count });
  } catch {}

  const cache = getLocalCache();
  if (cache[id]) {
    cache[id].total_scans = count;
    saveLocalCache(cache);
  }
  return true;
}

/**
 * Delete a QR Code permanently from database (Firestore SDK + REST), server, and local cache.
 * Purges the code completely whether active (is_active: true) or inactive (is_active: false).
 */
export async function deleteQrCode(id: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const cleanId = id.trim();
  if (!cleanId) return false;

  // 1. Immediately purge from local cache
  const cache = getLocalCache();
  delete cache[cleanId];
  saveLocalCache(cache);

  // 2. Delete from Cloud Firestore via Firebase SDK
  const sdkPromise = deleteDoc(doc(db, 'qr_codes', cleanId)).catch((fsErr) => {
    console.warn('Firestore SDK deleteDoc warning:', fsErr);
  });

  // 3. Delete directly from Cloud Firestore via REST API (ensures immediate backend purge)
  const pId = firebaseConfig?.projectId || 'clean-plateau-dv4kj';
  const dbId = firebaseConfig?.firestoreDatabaseId || 'ai-studio-41f04a06-1999-41b9-b7be-e25b21dc1fd0';
  const apiKey = firebaseConfig?.apiKey || 'AIzaSyAjyCP484AEQ52-9CDgp8TDkAw5woA1TFc';
  const restUrl = `https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/${pId}/databases/${dbId}/documents/qr_codes/${encodeURIComponent(cleanId)}?key=${apiKey}`;
  
  const restPromise = fetch(restUrl, { method: 'DELETE' }).catch((restErr) => {
    console.warn('Firestore REST delete warning:', restErr);
  });

  // 4. Delete from Backend Server API
  const serverPromise = authFetch(`/api/qr/${encodeURIComponent(cleanId)}`, {
    method: 'DELETE',
  }).catch((err) => {
    console.warn('Server delete request warning:', err);
  });

  // Wait for all delete operations to complete concurrently
  await Promise.allSettled([sdkPromise, restPromise, serverPromise]);

  // Clean local cache again defensively
  const finalCache = getLocalCache();
  delete finalCache[cleanId];
  saveLocalCache(finalCache);

  return true;
}

/**
 * Generate Classic QR Code Image Data URL (High Resolution for Printing)
 */
export async function generateQrDataUrl(qrUrl: string, width = 512): Promise<string> {
  return QRCode.toDataURL(qrUrl, {
    errorCorrectionLevel: 'M',
    margin: 2,
    width,
    color: {
      dark: '#000000',
      light: '#ffffff',
    },
  });
}

/**
 * Decode QR Code from an uploaded image file using jsQR
 */
export async function decodeQrFromImageFile(file: File): Promise<string | null> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      const img = new Image();
      img.onload = () => {
        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
        if (!ctx) {
          resolve(null);
          return;
        }

        canvas.width = img.width;
        canvas.height = img.height;
        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, img.width, img.height);

        const imageData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
        const code = jsQR(imageData.data, imageData.width, imageData.height, {
          inversionAttempts: 'attemptBoth',
        });

        if (code && code.data) {
          resolve(code.data);
        } else {
          resolve(null);
        }
      };
      img.onerror = () => resolve(null);
      img.src = e.target?.result as string;
    };
    reader.onerror = () => resolve(null);
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}

/**
 * Extract QR ID from scanned string or URL.
 */
export function extractQrIdFromUrl(rawText: string): string {
  const trimmed = rawText.trim();
  if (!trimmed) return '';

  const match = trimmed.match(/\/q\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
  if (match && match[1]) {
    return match[1];
  }

  if (/^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{6,32}$/.test(trimmed)) {
    return trimmed;
  }

  const parts = trimmed.split('/');
  const lastPart = parts[parts.length - 1]?.split('?')[0];
  if (lastPart && /^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+$/.test(lastPart)) {
    return lastPart;
  }

  return trimmed;
}

const DEFAULT_PLATFORM_SETTINGS: PlatformSettings = {
  developer_whatsapp: '01550523365',
  developer_contact_url: 'https://wa.me/201550523365',
  developer_title: 'تواصل مع المطور',
  support_message: 'مرحباً، أود التواصل بخصوص البطاقة الذكية',
  public_domain_url: 'https://smart-card-inky.vercel.app',
};

/**
 * Fetch platform settings from server with local fallback
 */
export async function fetchPlatformSettings(): Promise<PlatformSettings> {
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/settings');
    if (res.ok) {
      const json = await res.json();
      if (json.success && json.data) {
        try {
          localStorage.setItem(SETTINGS_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(json.data));
        } catch {}
        return json.data;
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Failed to fetch settings from server, using local fallback:', err);
  }

  try {
    const snap = await getDoc(doc(db, 'settings', 'platform'));
    if (snap.exists()) {
      const data = { ...DEFAULT_PLATFORM_SETTINGS, ...snap.data() } as PlatformSettings;
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(SETTINGS_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
      } catch {}
      return data;
    }
  } catch {}

  try {
    const cached = localStorage.getItem(SETTINGS_CACHE_KEY);
    if (cached) return JSON.parse(cached);
  } catch {}

  return DEFAULT_PLATFORM_SETTINGS;
}

/**
 * Update platform settings (developer contact / WhatsApp link / Public Domain)
 */
export async function updatePlatformSettings(settings: Partial<PlatformSettings>): Promise<PlatformSettings> {
  let cleanPhone = (settings.developer_whatsapp || '').trim();
  let contactUrl = (settings.developer_contact_url || '').trim();
  let cleanDomain = (settings.public_domain_url !== undefined ? settings.public_domain_url : '').trim();
  if (cleanDomain.endsWith('/')) cleanDomain = cleanDomain.slice(0, -1);

  if (!contactUrl && cleanPhone) {
    let num = cleanPhone.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
    if (num.startsWith('01')) num = '2' + num;
    contactUrl = `https://wa.me/${num}`;
  }

  const payload: PlatformSettings = {
    developer_whatsapp: cleanPhone || '01550523365',
    developer_contact_url: contactUrl || 'https://wa.me/201550523365',
    developer_title: settings.developer_title || 'تواصل مع المطور',
    support_message: settings.support_message || 'مرحباً، أود التواصل بخصوص البطاقة الذكية',
    public_domain_url: cleanDomain,
  };

  try {
    const res = await authFetch('/api/settings', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    });

    if (res.ok) {
      const json = await res.json();
      if (json.success && json.data) {
        try {
          localStorage.setItem(SETTINGS_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(json.data));
        } catch {}
        return json.data;
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Failed to save settings on server, updating local cache only:', err);
  }

  try {
    await setDoc(doc(db, 'settings', 'platform'), payload, { merge: true });
  } catch {}

  try {
    localStorage.setItem(SETTINGS_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(payload));
  } catch {}
  return payload;
}

/**
 * Smart Wallet Provider Icon Selector
 * Directly renders the official brand logo according to the wallet operator
 */
export function WalletProviderIcon({
  provider,
  className = 'w-6 h-6',
  size,
}: {
  provider?: 'vodafone' | 'orange' | 'etisalat' | 'we' | 'generic';
  className?: string;
  size?: number;
}) {
  switch (provider) {
    case 'vodafone':
      return <VodafoneCashIcon className={className} size={size} />;
    case 'orange':
      return <OrangeCashIcon className={className} size={size} />;
    case 'etisalat':
      return <EtisalatCashIcon className={className} size={size} />;
    case 'we':
      return <WePayIcon className={className} size={size} />;
    default:
      return <WalletCashIcon className={className} size={size} />;
  }
}

/**
 * 9. Luxury Bank Account Squircle Icon (24K Gold & Obsidian Pillars)
 */
export function BankAccountIcon({ className = 'w-6 h-6', size }: BrandIconProps) {
  return (
    <svg
      viewBox="0 0 100 100"
      width={size}

      xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
    >

      </defs>
      {/* Obsidian Squircle with Gold Border */}
      <rect width="100" height="100" rx="22" fill="url(#bank_obsidian_grad)" stroke="url(#bank_gold_grad)" strokeWidth="1.5" />
      {/* Classical Temple Pediment / Roof */}
      <path d="M50 22L22 36H78L50 22Z" fill="url(#bank_gold_grad)" />

      <rect x="27" y="45" width="7" height="24" rx="2" fill="#F8FAFC" />
      <rect x="39" y="45" width="7" height="24" rx="2" fill="#F8FAFC" />
      <rect x="54" y="45" width="7" height="24" rx="2" fill="#F8FAFC" />
      <rect x="66" y="45" width="7" height="24" rx="2" fill="#F8FAFC" />
      {/* Base Steps */}
      <rect x="20" y="71" width="60" height="4" rx="1" fill="#FEF08A" />
      <rect x="16" y="77" width="68" height="5" rx="1.5" fill="url(#bank_gold_grad)" />
