================================================================================
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Sparkles, Download, CheckCircle2, QrCode as QrIcon, Loader2, X, Hash } from 'lucide-react';
import { generateBulkQrCodes, generateQrDataUrl } from '../lib/qrService';
import { QrCodeRecord } from '../types/qr';

interface BulkGenerateModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onGenerated: (codes: QrCodeRecord[]) => void;
  domainUrl: string;
}

export function BulkGenerateModal({ isOpen, onClose, onGenerated, domainUrl }: BulkGenerateModalProps) {
  // Direct editable number input (defaults to 10 or empty for easy typing)
  const [countInput, setCountInput] = useState<string>('10');
  const [monthsInput, setMonthsInput] = useState<string>('1');
  const [isPermanent, setIsPermanent] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [generatedCodes, setGeneratedCodes] = useState<Array<{ record: QrCodeRecord; qrDataUrl: string }>>([]);
  const [completed, setCompleted] = useState(false);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const parsedCount = parseInt(countInput, 10);
  const parsedMonths = isPermanent ? 0 : parseInt(monthsInput, 10);

  const handleGenerate = async () => {
    if (isNaN(parsedCount) || parsedCount < 1) {
      setErrorMsg('يرجى كتابة عدد صحيح للأكواد أكبر من صفر.');
      return;
    }

    if (parsedCount > 500) {
      setErrorMsg('الحد الأقصى للتوليد في الدفعة الواحدة هو 500 كود لضمان كفاءة السيرفر والذاكرة.');
      return;
    }

    if (!isPermanent && (isNaN(parsedMonths) || parsedMonths < 1)) {
      setErrorMsg('يرجى كتابة عدد شهور صحيح أكبر من صفر، أو اختيار "دائم".');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setErrorMsg(null);

    try {
      const durationMonths = isPermanent ? 0 : Math.max(1, parsedMonths);
      const records = await generateBulkQrCodes(parsedCount, durationMonths);
      const withDataUrls = await Promise.all(
        records.map(async (record) => {
          const fullScanUrl = `${domainUrl}/q/${record.id}`;
          const qrDataUrl = await generateQrDataUrl(fullScanUrl, 600);
          return { record, qrDataUrl };
        })
      );
      setGeneratedCodes(withDataUrls);
      setCompleted(true);
      onGenerated(records);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Bulk generation error:', e);
      setErrorMsg('حدث خطأ أثناء توليد الأكواد.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDownloadSingle = (dataUrl: string, id: string) => {
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = dataUrl;
    link.download = `QR_${id}.png`;
    link.click();
  };

  const handleDownloadAll = () => {
    generatedCodes.forEach((item, idx) => {
      setTimeout(() => {
        handleDownloadSingle(item.qrDataUrl, item.record.id);
      }, idx * 120);
    });
  };

  const reset = () => {
    setGeneratedCodes([]);
    setCompleted(false);
    setErrorMsg(null);
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-2xl bg-[#0e0e12] rounded-3xl shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(212,175,55,0.2)] border border-amber-500/35 overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh] text-white">
        {/* Ambient Gold Glow */}
        <div className="absolute -top-12 -right-12 w-32 h-32 bg-amber-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="absolute -bottom-12 -left-12 w-32 h-32 bg-yellow-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />

        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-b border-amber-500/20 bg-black/60">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 text-black flex items-center justify-center shadow-md shadow-amber-500/20 font-black">
              <QrIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-black" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500">
                توليد أكواد الـ QR بالعدد المطلوب
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">اكتب أي عدد ترغب في إنشائه دفعة واحدة مع معرّف nanoid فريد</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={reset}
            className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10 rounded-xl transition"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Content */}
        <div className="p-6 overflow-y-auto space-y-6">
          {!completed ? (
            <div className="space-y-6">
              {/* Direct Count Input Box (As Requested by User) */}
              <div className="p-6 bg-gradient-to-br from-[#16161c] via-[#121216] to-[#0e0e12] border border-amber-500/30 rounded-3xl shadow-xl space-y-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <label className="text-sm font-black text-amber-200/90 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Hash className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400" />
                    اكتب عدد الرموز التي تريد توليدها مباشرة:
                  </label>
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-black text-amber-300 bg-amber-400/15 border border-amber-400/30 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                    حقل كتابة حر
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="relative flex items-center">
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min="1"
                    max="500"
                    value={countInput}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      setCountInput(e.target.value);
                      setErrorMsg(null);
                    }}
                    placeholder="اكتب العدد هنا مثلاً: 15"
                    className="w-full text-center text-3xl font-black text-amber-400 bg-black/60 border-2 border-amber-500/30 focus:border-amber-400 rounded-2xl py-4 px-6 shadow-inner focus:outline-none focus:ring-4 focus:ring-amber-500/10 transition"
                    autoFocus
                  />
                  <span className="absolute left-6 text-sm font-bold text-slate-500 pointer-events-none">
                    كود QR
                  </span>
                </div>

                {/* Quick Add Buttons for Convenience */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-semibold">إضافة سريعة للعدد:</span>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
                    {[1, 5, 10, 20, 50, 100].map((num) => (
                      <button
                        key={num}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setCountInput(num.toString())}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold bg-black/40 hover:bg-amber-400 hover:text-black text-amber-200 border border-amber-500/25 rounded-xl transition shadow-inner"
                      >
                        {num}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Subscription Duration Selector with Free Typing Number Input */}
              <div className="space-y-3 p-4 bg-black/40 border-2 border-amber-500/25 rounded-2xl">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <label className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-200 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                    <span>مدة تفعيل الرمز (كم شهر):</span>
                  </label>
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-black text-amber-300 bg-amber-400/15 border border-amber-400/30 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                    حقل كتابة حر للشهور
                  </span>
                </div>

                {/* Free Typing Input Box */}
                <div className="relative flex items-center">
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    min="1"
                    max="120"
                    value={isPermanent ? '' : monthsInput}
                    disabled={isPermanent}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      setIsPermanent(false);
                      setMonthsInput(e.target.value);
                      setErrorMsg(null);
                    }}
                    placeholder={isPermanent ? 'تم ضبطه: دائم / غير محدود' : 'اكتب عدد الشهور هنا مثلاً: 1'}
                    className={`w-full text-center text-3xl font-black rounded-2xl py-3.5 px-6 shadow-inner focus:outline-none transition border-2 ${
                      isPermanent
                        ? 'bg-cyan-950/40 text-cyan-300 border-cyan-500/40 cursor-not-allowed'
                        : 'text-amber-400 bg-black/60 border-amber-500/30 focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-amber-500/10'
                    }`}
                  />
                  <span className="absolute left-6 text-sm font-bold text-slate-400 pointer-events-none">
                    {isPermanent ? '✦ دائم' : 'شهر تفعيل'}
                  </span>
                </div>

                {/* Quick Add Buttons for Duration */}
                <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 pt-1">
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-semibold">تحديد سريع للشهر:</span>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
                    {[
                      { label: '1 شهر', val: '1' },
                      { label: '2 شهر', val: '2' },
                      { label: '3 شهور', val: '3' },
                      { label: '6 شهور', val: '6' },
                      { label: '12 شهر (سنة)', val: '12' },
                    ].map((item) => (
                      <button
                        key={item.val}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => {
                          setIsPermanent(false);
                          setMonthsInput(item.val);
                          setErrorMsg(null);
                        }}
                        className={`px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition shadow-inner border ${
                          !isPermanent && monthsInput === item.val
                            ? 'bg-amber-400 text-black border-amber-300 shadow-amber-400/20'
                            : 'bg-black/40 hover:bg-amber-400/20 text-amber-200 border-amber-500/25'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {item.label}
                      </button>
                    ))}

                    {/* Permanent Option */}
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setIsPermanent(true);
                        setErrorMsg(null);
                      }}
                      className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition shadow-inner border ${
                        isPermanent
                          ? 'bg-cyan-500 text-black border-cyan-300 font-black'
                          : 'bg-cyan-500/10 hover:bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300 border-cyan-500/30'
                      }`}
                    >
                      دائم (بدون انتهاء)
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="p-2.5 bg-black/50 rounded-xl border border-white/5 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                  <span className="text-slate-400 font-medium">النتيجة بعد التوليد:</span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-amber-300">
                    {isPermanent ? (
                      <span className="text-cyan-300">يعمل باستمرار بدون انتهاء صلاحية ✦</span>
                    ) : (
                      <span>
                        سيعمل لمدة <strong>{monthsInput || 1}</strong> شهر (ينتهي في {
                          new Date(Date.now() + (parseInt(monthsInput || '1', 10) || 1) * 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', {
                            year: 'numeric',
                            month: 'long',
                            day: 'numeric',
                          })
                        })
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {errorMsg && (
                <div className="p-3.5 bg-rose-500/20 border border-rose-500/40 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold text-rose-300 animate-in fade-in">
                  {errorMsg}
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Info guidelines */}
              <div className="bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl p-4 text-xs text-slate-300 space-y-1.5 leading-relaxed">
                <p className="font-bold text-amber-300 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                  قواعد التوليد التلقائي:
                </p>
                <p>• سيقوم النظام بإنشاء <strong>{isNaN(parsedCount) || parsedCount < 1 ? '—' : parsedCount}</strong> معرّف ID فريد ومستقل لكل كود باستخدام nanoid(10).</p>
                <p>• جميع الأكواد تُحفظ فورياً في قاعدة البيانات المركزية وتكون جاهزة للمسح أو التعديل.</p>
                <p>• الرابط الثابت داخل كل كود هو: <span className="font-mono text-amber-400 font-bold" dir="ltr">{domainUrl}/q/[id]</span></p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-6">
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-2xl p-5 text-white">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-7 h-7 text-amber-400 flex-shrink-0" />
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="font-black text-sm text-amber-300">تم توليد {generatedCodes.length} كود بنجاح!</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">تم حفظ المعرفات الفريدة في قاعدة البيانات وهي جاهزة للطباعة والتوزيع الآن.</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={handleDownloadAll}
                  className="w-full sm:w-auto flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-xl shadow-md transition"
                >
                  <Download className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
                  تحميل الكل دفعة واحدة (PNG)
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Grid of Generated QR Codes */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 max-h-[360px] overflow-y-auto p-1">
                {generatedCodes.map(({ record, qrDataUrl }, idx) => (
                  <div
                    key={record.id}
                    className="p-3 bg-[#141418] rounded-2xl border border-amber-500/20 shadow-md hover:border-amber-400/40 transition flex flex-col items-center text-center group"
                  >
                    <div className="relative p-1.5 bg-white border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl shadow-xs">
                      <img
                        src={qrDataUrl}
                        alt={`QR ${record.id}`}
                        className="w-28 h-28 object-contain"
                      />
                      <span className="absolute top-1 right-1 text-[10px] font-mono bg-black/80 text-amber-300 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-md">
                        #{idx + 1}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <span className="mt-2 text-xs font-mono font-black text-amber-300 tracking-wider bg-black/60 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg border border-amber-500/20" dir="ltr">
                      {record.id}
                    </span>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleDownloadSingle(qrDataUrl, record.id)}
                      className="mt-2 w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-1 py-1.5 px-2 bg-white/5 hover:bg-amber-400 hover:text-black text-amber-300 text-xs font-bold rounded-lg transition border border-amber-500/20"
                    >
                      <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      تنزيل الكود
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3 px-6 py-4 border-t border-amber-500/20 bg-black/60">
          {!completed ? (
            <>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={reset}
                className="px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-xl transition"
              >
                إلغاء
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleGenerate}
                disabled={loading || !countInput || parsedCount < 1}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 disabled:opacity-40 text-black font-black text-xs rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 transition cursor-pointer"
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin text-black" />
                    <span>جاري التوليد...</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
                    <span>توليد {parsedCount || ''} كود الآن</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </>
          ) : (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={reset}
              className="px-6 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 text-black font-black text-xs rounded-xl transition shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 cursor-pointer"
            >
              تم والعودة للوحة التحكم
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/components/PrintSheetModal.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Printer, X, Download, Sliders, Sparkles, Filter } from 'lucide-react';
import { generateQrDataUrl } from '../lib/qrService';
import { QrCodeRecord } from '../types/qr';

interface PrintSheetModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  records: QrCodeRecord[];
  domainUrl: string;
}

export function PrintSheetModal({ isOpen, onClose, records, domainUrl }: PrintSheetModalProps) {
  const [qrMap, setQrMap] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [columns, setColumns] = useState<number>(3);
  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState<'all' | 'inactive' | 'active'>('all');

  const filtered = records.filter((r) => {
    if (!r || !r.id) return false;
    if (filterType === 'inactive') return !r.is_active;
    if (filterType === 'active') return r.is_active;
    return true;
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    let isMounted = true;
    const generateAll = async () => {
      const map: Record<string, string> = {};
      for (const record of filtered) {
        const fullScanUrl = `${domainUrl}/q/${record.id}`;
        map[record.id] = await generateQrDataUrl(fullScanUrl, 400);
      }
      if (isMounted) setQrMap(map);
    };

    generateAll();
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [isOpen, filterType, filtered.length, domainUrl]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handlePrint = () => {
    window.print();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-4xl bg-[#0e0e12] rounded-3xl shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(212,175,55,0.2)] border border-amber-500/35 overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[92vh] text-white">
        {/* Ambient Gold Glow */}
        <div className="absolute -top-12 -right-12 w-32 h-32 bg-amber-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="absolute -bottom-12 -left-12 w-32 h-32 bg-yellow-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />

        {/* Header - Not printed */}
        <div className="no-print flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-b border-amber-500/20 bg-black/60">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 text-black flex items-center justify-center shadow-md shadow-amber-500/20 font-black">
              <Printer className="w-5 h-5 text-black" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500">
                تجهيز وطباعة رموز الـ QR للبطاقات والملصقات
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">مخرجات مجهزة لورق الطباعة A4 بدقة عالية وتنسيق جاهز للقص المباشر</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10 rounded-xl transition"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Toolbar - Not printed */}
        <div className="no-print px-6 py-3 bg-[#121216] border-b border-amber-500/20 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4 flex-wrap">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="font-bold text-amber-200/90">تصفية:</span>
              <select
                value={filterType}
                onChange={(e) => setFilterType(e.target.value as any)}
                className="bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 text-amber-300 font-bold focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
              >
                <option value="all">جميع الأكواد ({records.length})</option>
                <option value="inactive">غير المفعلة فقط (جاهزة للعملاء)</option>
                <option value="active">المفعلة فقط</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="font-bold text-amber-200/90">أعمدة الورقة:</span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setColumns(2)}
                className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold transition ${
                  columns === 2 
                    ? 'bg-amber-400 text-black border-amber-300 font-black shadow-md' 
                    : 'bg-black/40 text-slate-300 border-amber-500/20 hover:border-amber-400/40'
                }`}
              >
                عمودين (كروت كبيرة)
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setColumns(3)}
                className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border text-xs font-bold transition ${
                  columns === 3 
                    ? 'bg-amber-400 text-black border-amber-300 font-black shadow-md' 
                    : 'bg-black/40 text-slate-300 border-amber-500/20 hover:border-amber-400/40'
                }`}
              >
                3 أعمدة (مقاس قياسي)
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={handlePrint}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black font-black rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 transition cursor-pointer"
          >
            <Printer className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
            طباعة الورقة (Print A4)
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Printable Sheet Area */}
        <div className="p-6 overflow-y-auto bg-black/40 flex-1">
          <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-xl min-h-[600px] border border-amber-500/20 text-black">
            {filtered.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center py-16 text-slate-400 font-bold">
                لا توجد أكواد مطابقة للتصفية المحددة.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div
                className={`grid gap-4 print-grid ${
                  columns === 2 ? 'grid-cols-2' : 'grid-cols-3'
                }`}
              >
                {filtered.map((r, i) => (
                  <div
                    key={r.id}
                    className="print-card p-4 rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white flex flex-col items-center text-center relative"
                  >
                    {/* Header of sticker */}
                    <div className="w-full flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-bold pb-2 border-b border-slate-200">
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-black font-black font-mono">
                        <span>✦</span>
                        <span>Smart Card</span>
                      </span>
                      <span>#{i + 1}</span>
                    </div>

                    {/* Classic crisp black & white QR */}
                    <div className="my-3 p-1 bg-white">
                      {qrMap[r.id] ? (
                        <img
                          src={qrMap[r.id]}
                          alt={`QR ${r.id}`}
                          className="w-32 h-32 object-contain"
                        />
                      ) : (
                        <div className="w-32 h-32 bg-slate-100 animate-pulse rounded" />
                      )}
                    </div>

                    {/* Instructions and ID */}
                    <div className="w-full space-y-1">
                      <p className="text-[11px] font-black text-slate-900">
                        امسح الكود بكاميرا هاتفك
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-600 font-semibold">
                        {r.is_active ? 'فتح الرابط المباشر' : 'تفعيل الكارت الذكي'}
                      </p>
                      <div className="pt-2">
                        <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold bg-slate-100 text-slate-900 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded tracking-widest inline-block border border-slate-300">
                          {r.id}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/components/UploadQrModal.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useRef, DragEvent } from 'react';
import { Upload, Search, AlertCircle, Loader2, X, Image as ImageIcon, Sparkles, PlusCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { decodeQrFromImageFile, extractQrIdFromUrl, lookupExistingQrCode, getQrCode } from '../lib/qrService';
import { QrCodeRecord } from '../types/qr';

interface UploadQrModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onCodeFound: (record: QrCodeRecord) => void;
}

export function UploadQrModal({ isOpen, onClose, onCodeFound }: UploadQrModalProps) {
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [notFoundId, setNotFoundId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [manualId, setManualId] = useState('');
  const [imagePreview, setImagePreview] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  // Process uploaded image file
  const handleProcessFile = async (file: File) => {
    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
      setErrorMsg('يرجى اختيار ملف صورة صالح (PNG, JPG, WEBP)');
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setErrorMsg(null);
    setNotFoundId(null);

    const previewUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
    setImagePreview(previewUrl);

    try {
      const decodedText = await decodeQrFromImageFile(file);
      if (!decodedText) {
        setErrorMsg('تعذر قراءة رمز QR من الصورة المرفوعة. تأكد من وضوح الصورة وتوسط الرمز.');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      const extractedId = extractQrIdFromUrl(decodedText);
      const cleanId = extractedId || decodedText.trim();

      // Query database
      const record = await lookupExistingQrCode(cleanId);
      if (!record) {
        setNotFoundId(cleanId);
        setErrorMsg(`تم استخراج المعرّف [${cleanId}] ولكن الكود غير مسجل بقاعدة البيانات.`);
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      // Found! Pass record to parent
      setLoading(false);
      onCodeFound(record);
      onClose();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Scan error:', err);
      setErrorMsg('حدث خطأ أثناء معالجة الصورة.');
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDragOver = (e: DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsDragging(true);
  };

  const handleDragLeave = () => {
    setIsDragging(false);
  };

  const handleDrop = (e: DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsDragging(false);
    if (e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files[0]) {
      handleProcessFile(e.dataTransfer.files[0]);
    }
  };

  // Search by manual ID
  const handleManualSearch = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    let raw = manualId.trim();
    if (!raw) return;

    // Handle full URL pasted
    const extracted = extractQrIdFromUrl(raw);
    const cleanId = extracted || raw;

    setLoading(true);
    setErrorMsg(null);
    setNotFoundId(null);

    try {
      const record = await lookupExistingQrCode(cleanId);
      if (!record) {
        setNotFoundId(cleanId);
        setErrorMsg(`لم يتم العثور على أي كود بالمعرّف "${cleanId}".`);
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }
      setLoading(false);
      onCodeFound(record);
      onClose();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Search error:', err);
      // Fallback
      try {
        const fallbackRecord = await getQrCode(cleanId);
        if (fallbackRecord) {
          setLoading(false);
          onCodeFound(fallbackRecord);
          onClose();
          return;
        }
      } catch {}
      setErrorMsg('تعذر البحث عن الكود، يرجى المحاولة مرة أخرى.');
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Create & Edit new card on the fly if not found
  const handleCreateAndEdit = () => {
    if (!notFoundId) return;
    const newRecord: QrCodeRecord = {
      id: notFoundId,
      is_active: false,
      target_url: null,
      created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      total_scans: 0,
      subscription_months: 1,
      expires_at: new Date(Date.now() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
    };
    onCodeFound(newRecord);
    onClose();
  };

  const handleReset = () => {
    setImagePreview(null);
    setErrorMsg(null);
    setNotFoundId(null);
    setManualId('');
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-lg bg-[#0e0e12] rounded-3xl shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(212,175,55,0.2)] border border-amber-500/30 overflow-hidden flex flex-col text-white">
        
        {/* Ambient gold glow */}
        <div className="absolute -top-12 -right-12 w-32 h-32 bg-amber-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="absolute -bottom-12 -left-12 w-32 h-32 bg-yellow-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />

        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-b border-amber-500/20 bg-black/60 relative z-10">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 text-black flex items-center justify-center shadow-md shadow-amber-500/20 font-black">
              <Upload className="w-5 h-5 text-black" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500">
                فحص وتعديل رمز الـ QR (رفع صورة أو بحث برقم الكارت)
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">ابحث برقم الكارت أو ارفع الصورة لجلب الكارت وتعديل بياناته واشتراكه</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={handleReset}
            className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10 rounded-xl transition cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Content */}
        <div className="p-6 space-y-6 relative z-10">
          
          {/* Section 1: Drag & Drop Photo Area */}
          <div>
            <input
              type="file"
              ref={fileInputRef}
              accept="image/*"
              className="hidden"
              onChange={(e) => {
                if (e.target.files && e.target.files[0]) {
                  handleProcessFile(e.target.files[0]);
                }
              }}
            />

            <div
              onDragOver={handleDragOver}
              onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}
              onDrop={handleDrop}
              onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
              className={`relative border-2 border-dashed rounded-2xl p-6 sm:p-8 text-center cursor-pointer transition flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-h-[170px] ${
                isDragging
                  ? 'border-amber-400 bg-amber-400/10 scale-[0.99]'
                  : 'border-amber-500/30 hover:border-amber-400/70 bg-black/50 hover:bg-[#15151c]'
              }`}
            >
              {loading ? (
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 text-amber-400">
                  <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin" />
                  <p className="text-sm font-bold text-amber-200">جاري قراءة وفك تشفير الـ QR Code...</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">تحليل مصفوفة الرمز واستخراج رقم الكارت</p>
                </div>
              ) : imagePreview ? (
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
                  <img
                    src={imagePreview}
                    alt="Preview"
                    className="w-24 h-24 object-contain rounded-xl border border-amber-500/30 bg-white p-1.5 shadow-lg"
                  />
                  <span className="text-xs text-amber-300 font-bold underline">انقر لاختيار صورة أخرى</span>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-amber-400/15 border border-amber-400/30 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center mb-2.5">
                    <ImageIcon className="w-6 h-6" />
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-black text-slate-100">اسحب وأفلت صورة الـ QR هنا</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">أو انقر لتصفح واختيار صورة من جهازك (PNG, JPG, WEBP)</p>
                  <span className="mt-3 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/30 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-300">
                    <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-400" />
                    <span>فك تشفير فوري واستدعاء الكارت</span>
                  </span>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Error Message */}
          {errorMsg && (
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 p-4 bg-rose-500/15 border border-rose-500/35 rounded-2xl text-xs text-rose-300 animate-in fade-in">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400 flex-shrink-0" />
                <span className="font-semibold">{errorMsg}</span>
              </div>
              {notFoundId && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleCreateAndEdit}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 text-black font-black rounded-xl text-[11px] flex items-center gap-1 shadow-md hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 flex-shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <PlusCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>تسجيل هذا الكود وتعديله الآن</span>
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Divider with High-Contrast Label */}
          <div className="relative flex items-center justify-center py-1">
            <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center">
              <div className="w-full border-t border-amber-500/25" />
            </div>
            <span className="relative bg-[#0e0e12] px-4 text-xs font-black text-amber-400 tracking-wide">
              أو اكتب رقم الكارت مباشرة (ID)
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Section 2: Manual ID Input with Pristine High-Contrast Colors */}
          <form onSubmit={handleManualSearch} className="space-y-2.5">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-amber-200/90">
                أدخل معرّف أو رقم الكارت (ID):
              </label>
              <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-mono">
                مثال: I9pFBZwn8Y
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2.5">
              <div className="relative flex-1">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="اكتب رقم الكارت هنا... مثل I9pFBZwn8Y"
                  value={manualId}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    setManualId(e.target.value);
                    setErrorMsg(null);
                    setNotFoundId(null);
                  }}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-black/80 border-2 border-amber-500/40 rounded-2xl text-amber-300 text-base font-mono font-black placeholder-slate-500 focus:bg-black focus:border-amber-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-4 focus:ring-amber-400/20 shadow-inner transition text-center sm:text-left selection:bg-amber-400 selection:text-black"
                  dir="ltr"
                  autoFocus
                />
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading || !manualId.trim()}
                className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 disabled:opacity-50 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-2xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/25 transition cursor-pointer active:scale-95 flex-shrink-0"
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin text-black" />
                ) : (
                  <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
                )}
                <span>بحث وجلب الكارت</span>
              </button>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 text-right">
              بمجرد النقر على "بحث وجلب الكارت" سيتم فتح نافذة التعديل مباشرة لتجديد الاشتراك، تعديل الرابط، أو تغيير المنصة.
            </p>
          </form>

        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/components/DeveloperSettingsModal.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, Check, ExternalLink, Sparkles, Phone, MessageSquare, Link, Globe } from 'lucide-react';
import { PlatformSettings } from '../types/qr';

interface DeveloperSettingsModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  settings: PlatformSettings;
  onSave: (updated: PlatformSettings) => Promise<void>;
}

export function DeveloperSettingsModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  settings,
  onSave,
}: DeveloperSettingsModalProps) {
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(settings.developer_whatsapp || '01550523365');
  const [contactUrl, setContactUrl] = useState(settings.developer_contact_url || 'https://wa.me/201550523365');
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(settings.developer_title || 'تواصل مع المطور');
  const [message, setMessage] = useState(settings.support_message || 'مرحباً، أود التواصل بخصوص البطاقة الذكية');
  const [publicDomain, setPublicDomain] = useState(settings.public_domain_url || 'https://smart-card-inky.vercel.app');
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setPhone(settings.developer_whatsapp || '01550523365');
      setContactUrl(settings.developer_contact_url || 'https://wa.me/201550523365');
      setTitle(settings.developer_title || 'تواصل مع المطور');
      setMessage(settings.support_message || 'مرحباً، أود التواصل بخصوص البطاقة الذكية');
      setPublicDomain(settings.public_domain_url || 'https://smart-card-inky.vercel.app');
      setSuccess(false);
    }
  }, [isOpen, settings]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  // Auto update direct contact URL when phone changes if using default wa.me scheme
  const handlePhoneChange = (val: string) => {
    setPhone(val);
    const clean = val.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
    if (clean) {
      const num = clean.startsWith('01') ? '2' + clean : clean;
      setContactUrl(`https://wa.me/${num}`);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsSaving(true);
    try {
      let finalUrl = contactUrl.trim();
      let cleanPhone = phone.trim();

      if (!finalUrl && cleanPhone) {
        let num = cleanPhone.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
        if (num.startsWith('01')) num = '2' + num;
        finalUrl = `https://wa.me/${num}`;
      }

      let cleanDomain = publicDomain.trim();
      if (cleanDomain.endsWith('/')) {
        cleanDomain = cleanDomain.slice(0, -1);
      }

      await onSave({
        developer_whatsapp: cleanPhone || '01550523365',
        developer_contact_url: finalUrl || 'https://wa.me/201550523365',
        developer_title: title.trim() || 'تواصل مع المطور',
        support_message: message.trim(),
        public_domain_url: cleanDomain,
      });
      setSuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setSuccess(false);
        onClose();
      }, 1400);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleTestLink = () => {
    let url = contactUrl.trim();
    if (!url && phone.trim()) {
      let num = phone.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
      if (num.startsWith('01')) num = '2' + num;
      url = `https://wa.me/${num}`;
    }
    if (!url) url = 'https://wa.me/201550523365';
    window.open(url, '_blank');
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md animate-in fade-in">
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg bg-[#0e0e12] border border-amber-500/30 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(212,175,55,0.2)] text-white space-y-6 relative overflow-hidden">
        {/* Ambient gold glow */}
        <div className="absolute -top-10 -right-10 w-28 h-28 bg-amber-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="absolute -bottom-10 -left-10 w-28 h-28 bg-yellow-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />

        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-amber-500/20 pb-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-400/20 to-yellow-600/10 border border-amber-400/40 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-400 shadow-inner">
              <MessageSquare className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <h3 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500">
                  إعدادات التواصل مع المطور والتحويل
                </h3>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                تعديل رابط ورقم الواتساب الخاص بالتحويل عند حدوث مشكلة أو حذف الرمز
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-xl hover:bg-white/10 transition"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {success && (
          <div className="p-3.5 bg-amber-500/20 border border-amber-400/40 rounded-2xl text-xs text-amber-300 font-bold flex items-center gap-2 animate-in fade-in">
            <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 flex-shrink-0" />
            <span>تم حفظ إعدادات التواصل بنجاح! تم التحديث في السيرفر والروابط فوراً.</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Form */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          {/* WhatsApp Phone */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-amber-200/90 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Phone className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              <span>رقم واتساب المطور (الأدمن):</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={phone}
              onChange={(e) => handlePhoneChange(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="01550523365"
              className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl text-sm text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
              dir="ltr"
              required
            />
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
              الرقم الحالي المعتمد: <span className="font-mono text-amber-300 font-bold" dir="ltr">01550523365</span>
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Custom Link / URL */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-amber-200/90 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Link className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              <span>الرابط المباشر للتحويل (URL):</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={contactUrl}
              onChange={(e) => setContactUrl(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="https://wa.me/201550523365"
              className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl text-sm text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
              dir="ltr"
              required
            />
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
              يمكنك كتابة رابط واتساب مباشر أو أي رابط مخصص تفضله للتحويل.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Button Text */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-amber-200/90 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              <span>نص زر التواصل الظاهر للعميل:</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="تواصل مع المطور"
              className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl text-sm text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
              required
            />
          </div>

          {/* Public Production Domain (For printing & live cards scanning) */}
          <div className="space-y-2 p-4 bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-500/10 via-yellow-500/5 to-transparent border border-amber-500/30 rounded-2xl">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-amber-300 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Globe className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                <span>رابط الدومين الأونلاين للموقع (Public Production Domain):</span>
              </label>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-black text-amber-400 bg-amber-400/20 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-amber-400/30">
                مهم للطباعة وهواتف العملاء
              </span>
            </div>
            <input
              type="url"
              value={publicDomain}
              onChange={(e) => setPublicDomain(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="مثال: https://cards.mybrand.com أو رابط السيرفر الأونلاين"
              className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 bg-black/70 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl text-sm text-amber-200 placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400"
              dir="ltr"
            />
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
              💡 <strong>حل مشكلة المسح من هاتف آخر:</strong> إذا قمت بتوليد أو طباعة الرمز وأنت على السيرفر المحلي (localhost)، فلن يفتح في هاتف العميل الخارجي. اكتب رابط موقعك المرفوع أونلاين هنا وسيتم تضمينه تلقائياً داخل كل كود QR وكل كارت مطبوع!
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Action Buttons */}
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center gap-2.5 pt-3 border-t border-amber-500/20">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTestLink}
              className="w-full sm:w-auto px-4 py-2.5 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 text-amber-300 hover:text-amber-200 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4" />
              اختبار الرابط فوراً
            </button>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full sm:w-auto sm:mr-auto justify-end">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onClose}
                className="px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              >
                إلغاء
              </button>
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isSaving}
                className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-xl shadow-[0_4px_20px_rgba(234,179,8,0.3)] transition transform active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {isSaving ? 'جاري الحفظ...' : 'حفظ الإعدادات'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Smartphone, Download, Sparkles, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { usePWAInstall } from '../hooks/usePWAInstall';
import { PWAInstallModal } from './PWAInstallModal';

export const PWAInstallBanner: React.FC = () => {
  const { isInstalled, isStandalone } = usePWAInstall();
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isDismissed, setIsDismissed] = useState(false);

  // If running as standalone app, do not display install banners
  if (isInstalled || isStandalone || isDismissed) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <>
      {/* Floating Bottom Action Banner on Mobile */}
      <div className="fixed bottom-4 left-4 right-4 sm:left-auto sm:right-6 sm:bottom-6 z-40 animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-5 duration-300 select-none">
        <div className="bg-[#121216]/95 border border-amber-500/40 rounded-2xl p-3 sm:py-3 sm:px-4 shadow-[0_4px_30px_rgba(212,175,55,0.25)] backdrop-blur-xl flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-white">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/30 p-1 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 shadow-md">
              <img src="/logo.png" alt="Smart Card" className="w-8 h-8 object-contain rounded-lg" />
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="text-xs font-black text-amber-300">تطبيق Smart Card</span>
                <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-ping" />
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300 truncate">تثبيت التطبيق على هاتفك للوصول السريع</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-shrink-0">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(true)}
              className="py-2 px-3.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black font-black text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md transition flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-black" />
              <span>تثبيت التطبيق</span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsDismissed(true)}
              className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-white/10 transition cursor-pointer"
              title="إخفاء"
            >
              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <PWAInstallModal isOpen={isModalOpen} onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)} />
    </>
  );
};

        title="تثبيت التطبيق على هاتفك"

        <span className="sm:hidden">التطبيق</span>
      </button>

      <PWAInstallModal isOpen={isModalOpen} onClose={() => setIsModalOpen(false)} />
    </>
  );
};

FILE: src/components/PWAInstallModal.tsx
