import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Smartphone, Download, CheckCircle2, X, Sparkles, Share2, PlusSquare, ShieldCheck, ArrowRight, Layers } from 'lucide-react';
import { usePWAInstall } from '../hooks/usePWAInstall';

interface PWAInstallModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const PWAInstallModal: React.FC<PWAInstallModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const { isInstallable, isInstalled, isIOS, install } = usePWAInstall();
  const [installSuccess, setInstallSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'pwa' | 'apk'>('pwa');

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleInstallClick = async () => {
    if (isInstallable) {
      const ok = await install();
      if (ok) {
        setInstallSuccess(true);
        setTimeout(() => {
          onClose();
        }, 1500);
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-md animate-in fade-in select-none">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-md bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/35 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-7 shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(212,175,55,0.2)] text-white space-y-5 overflow-hidden">
        {/* Glow */}
        <div className="absolute -top-12 -right-12 w-36 h-36 bg-amber-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />
        <div className="absolute -bottom-12 -left-12 w-36 h-36 bg-yellow-500/10 rounded-full blur-2xl pointer-events-none" />

        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-white/10">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/30 p-1 flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/10">
              <img src="/logo.png" alt="Smart Card App" className="w-10 h-10 object-contain rounded-xl" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <h3 className="text-base font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500">
                  تطبيق Smart Card
                </h3>
                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-black rounded-full bg-amber-400/15 text-amber-300 border border-amber-400/30">
                  تطبيق هاتف رسمي
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">تثبيت فوري على شاشة الموبايل الرئيسية</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-xl hover:bg-white/10 transition cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Tab switch: Direct Phone App (PWA) vs Native APK Info */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 p-1 bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('pwa')}
            className={`flex-1 py-2 px-3 rounded-xl transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer ${
              activeTab === 'pwa'
                ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 text-black font-black shadow-md'
                : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
            }`}
          >
            <Smartphone className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>التثبيت الفوري (PWA)</span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('apk')}
            className={`flex-1 py-2 px-3 rounded-xl transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer ${
              activeTab === 'apk'
                ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 text-black font-black shadow-md'
                : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
            }`}
          >
            <Layers className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>حزمة أندرويد (APK)</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {activeTab === 'pwa' ? (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {/* Features */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs">
              <div className="p-3 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl space-y-1">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-amber-300 font-bold">
                  <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                  <span>يعمل كتطبيق كامل</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">بدون شريط المتصفح (Standalone) مع لوجو وأيقونة مستقلة.</p>
              </div>
              <div className="p-3 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl space-y-1">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-amber-300 font-bold">
                  <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                  <span>سرعة فائقة وذاكرة</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">تخزين مؤقت وسرعة استجابة فورية 0.0 ثانية.</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Install Button & Status */}
            {isInstalled ? (
              <div className="p-4 bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-500/30 rounded-2xl text-center space-y-2">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-8 h-8 text-emerald-400 mx-auto" />
                <p className="text-sm font-black text-emerald-300">التطبيق مثبت بالفعل على جهازك! 🎉</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">يمكنك فتحه مباشرة من الشاشة الرئيسية لهاتفك في أي وقت.</p>
              </div>
            ) : installSuccess ? (
              <div className="p-4 bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-500/30 rounded-2xl text-center space-y-2 animate-in zoom-in-95">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-8 h-8 text-emerald-400 mx-auto animate-bounce" />
                <p className="text-sm font-black text-emerald-300">تم تثبيت التطبيق بنجاح! 🚀</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">أيقونة Smart Card موجودة الآن على شاشة هاتفك.</p>
              </div>
            ) : isInstallable ? (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleInstallClick}
                className="w-full py-4 px-6 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black font-black text-sm rounded-2xl shadow-xl shadow-amber-500/25 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2.5 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <Download className="w-5 h-5 text-black" />
                <span>تثبيت التطبيق على الهاتف الآن بنقرة واحدة</span>
              </button>
            ) : isIOS ? (
              <div className="p-4 bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl space-y-3 text-right">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-black text-amber-300">
                  <Smartphone className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                  <span>طريقة التثبيت على آيفون (iPhone / Safari):</span>
                </div>
                <ol className="list-decimal list-inside space-y-2 text-xs text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
                  <li>
                    اضغط على زر <strong>المشاركة (Share)</strong> <Share2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 inline text-amber-400" /> في أسفل شاشة سفاري.
                  </li>
                  <li>
                    مرر لأسفل واختر <strong>إضافة إلى الصفحة الرئيسية (Add to Home Screen)</strong> <PlusSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5 inline text-amber-400" />.
                  </li>
                  <li>
                    اضغط <strong>إضافة (Add)</strong> بالأعلى ليظهر التطبيق في شاشتك الرئيسية فوراً.
                  </li>
                </ol>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="p-4 bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl space-y-2 text-center text-xs">
                <p className="font-bold text-amber-300">لتثبيت التطبيق من متصفحك (Brave / Chrome):</p>
                <p className="text-slate-300">
                  اضغط على <strong>القائمة (⋮)</strong> في متصفحك ثم اختر <strong>"تثبيت التطبيق"</strong> أو <strong>"إضافة إلى الشاشة الرئيسية"</strong>.
                </p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          /* Native APK Tab */
          <div className="space-y-3.5 text-xs">
            <div className="p-4 bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl space-y-2.5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-amber-300 font-bold">
                <Layers className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                <span>تحويل الموقع إلى ملف APK حقيقي لأندرويد:</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-slate-300 leading-relaxed text-[11px]">
                تم تجهيز الموقع بملف <strong>Web App Manifest</strong> متكامل وخدمة <strong>Service Worker</strong> مطابقة 100% لمعايير Google Play و TWA (Trusted Web Activity).
              </p>
              <div className="p-2.5 bg-black/80 rounded-xl border border-white/10 space-y-1.5 font-mono text-[10px] text-amber-200">
                <p className="text-slate-400 font-sans font-bold">رابط الموقع الرسمي لتوليد APK:</p>
                <p className="select-all bg-black/90 p-1.5 rounded border border-amber-500/30">https://smart-card-inky.vercel.app</p>
              </div>
              <p className="text-slate-400 text-[11px] leading-relaxed">
                💡 يمكنك تحويله إلى ملف <strong className="text-amber-300">.APK</strong> قابل للتثبيت أو النشر على متجر Google Play في دقيقة واحدة مجاناً عبر أداة <strong>PWABuilder</strong> الرسمية من مايكروسوفت أو <strong>Bubblewrap</strong> من جوجل.
              </p>
            </div>

            <a
              href="https://www.pwabuilder.com/?url=https://smart-card-inky.vercel.app"
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="w-full py-3 px-4 bg-amber-400/15 hover:bg-amber-400/25 border border-amber-400/40 rounded-xl text-amber-300 font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition"
            >
              <span>فتح مولد الـ APK المباشر (PWABuilder)</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4 rotate-180" />
            </a>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="pt-2 text-center">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="text-xs text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition font-bold"
          >
            إغلاق
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

================================================================================
FILE: src/views/HomeLandingView.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  QrCode as QrIcon, 
  Smartphone, 
  Camera, 
  Upload, 
  Search, 
  Sparkles, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  HelpCircle, 
  ArrowLeft, 
  ShieldCheck, 
  Lock,
  Building2,
  Wallet,
  MessageSquare,
  AlertCircle,
  Loader2,
  Phone
} from 'lucide-react';
import { decodeQrFromImageFile, extractQrIdFromUrl, getQrCode, fetchPlatformSettings } from '../lib/qrService';
import { QrScannerModal } from '../components/QrScannerModal';
import { PWAHeaderButton } from '../components/PWAInstallBanner';
import { PlatformSettings } from '../types/qr';

interface HomeLandingViewProps {
  onGoToAdmin: () => void;
  onSimulateScan: (id: string) => void;
  domainUrl: string;
}

export function HomeLandingView({ onGoToAdmin, onSimulateScan }: HomeLandingViewProps) {
  const [manualCode, setManualCode] = useState('');
  const [isScannerOpen, setIsScannerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [cameraDeclinedOnce, setCameraDeclinedOnce] = useState(false);
  const [isUploading, setIsUploading] = useState(false);
  const [feedbackMsg, setFeedbackMsg] = useState<{ type: 'error' | 'success'; text: string } | null>(null);
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<PlatformSettings>({
    developer_whatsapp: '01550523365',
    developer_contact_url: 'https://wa.me/201550523365',
    developer_title: 'تواصل مع المطور',
    support_message: 'مرحباً، أود التواصل بخصوص البطاقة الذكية',
  });
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchPlatformSettings().then((s) => {
      if (s) setSettings(s);
    });
  }, []);

  // Submit manual card ID
  const handleManualSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const cleanId = manualCode.trim();
    if (!cleanId) {
      setFeedbackMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'يرجى كتابة كود البطاقة أولاً' });
      return;
    }

    setFeedbackMsg(null);
    try {
      const record = await getQrCode(cleanId);
      if (record) {
        window.location.href = `/q/${record.id}`;
      } else {
        setFeedbackMsg({ type: 'error', text: `كود البطاقة "${cleanId}" غير موجود أو غير صحيح.` });
      }
    } catch {
      window.location.href = `/q/${cleanId}`;
    }
  };

  // Upload card/QR photo
  const handlePhotoUpload = async (file: File) => {
    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
      setFeedbackMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'يرجى اختيار ملف صورة صالح (PNG أو JPG).' });
      return;
    }

    setIsUploading(true);
    setFeedbackMsg(null);

    try {
      const decodedText = await decodeQrFromImageFile(file);
      if (!decodedText) {
        setFeedbackMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'تعذر قراءة رمز QR من الصورة المرفوعة. تأكد من وضوح الصورة وتوسط الرمز.' });
        setIsUploading(false);
        return;
      }

      const extractedId = extractQrIdFromUrl(decodedText);
      if (!extractedId) {
        setFeedbackMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'تم فحص الرمز ولكن لم يتم العثور على كود بطاقة صحيح.' });
        setIsUploading(false);
        return;
      }

      window.location.href = `/q/${extractedId}`;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err);
      setFeedbackMsg({ type: 'error', text: 'حدث خطأ أثناء فحص الصورة. جرب إدخال الكود يدويًا.' });
      setIsUploading(false);
    }
  };

  const getWaLink = () => {
    if (settings.developer_contact_url?.trim()) {
      return settings.developer_contact_url.trim();
    }
    const clean = (settings.developer_whatsapp || '01550523365').replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
    const num = clean.startsWith('01') ? '2' + clean : clean;
    return `https://wa.me/${num}`;
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#09090b] text-white selection:bg-amber-500 selection:text-black flex flex-col justify-between">
      {/* Top Customer Header */}
      <header className="border-b border-amber-500/20 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-md sticky top-0 z-30">
        <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 h-16 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <img 
              src="/logo.png" 
              alt="Smart Card" 
              className="w-10 h-10 object-contain rounded-2xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 p-0.5 bg-[#121216] border border-amber-400/40" 
            />
            <div>
              <span className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black tracking-wider text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500 font-mono block">
                Smart Card
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <PWAHeaderButton />

            <a
              href={getWaLink()}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-amber-400/10 hover:bg-amber-400/20 border border-amber-400/30 text-amber-300 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition"
              title="تواصل مع المطور"
            >
              <MessageSquare className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
              <span className="hidden sm:inline">تواصل مع المطور</span>
            </a>

            <button
              onClick={() => setIsScannerOpen(true)}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-xl transition shadow-md shadow-amber-500/20"
            >
              <Camera className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
              <span>مسح البطاقة</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Main Customer Portal Content */}
      <main className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-10 sm:py-16 w-full space-y-10">
        {/* Welcome & Instructions */}
        <div className="text-center space-y-4">
          <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/30 text-amber-300 text-xs font-bold">
            <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
            منصة الفحص والتوجيه الفوري — Smart Card
          </div>

          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-black text-white leading-tight">
            أهلاً بك في منصة
            <span className="bg-gradient-to-l from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500 bg-clip-text text-transparent block mt-1.5 font-mono">
              Smart Card
            </span>
          </h1>

          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300 max-w-lg mx-auto leading-relaxed">
            إذا قمت بمسح بطاقتك الذكية أو واجهتك أي مشكلة، يمكنك فحص البطاقة مباشرة بالكاميرا، أو رفع صورتها، أو كتابة كود البطاقة بالأسفل للوصول الفوري لبياناتك وتطبيقاتك.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Feedback message banner */}
        {feedbackMsg && (
          <div className={`p-4 rounded-2xl text-xs flex items-center gap-3 animate-in fade-in ${
            feedbackMsg.type === 'error'
              ? 'bg-rose-500/15 border border-rose-500/30 text-rose-300'
              : 'bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-300'
          }`}>
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
            <span className="flex-1 font-semibold">{feedbackMsg.text}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Re-appear Camera Scan Choice Banner after first decline */}
        {cameraDeclinedOnce && !isScannerOpen && (
          <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/35 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3 animate-in fade-in duration-300">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-right">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-amber-400/20 border border-amber-400/30 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
                <Camera className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-extrabold text-amber-200">
                  خيار مسح الرمز بالكاميرا متاح مرة أخرى
                </p>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300">
                  تم إغلاق نافذة إذن الكاميرا — يمكنك إظهار اختيار التفعيل ومسح الرمز الآن في أي وقت.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setCameraDeclinedOnce(false);
                setIsScannerOpen(true);
              }}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black font-black text-xs rounded-xl shadow-md transition flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Camera className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-black" />
              <span>إظهار مسح الرمز بالكاميرا</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Hidden File Input for Image Upload */}
        <input
          type="file"
          ref={fileInputRef}
          accept="image/*"
          className="hidden"
          onChange={(e) => {
            const file = e.target.files?.[0];
            if (file) handlePhotoUpload(file);
          }}
        />

        {/* Quick Customer Action Cards */}
        <div className="bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/30 rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-6 shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(212,175,55,0.08)] backdrop-blur-xl">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            {/* Option 1: Live Camera Scan */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsScannerOpen(true)}
              className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-amber-400/10 via-yellow-500/5 to-transparent hover:from-amber-400/20 hover:to-yellow-500/10 border border-amber-500/30 hover:border-amber-400/60 transition group text-right flex items-center gap-4 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-amber-400/15 border border-amber-400/30 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 group-hover:scale-105 transition">
                <Camera className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-400" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-white">مسح فوري بالكاميرا</h3>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-200/70 mt-0.5">افتح كاميرا الهاتف واقرأ البطاقة مباشرة</p>
              </div>
            </button>

            {/* Option 2: Upload Photo */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
              disabled={isUploading}
              className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-white/10 hover:border-amber-400/40 transition group text-right flex items-center gap-4 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-white/10 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 group-hover:scale-105 transition">
                {isUploading ? (
                  <Loader2 className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-400 animate-spin" />
                ) : (
                  <Upload className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-300" />
                )}
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-white">
                  {isUploading ? 'جاري فحص الصورة...' : 'رفع صورة البطاقة'}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">من ألبوم الصور أو لقطات الشاشة</p>
              </div>
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Divider with text */}
          <div className="relative flex items-center justify-center py-2">
            <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center">
              <div className="w-full border-t border-amber-500/20" />
            </div>
            <span className="relative bg-[#121216] px-3 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-300/80">
              أو اكتب كود البطاقة المطبوع عليها
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Option 3: Manual Code Entry */}
          <form onSubmit={handleManualSubmit} className="space-y-3">
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2.5">
              <div className="relative flex-1">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={manualCode}
                  onChange={(e) => setManualCode(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="اكتب كود البطاقة المطبوع (مثال: k9X2mQp7)"
                  className="w-full px-4 py-3 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/20 rounded-xl text-sm font-mono text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400 transition text-center sm:text-right"
                  dir="ltr"
                />
              </div>

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={!manualCode.trim()}
                className="px-6 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 disabled:opacity-40 text-black font-black text-xs sm:text-sm rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
              >
                <Search className="w-4 h-4" />
                فتح البطاقة
              </button>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-center text-slate-400">
              تجد الكود مطبوعاً بخط واضح على البطاقة الذكية أسفل رمز الـ QR
            </p>
          </form>
        </div>

        {/* Customer Guidance & Feature Cards */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4 pt-2">
          <div className="p-4 bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl space-y-2 text-right">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-amber-400/10 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Building2 className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-xs font-extrabold text-white">التحويل البنكي المباشر</h4>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
              نسخ فوري لرقم الحساب والآيبان IBAN مع إمكانية فتح تطبيق البنك أو InstaPay بضغطة واحدة.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl space-y-2 text-right">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-amber-400/10 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Wallet className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-xs font-extrabold text-white">محافظ الكاش الإلكترونية</h4>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
              تحويل سريع عبر فودافون كاش، أورنج، اتصالات، وWE مع كود التحويل السريع المباشر.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/20 rounded-2xl space-y-2 text-right">
            <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-amber-400/10 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <MessageSquare className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-xs font-extrabold text-white">التواصل المباشر والواتساب</h4>
            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
              فتح محادثات واتساب، صفحات السوشيال ميديا، أو الاتصال الهاتفي فوراً دون الحاجة لتسجيل الرقم.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Customer FAQ / Help Section with Direct WhatsApp Support Button */}
        <div className="p-6 bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/20 rounded-3xl text-right space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-amber-400 text-xs font-bold">
              <HelpCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
              تواجه صعوبة في مسح البطاقة أو تم حذف الكود؟
            </div>

            <a
              href={getWaLink()}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-xl shadow-md hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 transition"
            >
              <MessageSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>تواصل مع المطور: {settings.developer_whatsapp || '01550523365'}</span>
            </a>
          </div>

          <ul className="text-[11px] text-slate-300 space-y-1.5 list-disc list-inside leading-relaxed">
            <li>تأكد من تسليط إضاءة كافية على البطاقة وعدم وجود انعكاسات قوية.</li>
            <li>إذا كنت تستخدم كاميرا الهاتف العادية، تأكد من وضوح التركيز (Focus) على رمز الـ QR.</li>
            <li>يمكنك دائماً كتابة الكود المطبوع على البطاقة في الخانة أعلاه للوصول المباشر.</li>
            <li>إذا تم مسح أو إلغاء تفعيل البطاقة، اضغط على زر تواصل مع المطور ليتم حل المشكلة فوراً.</li>
          </ul>
        </div>
      </main>

      {/* Discrete Footer with Subtle Admin Login */}
      <footer className="border-t border-white/5 bg-black/60 py-6 px-4">
        <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 text-xs text-slate-500">
          <p>© {new Date().getFullYear()} Smart Card — جميع الحقوق محفوظة</p>
          
          <button
            onClick={onGoToAdmin}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-amber-300 transition text-[11px] py-1 px-2.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-white/5"
            title="تسجيل دخول الإدارة"
          >
            <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
            <span>دخول الإدارة</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </footer>

      {/* Live Camera Scanner Modal */}
      <QrScannerModal
        isOpen={isScannerOpen}
        mode="customer"
        onClose={() => setIsScannerOpen(false)}
        onDeclined={() => setCameraDeclinedOnce(true)}
        onUploadFallback={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
        onScannedId={(id) => {
          setCameraDeclinedOnce(false);
          setIsScannerOpen(false);
          window.location.href = `/q/${id}`;
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Eye, EyeOff, ShieldCheck, ArrowRight, AlertCircle, Lock } from 'lucide-react';
import { loginAdmin } from '../lib/auth';

interface AdminLoginViewProps {
  onLoginSuccess: () => void;
  onNavigateHome: () => void;
}

export function AdminLoginView({ onLoginSuccess, onNavigateHome }: AdminLoginViewProps) {
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!password.trim()) return;

    setErrorMsg(null);
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const ok = await loginAdmin(password.trim());
      if (ok) {
        onLoginSuccess();
      } else {
        setErrorMsg('كلمة المرور غير صحيحة. يرجى التأكد وإعادة المحاولة.');
      }
    } catch {
      setErrorMsg('حدث خطأ أثناء التحقق من كلمة المرور.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#09090b] text-white selection:bg-amber-500 selection:text-black flex flex-col justify-center items-center p-4 relative overflow-hidden">
      {/* Background ambient gold glows */}
      <div className="absolute top-1/4 -left-20 w-80 h-80 bg-amber-500/10 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />
      <div className="absolute bottom-1/4 -right-20 w-80 h-80 bg-yellow-500/10 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none" />

      {/* Back to Home Button */}
      <button
        onClick={onNavigateHome}
        className="absolute top-6 right-6 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-300/80 hover:text-white bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl backdrop-blur-sm border border-amber-500/20 transition cursor-pointer"
      >
        <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
        العودة للرئيسية
      </button>

      {/* Login Card */}
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#121216] border border-amber-500/30 rounded-3xl p-8 sm:p-10 shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(212,175,55,0.15)] relative z-10 backdrop-blur-2xl">
        <div className="text-center space-y-3 mb-8">
          <img 
            src="/logo.png" 
            alt="Smart Card" 
            className="w-16 h-16 object-contain rounded-2xl mx-auto shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/25 border border-amber-400/40 p-1 bg-[#121216]" 
          />
          <div>
            <span className="inline-block text-[11px] font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-300 bg-amber-400/15 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-amber-400/30 mb-2">
              لوحة التحكم
            </span>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500 font-mono">
              Smart Card
            </h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">
              تسجيل دخول الأدمن — إدارة الرموز والتوجيه الذكي
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {errorMsg && (
          <div className="mb-6 p-3.5 bg-rose-500/20 border border-rose-500/40 rounded-2xl text-xs text-rose-300 flex items-start gap-2.5 animate-in fade-in">
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
            <span className="leading-relaxed font-semibold">{errorMsg}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-5">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-amber-200/90">
              كلمة المرور السرية للأدمن:
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
              <input
                type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="أدخل كلمة المرور..."
                className="w-full px-4 py-3.5 bg-black/50 border border-amber-500/25 rounded-2xl text-sm text-white placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-amber-400 transition"
                dir="ltr"
                required
                autoFocus
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-amber-300 transition cursor-pointer"
                title={showPassword ? 'إخفاء' : 'إظهار'}
              >
                {showPassword ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading || !password.trim()}
            className="w-full py-3.5 px-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 disabled:opacity-50 text-black font-black text-sm rounded-2xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer active:scale-98"
          >
            {loading ? (
              <span>جاري التحقق...</span>
            ) : (
              <>
                <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5 text-black" />
                <span>دخول لوحة التحكم</span>
              </>
            )}
          </button>
        </form>

        <div className="mt-8 pt-6 border-t border-white/5 text-center flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-slate-500">
          <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400/60" />
          <span>آمنة ومحمية</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  Plus, 
  Upload, 
  Camera, 
  Printer, 
  Search, 
  Filter, 
  QrCode as QrIcon, 
  Download, 
  Copy, 
  Check, 
  ExternalLink, 
  LogOut, 
  Lock, 
  RefreshCw, 
  Smartphone, 
  Eye, 
  Activity, 
  BarChart3, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  Clock, 
  Sparkles,
  KeyRound,
  Grid,
  List,
  SlidersHorizontal,
  Trash2,
  AlertTriangle,
  AlertCircle,
  MessageSquare,
  Phone,
  Settings,
  Globe,
  Edit3,
  Calendar,
  FileText,
  Code2
} from 'lucide-react';
import { fetchAllQrCodes, generateQrDataUrl, deleteQrCode, getQrCode, lookupExistingQrCode, fetchPlatformSettings, updatePlatformSettings, renewQrSubscription, subscribeToQrCodes } from '../lib/qrService';
import { logoutAdmin, changeAdminPassword } from '../lib/auth';
import { BulkGenerateModal } from '../components/BulkGenerateModal';
import { UploadQrModal } from '../components/UploadQrModal';
import { EditQrModal } from '../components/EditQrModal';
import { PrintSheetModal } from '../components/PrintSheetModal';

  onLogout: () => void;

function formatDisplayTarget(url: string | null | undefined, appType?: string, wallets?: any[]): string {
  if (!url) return '— (غير معين بعد)';
  if (appType === 'wallet') {
    if (wallets && wallets.length > 1) {
      const phones = wallets.map((w: any) => w.phone).filter(Boolean).join(' • ');
      return `${wallets.length} محافظ كاش (${phones})`;
    }
    if (wallets && wallets.length === 1 && wallets[0]?.ownerName) {
      return `محفظة: ${wallets[0].phone} (${wallets[0].ownerName})`;
    }
    if (url.startsWith('tel:*9*7*')) {
      const num = url.replace('tel:*9*7*', '').replace('#', '').replace('%23', '');
      return num ? `محفظة: ${num}` : 'محفظة كاش';
    }
    if (!url.startsWith('http://') && !url.startsWith('https://')) {
      return `محفظة: ${url}`;
    }
  }
  if (url.startsWith('bank://')) {
    return 'بيانات حساب بنكي';
  }
  return url;
}

export function AdminDashboardView({ onLogout, domainUrl, onSimulateScan }: AdminDashboardViewProps) {
  const [records, setRecords] = useState<QrCodeRecord[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('');
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState<'all' | 'active' | 'inactive' | 'expired'>('all');
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'grid' | 'table'>('grid');
  const [renewingId, setRenewingId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Modals state
  const [isBulkOpen, setIsBulkOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isUploadOpen, setIsUploadOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isScannerOpen, setIsScannerOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isPrintOpen, setIsPrintOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isPasswordModalOpen, setIsPasswordModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState('');
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState('');
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('');
  const [passwordError, setPasswordError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [passwordSaving, setPasswordSaving] = useState(false);
  const [passwordSuccess, setPasswordSuccess] = useState(false);

  // Developer Settings Modal State
  const [isSettingsModalOpen, setIsSettingsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [platformSettings, setPlatformSettings] = useState<PlatformSettings>({
    developer_whatsapp: '01550523365',
    developer_contact_url: 'https://wa.me/201550523365',
    developer_title: 'تواصل مع المطور',
    support_message: 'مرحباً، أود التواصل بخصوص البطاقة الذكية',
  });

  // Edit modal
  const [selectedEditRecord, setSelectedEditRecord] = useState<QrCodeRecord | null>(null);

  // Delete modal state
  const [codeToDelete, setCodeToDelete] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isDeleting, setIsDeleting] = useState(false);

  // QR Thumbnails cache
  const [qrThumbs, setQrThumbs] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [copiedId, setCopiedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [adminNotice, setAdminNotice] = useState<{ type: 'error' | 'info' | 'success'; text: string } | null>(null);
  const [isCopyingFullCode, setIsCopyingFullCode] = useState(false);
  const [fullCodeCopied, setFullCodeCopied] = useState(false);

  const handleCopyFullPlatformCode = async () => {
    setIsCopyingFullCode(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/SMART_CARD_FULL_CODE.txt');
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch source file');
      const text = await res.text();
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      setFullCodeCopied(true);
      setAdminNotice({
        type: 'success',
        text: 'تم نسخ كود المنصة بالكامل (ملف واحد) إلى الحافظة بنجاح!',
      });
      setTimeout(() => setFullCodeCopied(false), 3000);
    } catch {
      setAdminNotice({
        type: 'info',
        text: 'يمكنك فتح أو تحميل الملف الموحد مباشرة من /SMART_CARD_FULL_CODE.txt',
      });
    } finally {
      setIsCopyingFullCode(false);
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmDelete = async () => {
    if (!codeToDelete) return;
    setIsDeleting(true);
    try {
      await deleteQrCode(codeToDelete);
      setRecords((prev) => prev.filter((r) => r.id !== codeToDelete));
      if (selectedEditRecord && selectedEditRecord.id === codeToDelete) {
        setSelectedEditRecord(null);
      }
      setCodeToDelete(null);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Delete failed:', err);
    } finally {
      setIsDeleting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleQuickRenew = async (id: string, months = 1) => {
    setRenewingId(id);
    try {
      const res = await renewQrSubscription(id, months);
      if (res.success && res.record) {
        setRecords((prev) => prev.map((r) => (r.id === id ? res.record! : r)));
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Quick renew failed:', err);
    } finally {
      setRenewingId(null);
    }
  };

  const loadData = async (silent = false) => {
    if (!silent) setLoading(true);
    try {
      const data = await fetchAllQrCodes();
      const safeData = Array.isArray(data)
        ? data.filter((item): item is QrCodeRecord => Boolean(item && item.id && item.id !== 'undefined' && item.id !== 'null'))
        : [];
      setRecords(safeData);

      // Pre-generate thumbnails for first batch
      if (!silent || Object.keys(qrThumbs).length === 0) {
        const thumbs: Record<string, string> = {};
        for (const item of safeData.slice(0, 30)) {
          if (item && item.id) {
            thumbs[item.id] = await generateQrDataUrl(`${domainUrl}/q/${item.id}`, 200);
          }
        }
        setQrThumbs(thumbs);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to load QR codes:', err);
    } finally {
      if (!silent) setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData(false);
    fetchPlatformSettings().then((s) => {
      if (s) setPlatformSettings(s);
    });

    // Real-time Live Firestore onSnapshot: instant updates without refresh!
    const unsubscribe = subscribeToQrCodes((liveRecords) => {
      const safe = Array.isArray(liveRecords)
        ? liveRecords.filter((r): r is QrCodeRecord => Boolean(r && r.id && r.id !== 'undefined' && r.id !== 'null'))
        : [];
      setRecords(safe);
      setLoading(false);
    });

    // Also periodic 4-second silent background refresh to guarantee live numbers
    const pollInterval = setInterval(() => {
      loadData(true);
    }, 4000);

    return () => {
      unsubscribe();
      clearInterval(pollInterval);
    };
  }, [domainUrl]);

  const handleCopyLink = (id: string) => {
    const fullUrl = `${domainUrl}/q/${id}`;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(fullUrl);
    setCopiedId(id);
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedId(null), 2000);
  };

  const handleDownloadQr = async (record: QrCodeRecord) => {
    const fullUrl = `${domainUrl}/q/${record.id}`;
    const dataUrl = await generateQrDataUrl(fullUrl, 800);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = dataUrl;
    link.download = `QR_${record.id}.png`;
    link.click();
  };

  const handleUpdatePassword = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setPasswordError(null);
    const cleanCurrent = currentPassword.trim();
    const cleanNew = newPassword.trim();

    if (!cleanCurrent) {
      setPasswordError('الرجاء إدخال كلمة المرور الحالية.');
      return;
    }

    if (cleanNew.length < 4) {
      setPasswordError('كلمة المرور الجديدة يجب أن لا تقل عن 4 خانات.');
      return;
    }

    if (cleanNew !== confirmPassword.trim()) {
      setPasswordError('كلمتا المرور الجديدتان غير متطابقتين.');
      return;
    }

    setPasswordSaving(true);
    try {
      const res = await changeAdminPassword(cleanCurrent, cleanNew);
      if (res.success) {
        setPasswordSuccess(true);
        setTimeout(() => {
          setPasswordSuccess(false);
          setIsPasswordModalOpen(false);
          setCurrentPassword('');
          setNewPassword('');
          setConfirmPassword('');
        }, 1600);
      } else {
        setPasswordError(res.message);
      }
    } catch {
      setPasswordError('حدث خطأ أثناء حفظ كلمة المرور.');
    } finally {
      setPasswordSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSaveDeveloperSettings = async (updated: PlatformSettings) => {
    const res = await updatePlatformSettings(updated);
    setPlatformSettings(res);
  };

  // Filtered records with bulletproof defensive guards
  const cleanSearch = (searchTerm || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const filtered = records.filter((r) => {
    if (!r || typeof r !== 'object') return false;
    const id = String(r.id || '').trim();
    if (!id || id === 'undefined' || id === 'null') return false;

    if (cleanSearch) {
      const idStr = id.toLowerCase();
      const targetStr = String(r.target_url || '').toLowerCase();
      const labelStr = String(r.label || '').toLowerCase();

      const matchesSearch =
        idStr.includes(cleanSearch) ||
        targetStr.includes(cleanSearch) ||
        labelStr.includes(cleanSearch);

      if (!matchesSearch) return false;
    }

    if (filterStatus === 'active') {
      return Boolean(r.is_active && (!r.expires_at || new Date(r.expires_at).getTime() >= Date.now()));
    }
    if (filterStatus === 'expired') {
      return Boolean(r.is_active && r.expires_at && new Date(r.expires_at).getTime() < Date.now());
    }
    if (filterStatus === 'inactive') {
      return !r.is_active;
    }
    return true;
  });

  // KPI Calculations
  const validRecords = records.filter((r): r is QrCodeRecord => Boolean(r && r.id && r.id !== 'undefined' && r.id !== 'null'));
  const totalCodes = validRecords.length;
  const expiredCodes = validRecords.filter((r) => r.is_active && r.expires_at && new Date(r.expires_at).getTime() < Date.now()).length;
  const activeCodes = validRecords.filter((r) => r.is_active && (!r.expires_at || new Date(r.expires_at).getTime() >= Date.now())).length;
  const pendingCodes = validRecords.filter((r) => !r.is_active).length;
  const totalScans = validRecords.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + (Number(curr?.total_scans) || 0), 0);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#09090b] text-slate-100 pb-16 selection:bg-amber-500 selection:text-black">
      {/* Top Navbar */}
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 bg-[#0e0e12]/95 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-amber-500/20 shadow-lg">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-3.5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <img 
              src="/logo.png" 
              alt="Smart Card Logo" 
              className="w-10 h-10 object-contain rounded-2xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 flex-shrink-0 p-0.5 bg-[#121216] border border-amber-400/40" 
            />
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                <h1 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500 font-mono">
                  Smart Card Admin
                </h1>
                <span className="text-[10px] bg-amber-400/15 text-amber-300 font-black px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-amber-400/30">
                  الأدمن الملكي
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-200/70 hidden sm:block">إدارة المعرّفات الفريدة والتوجيه الذكي المباشر</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Top Actions */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
            <PWAHeaderButton />

            {/* Developer Contact & WhatsApp Settings Button */}
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsSettingsModalOpen(true)}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400/10 via-yellow-400/15 to-amber-500/10 hover:bg-amber-400/25 text-amber-300 hover:text-amber-200 border border-amber-400/40 rounded-xl transition text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(212,175,55,0.15)] cursor-pointer"
              title="إعدادات التواصل مع المطور والواتساب"
            >
              <MessageSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              <span className="text-[11px]">تواصل مع المطور (الواتساب)</span>
            </button>

            {/* Copy Full Platform Code (Single File) */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleCopyFullPlatformCode}
              disabled={isCopyingFullCode}
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-cyan-300 hover:text-black hover:bg-cyan-400 bg-cyan-400/10 rounded-xl transition text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-cyan-400/30 cursor-pointer"
              title="نسخ كود المنصة بالكامل في ملف واحد بضغطة واحدة"
            >
              {fullCodeCopied ? <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
              <span className="text-[11px]">
                {fullCodeCopied ? 'تم نسخ الكود!' : 'نسخ كود المنصة'}
              </span>
            </button>

            {/* Download Full Source Code Single Text File */}
            <a
              href="/SMART_CARD_FULL_CODE.txt"
              download="SMART_CARD_FULL_CODE.txt"
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-emerald-300 hover:text-black hover:bg-emerald-400 bg-emerald-400/10 rounded-xl transition text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-emerald-400/30"
              title="فتح / تحميل كود المنصة بالكامل في ملف نصي واحد سهل النسخ"
            >
              <Code2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span className="text-[11px] hidden sm:inline">ملف الكود (.txt)</span>
            </a>

            {/* Download Documentation PDF */}
            <a
              href="/Smart_Card_Documentation_and_Updates.pdf"
              download="Smart_Card_Documentation_and_Updates.pdf"
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-amber-300 hover:text-black hover:bg-amber-400 bg-amber-400/10 rounded-xl transition text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-amber-400/30"
              title="تحميل ملف التوثيق والتحديثات (PDF)"
            >
              <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span className="text-[11px] hidden sm:inline">تقرير التوثيق (PDF)</span>
            </a>

            <button
              onClick={() => setIsPasswordModalOpen(true)}
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 text-slate-300 hover:text-white bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 rounded-xl transition text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-white/10"
              title="تغيير كلمة المرور"
            >
              <KeyRound className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              <span className="text-[11px]">كلمة المرور</span>
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={onLogout}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-rose-500/15 hover:bg-rose-500/25 text-rose-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition border border-rose-500/30"
            >
              <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span className="text-[11px]">خروج</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Main Body */}
      <main className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 pt-8 space-y-8">
        {adminNotice && (
          <div
            className={`p-3.5 rounded-2xl border text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-between gap-3 animate-in fade-in ${
              adminNotice.type === 'error'
                ? 'bg-rose-500/15 border-rose-500/35 text-rose-200'
                : adminNotice.type === 'success'
                ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 border-emerald-500/35 text-emerald-200'
                : 'bg-amber-400/15 border-amber-400/35 text-amber-200'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{adminNotice.text}</span>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setAdminNotice(null)}
              className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-[11px] cursor-pointer"
            >
              إغلاق
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Localhost / Online Domain Guidance Banner */}
        {(domainUrl.includes('localhost') || domainUrl.includes('127.0.0.1')) && !platformSettings.public_domain_url && (
          <div className="p-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500/15 via-yellow-500/10 to-amber-500/15 border border-amber-500/35 rounded-2xl flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 text-right shadow-lg">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-amber-400/20 border border-amber-400/40 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-400 flex-shrink-0">
                <Globe className="w-5 h-5" />
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-0.5">
                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-200 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span>تنبيه مهم لمسح وتفعيل الرموز من هواتف العملاء:</span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] bg-amber-400/20 text-amber-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-amber-400/30">بيئة محلية (Localhost)</span>
                </p>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
                  أنت تعمل حالياً على السيرفر المحلي. إذا قمت بعمل سكان للـ QR من هاتف خارجي، لن يفتح الهاتف لأن هاتف العميل لا يستطيع الاتصال بـ localhost بجهازك. لحل ذلك: اكتب رابط الدومين الأونلاين لموقعك في الإعدادات، أو ارفع الموقع مباشرة على الاستضافة (Render / Railway / VPS).
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsSettingsModalOpen(true)}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black font-black text-xs rounded-xl flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-[0_4px_15px_rgba(234,179,8,0.25)] flex-shrink-0 transition active:scale-95"
            >
              <Settings className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-black" />
              <span>ضبط رابط الموقع الأونلاين</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* KPI Stats Cards */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-3.5">
          <div className="p-4 bg-[#121216] rounded-2xl border border-amber-500/20 hover:border-amber-400/40 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between transition">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">إجمالي الرموز</p>
              <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-amber-400 mt-0.5">{totalCodes}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">معرّفات nanoid فريدة</p>
            </div>
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/20 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <QrIcon className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 bg-[#121216] rounded-2xl border border-amber-500/20 hover:border-emerald-400/40 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between transition">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">الاشتراكات النشطة</p>
              <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-emerald-400 mt-0.5">{activeCodes}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">سارية وتعمل الآن</p>
            </div>
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-emerald-400/10 border border-emerald-400/20 text-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 bg-[#121216] rounded-2xl border border-rose-500/20 hover:border-rose-400/40 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between transition">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">اشتراكات منتهية</p>
              <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-rose-400 mt-0.5">{expiredCodes}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">تتطلب التجديد</p>
            </div>
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/25 text-rose-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Clock className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 bg-[#121216] rounded-2xl border border-amber-500/20 hover:border-amber-400/40 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between transition">
            <div>
              <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">بانتظار التفعيل</p>
              <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-yellow-400 mt-0.5">{pendingCodes}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">كروت جديدة غير معينة</p>
            </div>
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-yellow-400/10 border border-yellow-400/20 text-yellow-400 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Clock className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 bg-[#121216] rounded-2xl border border-amber-500/20 hover:border-amber-400/40 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between transition col-span-2 md:col-span-1">
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">إجمالي المسح</p>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-500/30 text-[9px] text-emerald-400 font-bold">
                  <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" />
                  مباشر
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-amber-300 mt-0.5">{totalScans}</p>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">توجيه فوري لحظي</p>
            </div>
            <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/20 text-amber-300 flex items-center justify-center">
              <BarChart3 className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Primary Action Ribbon */}
        <div className="p-6 bg-gradient-to-r from-[#121216] via-[#191920] to-[#121216] rounded-3xl text-white shadow-xl shadow-amber-500/5 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-6 border border-amber-500/30">
          <div className="space-y-1.5 text-center md:text-right">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/30 rounded-full text-xs font-bold text-amber-300">
              <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              أمان ومطابقة: التعديل متاح فقط بعد مسح الرمز أو رفع صورته
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-transparent bg-clip-text bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-200 via-amber-400 to-yellow-500">
              أدوات الإدارة والمسح والتعديل الآمن
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 max-w-xl">
              تم حجب أزرار التعديل اليدوي العشوائي؛ لتعديل أي كود يجب التحقق منه عبر مسحه بكاميرا الأدمن أو رفع صورته لضمان مطابقة البطاقة الفعلية.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2.5 w-full md:w-auto">
            {/* Live Camera Scanner to Edit */}
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsScannerOpen(true)}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-700 hover:from-emerald-500 hover:to-teal-600 text-white text-xs font-extrabold rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-emerald-600/20 transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Camera className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
              مسح بالكاميرا للتعديل
            </button>

            {/* Upload QR Image to Edit */}
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsUploadOpen(true)}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 bg-[#1e1e26] hover:bg-[#252530] text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/40 text-xs font-extrabold rounded-xl shadow-lg transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Upload className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
              رفع صورة كود للتعديل
            </button>

            {/* Bulk Generate */}
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsBulkOpen(true)}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 via-yellow-400 to-amber-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
              توليد أكواد مجمعة
            </button>

            {/* Print Sheet A4 */}
            <button
              onClick={() => setIsPrintOpen(true)}
              className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10 border border-amber-500/20 text-slate-200 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Printer className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
              طباعة كروت A4
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Search, Filters, and Table */}
        <div className="bg-[#121216] rounded-3xl border border-amber-500/20 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
          {/* Controls Header */}
          <div className="p-5 border-b border-white/5 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
            {/* Search Input */}
            <div className="relative w-full md:w-80">
              <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 absolute right-3.5 top-3" />
              <input
                type="text"
                value={searchTerm}
                onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="ابحث بالمعرّف ID أو الرابط المستهدف... (مثل I9pFBZwn8Y)"
                className="w-full pr-10 pl-4 py-2.5 bg-black/70 border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl text-xs font-mono font-black text-amber-300 placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-400 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-400/20 transition selection:bg-amber-400 selection:text-black"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Filters and View Switcher */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 w-full md:w-auto justify-between md:justify-end">
              {/* Category Tabs: Active, Pending, All */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-black/40 p-1 rounded-2xl text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 border border-white/5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setFilterStatus('active')}
                  className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl transition ${
                    filterStatus === 'active'
                      ? 'bg-amber-400 text-black shadow-md font-black'
                      : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                  }`}
                >
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>النشطة</span>
                  <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ${
                    filterStatus === 'active' ? 'bg-black/20 text-black' : 'bg-amber-400/20 text-amber-300'
                  }`}>
                    {activeCodes}
                  </span>
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setFilterStatus('expired')}
                  className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl transition ${
                    filterStatus === 'expired'
                      ? 'bg-rose-500 text-white shadow-md font-black'
                      : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                  }`}
                >
                  <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>منتهية الصلاحية</span>
                  <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ${
                    filterStatus === 'expired' ? 'bg-black/30 text-white' : 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300'
                  }`}>
                    {expiredCodes}
                  </span>
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setFilterStatus('inactive')}
                  className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl transition ${
                    filterStatus === 'inactive'
                      ? 'bg-yellow-500 text-black shadow-md font-black'
                      : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>في انتظار التفعيل</span>
                  <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ${
                    filterStatus === 'inactive' ? 'bg-black/20 text-black' : 'bg-yellow-400/20 text-yellow-300'
                  }`}>
                    {pendingCodes}
                  </span>
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setFilterStatus('all')}
                  className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl transition ${
                    filterStatus === 'all'
                      ? 'bg-white/20 text-white shadow-md font-black'
                      : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/5'
                  }`}
                >
                  <span>الكل</span>
                  <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ${
                    filterStatus === 'all' ? 'bg-black/20 text-white' : 'bg-white/10 text-slate-300'
                  }`}>
                    {records.length}
                  </span>
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* View Switcher */}
              <div className="flex items-center border border-white/10 rounded-xl overflow-hidden p-0.5 bg-black/40">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setViewMode('grid')}
                  className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg transition ${
                    viewMode === 'grid' ? 'bg-amber-400 text-black shadow-sm font-bold' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-white'
                  }`}
                  title="عرض شبكي"
                >
                  <Grid className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setViewMode('table')}
                  className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg transition ${
                    viewMode === 'table' ? 'bg-amber-400 text-black shadow-sm font-bold' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-white'
                  }`}
                  title="عرض جدولي"
                >
                  <List className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Refresh Button */}
              <button
                onClick={() => loadData(false)}
                disabled={loading}
                className="p-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10 rounded-xl transition border border-white/5"
                title="تحديث البيانات"
              >
                <RefreshCw className={`w-4 h-4 ${loading ? 'animate-spin text-amber-400' : ''}`} />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* List Content */}
          {loading && records.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="py-24 text-center space-y-3">
              <RefreshCw className="w-8 h-8 text-amber-400 animate-spin mx-auto" />
              <p className="text-xs text-amber-200/80 font-bold">جاري تحميل بيانات الأكواد الملكية...</p>
            </div>
          ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="py-20 text-center space-y-3">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full bg-white/5 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto border border-amber-500/20">
                <QrIcon className="w-8 h-8" />
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-white">
                {cleanSearch ? `لم يتم العثور على نتائج محلية مطابقة لـ "${cleanSearch}"` : 'لم يتم العثور على أي رموز'}
              </p>
              {cleanSearch ? (
                <div className="space-y-2 pt-1">
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">قد يكون الكود مسجلاً بالسيرفر أو يحتاج جلباً مباشراً:</p>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={async () => {
                      setLoading(true);
                      const found = await lookupExistingQrCode(cleanSearch);
                      setLoading(false);
                      if (found) {
                        setSelectedEditRecord(found);
                        setRecords((prev) => [found, ...prev.filter((r) => r.id !== found.id)]);
                      } else {
                        alert(`الكود "${cleanSearch}" غير مسجل بالسيرفر.`);
                      }
                    }}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-xl transition shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20 cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-black" />
                    جلب وفحص الكود [{cleanSearch}] من السيرفر
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">اضغط على زر "توليد أكواد مجمعة" لإنشاء أول مجموعة من الرموز الذكية</p>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setIsBulkOpen(true)}
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400 to-yellow-500 hover:from-amber-300 hover:to-amber-400 text-black text-xs font-black rounded-xl transition mt-2 shadow-lg shadow-amber-500/20"
                  >
                    <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
                    توليد 10 أكواد الآن
                  </button>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : viewMode === 'grid' ? (
            /* Grid View */
            <div className="p-6 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
              {filtered.map((record) => {
                const scanUrl = `${domainUrl}/q/${record.id}`;
                return (
                  <div
                    key={record.id}
                    className="p-4 bg-[#16161c] rounded-2xl border border-amber-500/20 hover:border-amber-400/50 hover:shadow-[0_0_25px_rgba(212,175,55,0.12)] transition flex flex-col justify-between group"
                  >
                    <div>
                      {/* Top Header of card */}
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-white/5">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          {record.is_active && record.app_type && (
                            <PlatformBrandIcon type={record.app_type as any} className="w-5 h-5 flex-shrink-0" targetUrl={record.target_url || undefined} />
                          )}
                          <span className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-extrabold flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                            record.is_active
                              ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30'
                              : 'bg-yellow-500/15 text-yellow-300 border border-yellow-500/30'
                          }`}>
                            <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${record.is_active ? 'bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse' : 'bg-yellow-400'}`} />
                            {record.is_active ? 'مفعل نشط' : 'في انتظار التفعيل'}
                          </span>
                        </div>

                        {/* Prominent Eye Badge for admin */}
                        <div 
                          className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/30 rounded-full text-amber-300 font-extrabold text-xs shadow-inner"
                          title={`تم فتح ومسح هذا الرابط ${record.total_scans || 0} مرة بواسطة العملاء`}
                        >
                          <Eye className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
                          <span>{record.total_scans || 0} مسحة</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      {/* QR Thumbnail */}
                      <div className="my-3 flex justify-center">
                        <div className="p-2.5 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-inner group-hover:scale-105 transition">
                          {qrThumbs[record.id] ? (
                            <img
                              src={qrThumbs[record.id]}
                              alt={`QR ${record.id}`}
                              className="w-28 h-28 object-contain"
                            />
                          ) : (
                            <div className="w-28 h-28 bg-slate-200 rounded animate-pulse" />
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      {/* ID and info */}
                      <div className="space-y-1.5 text-center">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
                          <span className="font-mono text-xs font-black text-amber-300 bg-black/60 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg tracking-wider border border-amber-500/30" dir="ltr">
                            {record.id}
                          </span>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleCopyLink(record.id)}
                            className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-amber-300 transition"
                            title="نسخ رابط المسح"
                          >
                            {copiedId === record.id ? (
                              <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
                            ) : (
                              <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            )}
                          </button>
                        </div>

                        {record.label && (
                          <p className="text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-200/90 truncate">
                            {record.label}
                          </p>
                        )}

                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate" dir="ltr">
                          {formatDisplayTarget(record.target_url, record.app_type, record.wallets)}
                        </p>

                        {/* Prominent Eye details banner */}
                        <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-white/5 flex items-center justify-between gap-1.5 text-xs text-amber-300 font-extrabold bg-amber-400/10 py-1.5 px-2.5 rounded-xl border border-amber-400/20">
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                            <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                            <span>
                              {record.is_active 
                                ? `تم المسح: ${record.total_scans || 0} مرة`
                                : 'في انتظار التفعيل'}
                            </span>
                          </span>
                        </div>

                        {/* Subscription Expiry & Duration Management Box */}
                        <div className="mt-2.5 p-3 bg-black/60 border border-amber-500/25 rounded-xl space-y-2 text-right">
                          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px]">
                            <span className="font-bold text-amber-200/90 flex items-center gap-1">
                              <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                              <span>صلاحية الكارت:</span>
                            </span>
                            {record.expires_at ? (
                              new Date(record.expires_at).getTime() < Date.now() ? (
                                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-black bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/35 animate-pulse">
                                  منتهي الصلاحية ⚠️
                                </span>
                              ) : (
                                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-black bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30">
                                  باقي {Math.max(0, Math.ceil((new Date(record.expires_at).getTime() - Date.now()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)))} يوم
                                </span>
                              )
                            ) : (
                              <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-black bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/30">
                                دائم / غير محدود ✦
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </div>

                          {record.expires_at && (
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-400 pt-0.5">
                              <span>تاريخ الانتهاء:</span>
                              <span className="font-mono text-amber-300 font-bold" dir="ltr">
                                {new Date(record.expires_at).toLocaleDateString('ar-EG', {
                                  year: 'numeric',
                                  month: 'short',
                                  day: 'numeric',
                                })}
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          )}

                          {/* Quick 1-Click Renewal Buttons */}
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 pt-1">
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              disabled={renewingId === record.id}
                              onClick={() => handleQuickRenew(record.id, 1)}
                              className="flex-1 py-1.5 px-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-400/20 to-yellow-500/20 hover:from-amber-400 hover:to-yellow-500 hover:text-black text-amber-300 border border-amber-400/35 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-black transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1 disabled:opacity-50"
                              title="تجديد/تمديد شهر إضافي (30 يوماً)"
                            >
                              <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3" />
                              <span>+1 شهر</span>
                            </button>

                            <button
                              type="button"
                              disabled={renewingId === record.id}
                              onClick={() => handleQuickRenew(record.id, 3)}
                              className="py-1.5 px-2 bg-white/5 hover:bg-amber-400/20 text-slate-300 hover:text-amber-300 border border-white/10 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-1 disabled:opacity-50"
                              title="تجديد 3 أشهر"
                            >
                              <span>+3 شهور</span>
                            </button>

                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => setSelectedEditRecord(record)}
